(clang ‑std=c17 ‑Wall ‑Wextra\n     9→-pedantic ‑O2 listing.c ‑o listing && ./listing)\n    10→-->\n    11→\n    12→# Micro-services & Bounded Contexts\n    13→\n    14→EduPay Ledger Academy follows Domain-Driven Design and splits the\n    15→business domain into **four bounded contexts** (BCs).  Each BC is\n    16→implemented as one or more micro-services—small autonomous executables\n    17→written in C and packaged as OCI containers.\n    18→\n    19→| Bounded Context         | Canonical Service | Purpose                                                                    |\n    20→|-------------------------|-------------------|----------------------------------------------------------------------------|\n    21→| Admissions              | `admissions-api`  | On-boards students, allocates student-ids, emits *StudentRegistered*       |\n    22→| Bursar (A/R)            | `ledger-core`     | Double-entry ledger, tuition invoicing, GL postings, payment gateway       |\n    23→| Financial-Aid           | `aid-engine`      | Award rules, stipend disbursement, compliance (FERPA / PSD2 / PCI-DSS)     |\n    24→| Continuing-Education    | `micro-cred`      | Per-course micro-credential tracking, pay-per-module split-payments         |\n    25→\n    26→A **Shared-Kernel** provides value-objects (`Money`, `Currency`,\n    27→`StudentId`) and the **AuditTrail** event store.\n    28→\n    29→```\n    30→                        +-----------+\n    31→                        |  API-GW   |\n    32→                        +-----------+\n    33→                              |\n    34→            +-----------------+------------------+\n    35→            |                 |                  |\n    36→    +--------------+  +---------------+  +---------------+\n    37→    | admissions   |  | ledger-core   |  | aid-engine    |\n    38→    +--------------+  +---------------+  +---------------+\n    39→            \\           /       \\              /\n    40→             \\         /         \\            /\n    41→              \\       /           \\          /\n    42→              +----- Event Bus (NATS / Kafka) -+\n    43→                        ^\n    44→                        |\n    45→                 +---------------+\n    46→                 |  AuditTrail   |\n    47→                 +---------------+\n    48→```\n    49→\n    50→## Event Catalog (Ubiquitous Language)\n    51→\n    52→* `StudentRegistered`\n    53→* `InvoiceRaised`\n    54→* `PaymentAuthorized`\n    55→* `AidDisbursed`\n    56→* `LedgerEntryPosted`\n    57→* `SagaCompensationRequired`\n    58→\n    59→### Example Domain Event (C, Protobuf-ish Header)\n    60→\n    61→```c\n    62→/* events/payment_authorized.h */\n    63→#ifndef EP_EVENTS_PAYMENT_AUTH_H\n    64→#define EP_EVENTS_PAYMENT_AUTH_H\n    65→/*\n    66→  Domain Event: PaymentAuthorized\n    67→  This event is emitted by ledger-core after the acquiring bank\n    68→  responds with an authorization code.  Other contexts (Financial-Aid,\n    69→  Continuing-Education) subscribe to project read models.\n    70→*/\n    71→#include <stdint.h>\n    72→#include <time.h>\n    73→\n    74→#define PAYMENT_AUTH_VERSION 1\n    75→\n    76→typedef struct ep_payment_auth_t {\n    77→    uint8_t  version;          /* schema version                     */\n    78→    char     id[37];           /* UUID v4 string                     */\n    79→    char     student_id[16];   /* canonical SID                      */\n    80→    char     invoice_id[37];   /* payment belongs to which invoice   */\n    81→    char     currency[4];      /* ISO-4217                           */\n    82→    int64_t  amount_minor;     /* 1099 = $10.99 ( if cents )         */\n    83→    char     auth_code[12];    /* provided by payment processor      */\n    84→    time_t   occurred_at_utc;  /* RFC 3339 ts                        */\n    85→} ep_payment_auth_t;\n    86→\n    87→#endif /* EP_EVENTS_PAYMENT_AUTH_H */\n    88→```\n    89→\n    90→## Service Skeleton: `ledger-core`\n    91→\n    92→The canonical micro-service exposes gRPC, publishes events on NATS,\n    93→and persists to PostgreSQL.  Internally, Clean Architecture layers are\n    94→segregated into `domain`, `usecase`, and `infra` directories.\n    95→\n    96→### `domain/money.h`\n    97→\n    98→```c\n    99→/* domain/money.h */\n   100→#ifndef EP_DOMAIN_MONEY_H\n   101→#define EP_DOMAIN_MONEY_H\n   102→\n   103→#include <stdint.h>\n   104→\n   105→typedef struct ep_money_t {\n   106→    int64_t amount_minor;  /* -922337203685477 */\n   107→    char    currency[4];   /* \"USD\"            */\n   108→} ep_money_t;\n   109→\n   110→/* Equality check without floating-point errors */\n   111→static inline int ep_money_eq(const ep_money_t *a, const ep_money_t *b)\n   112→{\n   113→    return a && b &&\n   114→           a->amount_minor == b->amount_minor &&\n   115→           a->currency[0] == b->currency[0] &&\n   116→           a->currency[1] == b->currency[1] &&\n   117→           a->currency[2] == b->currency[2] &&\n   118→           a->currency[3] == b->currency[3];\n   119→}\n   120→\n   121→#endif /* EP_DOMAIN_MONEY_H */\n   122→```\n   123→\n   124→### `usecase/post_payment.c`\n   125→\n   126→```c\n   127→/* usecase/post_payment.c */\n   128→#include \"domain/money.h\"\n   129→#include \"events/payment_authorized.h\"\n   130→#include <stdio.h>\n   131→#include <string.h>\n   132→\n   133→typedef struct ep_payment_repo ep_payment_repo_t;\n   134→typedef struct ep_event_bus   ep_event_bus_t;\n   135→\n   136→/* Dependency inversion via pure C interfaces */\n   137→struct ep_payment_repo {\n   138→    int (*begin_tx)(ep_payment_repo_t *);\n   139→    int (*commit_tx)(ep_payment_repo_t *);\n   140→    int (*rollback_tx)(ep_payment_repo_t *);\n   141→    int (*insert_ledger_entry)(ep_payment_repo_t *,\n   142→                               const char  *invoice_id,\n   143→                               const ep_money_t *amount,\n   144→                               const char  *auth_code);\n   145→};\n   146→\n   147→struct ep_event_bus {\n   148→    int (*publish)(ep_event_bus_t *, const void *evt, size_t sz);\n   149→};\n   150→\n   151→/*\n   152→  Post a payment to the double-entry ledger.\n   153→\n   154→  Returns 0 on success, < 0 on domain error, > 0 on infra error.\n   155→*/\n   156→int ep_uc_post_payment(ep_payment_repo_t *repo,\n   157→                       ep_event_bus_t    *bus,\n   158→                       const char        *invoice_id,\n   159→                       const ep_money_t  *amount,\n   160→                       const char        *auth_code)\n   161→{\n   162→    if (!repo || !bus || !invoice_id || !amount || !auth_code)\n   163→        return -1; /* pre-condition failed */\n   164→\n   165→    if (repo->begin_tx(repo) != 0)\n   166→        return  1; /* infra error */\n   167→\n   168→    if (repo->insert_ledger_entry(repo, invoice_id, amount, auth_code) != 0) {\n   169→        repo->rollback_tx(repo);\n   170→        return  2;\n   171→    }\n   172→\n   173→    ep_payment_auth_t evt = {\n   174→        .version        = PAYMENT_AUTH_VERSION,\n   175→        .occurred_at_utc= time(NULL),\n   176→    };\n   177→    /* (Safe) string copy utilities omitted for brevity */\n   178→    strncpy(evt.id,        \"UUID-GEN-STUB\", sizeof evt.id);\n   179→    strncpy(evt.student_id,\"SID-LOOKUP-X\", sizeof evt.student_id);\n   180→    strncpy(evt.invoice_id,invoice_id,     sizeof evt.invoice_id);\n   181→    strncpy(evt.currency,  amount->currency, sizeof evt.currency);\n   182→    evt.amount_minor = amount->amount_minor;\n   183→    strncpy(evt.auth_code, auth_code, sizeof evt.auth_code);\n   184→\n   185→    if (bus->publish(bus, &evt, sizeof evt) != 0) {\n   186→        repo->rollback_tx(repo);\n   187→        return  3;\n   188→    }\n   189→\n   190→    return repo->commit_tx(repo);\n   191→}\n   192→```\n   193→\n   194→### Saga Orchestrator (Distributed Tuition Payment)\n   195→\n   196→```c\n   197→/* saga/tuition_payment_saga.h */\n   198→#ifndef EP_SAGA_TUITION_PAYMENT_H\n   199→#define EP_SAGA_TUITION_PAYMENT_H\n   200→\n   201→/*\n   202→  TuitionPaymentSaga orchestrates:\n   203→\n   204→  1. Reserve Financial-Aid budget\n   205→  2. Authorize card payment (Bursar)\n   206→  3. Disburse stipends\n   207→  4. Issue micro-credential revenue split\n   208→\n   209→  Compensation order is reverse.\n   210→\n   211→  State machine is stored in the EventStore; students step through\n   212→  failures during outage simulations.\n   213→*/\n   214→\n   215→typedef enum {\n   216→    SAGA_STEP_NONE = 0,\n   217→    SAGA_STEP_AID_RESERVED,\n   218→    SAGA_STEP_PAYMENT_AUTHORIZED,\n   219→    SAGA_STEP_STIPEND_PAID,\n   220→    SAGA_STEP_MICROCRED_SPLIT,\n   221→    SAGA_STEP_DONE,\n   222→    SAGA_STEP_FAILED\n   223→} ep_saga_state_t;\n   224→\n   225→#endif /* EP_SAGA_TUITION_PAYMENT_H */\n   226→```\n   227→\n   228→## Anti-corruption Layer (ACL) Sample\n   229→\n   230→Admissions uses an external vendor for SIS and must translate its JSON\n   231→schema into *StudentRegistered* events.\n   232→\n   233→```c\n   234→/* acl/sis_adapter.c */\n   235→#include <jansson.h>\n   236→#include \"events/student_registered.h\"\n   237→\n   238→/* Returns 0 on success */\n   239→int ep_acl_translate_sis_event(const char *json,\n   240→                               ep_student_reg_t *out_evt)\n   241→{\n   242→    json_error_t err;\n   243→    json_t *root = json_loads(json, 0, &err);\n   244→    if (!root) return -1;\n   245→\n   246→    const char *sid   = json_string_value(json_object_get(root, \"sid\"));\n   247→    const char *email = json_string_value(json_object_get(root, \"email\"));\n   248→    if (!sid || !email) {\n   249→        json_decref(root);\n   250→        return -2;\n   251→    }\n   252→\n   253→    strncpy(out_evt->student_id, sid, sizeof out_evt->student_id);\n   254→    strncpy(out_evt->email,      email, sizeof out_evt->email);\n   255→    out_evt->occurred_at_utc = time(NULL);\n   256→\n   257→    json_decref(root);\n   258→    return 0;\n   259→}\n   260→```\n   261→\n   262→## Data Ownership & ACID Boundaries\n   263→\n   264→* **Admissions** owns `Student` aggregate; others reference via SID.\n   265→* **Ledger-core** owns `Invoice`, `LedgerEntry`.\n   266→* **Financial-Aid** owns `Award`, `AidDisbursement`.\n   267→* Shared Kernel’s `AuditTrail` is append-only; no service can mutate.\n   268→\n   269→| Service        | DB Schema          | Tx Boundary (Local ACID)    |\n   270→|----------------|--------------------|-----------------------------|\n   271→| admissions     | `admissions.*`     | `Student`, `Applicant`      |\n   272→| ledger-core    | `ledger.*`         | `Invoice`, `LedgerEntry`    |\n   273→| aid-engine     | `aid.*`            | `Award`, `Disbursement`     |\n   274→| micro-cred     | `microcred.*`      | `CourseRun`, `SplitPayout`  |\n   275→\n   276→## Resilience Policy\n   277→\n   278→1. Idempotent command handlers.\n   279→2. At-least-once event delivery (NATS JetStream).\n   280→3. Circuit-breaker around external PSP, open after 3 failures / 30 s.\n   281→4. Saga compensation for inter-context failure.\n   282→\n   283→```c\n   284→/* infra/circuit_breaker.h */\n   285→#ifndef EP_INFRA_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_H\n   286→#define EP_INFRA_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_H\n   287→#include <time.h>\n   288→typedef enum { CB_CLOSED, CB_OPEN, CB_HALF_OPEN } cb_state_t;\n   289→\n   290→typedef struct circuit_breaker {\n   291→    cb_state_t state;\n   292→    unsigned   failure_count;\n   293→    time_t     last_failure;\n   294→    unsigned   open_timeout_sec;\n   295→    unsigned   threshold;\n   296→} circuit_breaker_t;\n   297→\n   298→void cb_init(circuit_breaker_t *cb,\n   299→             unsigned threshold,\n   300→             unsigned open_timeout_sec);\n   301→\n   302→int  cb_before_request(circuit_breaker_t *cb);\n   303→void cb_after_success(circuit_breaker_t *cb);\n   304→void cb_after_failure(circuit_breaker_t *cb);\n   305→\n   306→#endif /* EP_INFRA_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_H */\n   307→```\n   308→\n   309→## Build & Deployment Standards\n   310→\n   311→* `Dockerfile` uses scratch + `musl-clang` static linking ⇒ 6 MB images.\n   312→* Each service exports `/livez` and `/readyz` over HTTP on port 8080.\n   313→* Kubernetes Helm charts pin CPU/memory, configure HPA on NATS lag.\n   314→\n   315→---\n   316→\n   317→> “Architecture is about the important stuff—whatever that is.”  \n   318→> — Ralph Johnson\n   319→\n   320→Learners should fork this repository, enable **Saga Pattern\n   321→Demonstration Mode** (`make chaos-on`), and trigger the `tuition\n   322→checkout` flow.  When the intentional fault in step 2 forces a\n   323→rollback, open the **AuditTrail dashboard** and correlate the saga\n   324→compensating transactions.\n   325→\n   326→Happy hacking!\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. (clang ‑std=c17 ‑Wall ‑Wextra\n-pedantic ‑O2 listing.c ‑o listing && ./listing)\n-->\n\n# Micro-services & Bounded Contexts\n\nEduPay Ledger Academy follows Domain-Driven Design and splits the\nbusiness domain into **four bounded contexts** (BCs).  Each BC is\nimplemented as one or more micro-services—small autonomous executables\nwritten in C and packaged as OCI containers.\n\n| Bounded Context         | Canonical Service | Purpose                                                                    |\n|-------------------------|-------------------|----------------------------------------------------------------------------|\n| Admissions              | `admissions-api`  | On-boards students, allocates student-ids, emits *StudentRegistered*       |\n| Bursar (A/R)            | `ledger-core`     | Double-entry ledger, tuition invoicing, GL postings, payment gateway       |\n| Financial-Aid           | `aid-engine`      | Award rules, stipend disbursement, compliance (FERPA / PSD2 / PCI-DSS)     |\n| Continuing-Education    | `micro-cred`      | Per-course micro-credential tracking, pay-per-module split-payments         |\n\nA **Shared-Kernel** provides value-objects (`Money`, `Currency`,\n`StudentId`) and the **AuditTrail** event store.\n\n```\n                        +-----------+\n                        |  API-GW   |\n                        +-----------+\n                              |\n            +-----------------+------------------+\n            |                 |                  |\n    +--------------+  +---------------+  +---------------+\n    | admissions   |  | ledger-core   |  | aid-engine    |\n    +--------------+  +---------------+  +---------------+\n            \\           /       \\              /\n             \\         /         \\            /\n              \\       /           \\          /\n              +----- Event Bus (NATS / Kafka) -+\n                        ^\n                        |\n                 +---------------+\n                 |  AuditTrail   |\n                 +---------------+\n```\n\n## Event Catalog (Ubiquitous Language)\n\n* `StudentRegistered`\n* `InvoiceRaised`\n* `PaymentAuthorized`\n* `AidDisbursed`\n* `LedgerEntryPosted`\n* `SagaCompensationRequired`\n\n### Example Domain Event (C, Protobuf-ish Header)\n\n```c\n/* events/payment_authorized.h */\n#ifndef EP_EVENTS_PAYMENT_AUTH_H\n#define EP_EVENTS_PAYMENT_AUTH_H\n/*\n  Domain Event: PaymentAuthorized\n  This event is emitted by ledger-core after the acquiring bank\n  responds with an authorization code.  Other contexts (Financial-Aid,\n  Continuing-Education) subscribe to project read models.\n*/\n#include <stdint.h>\n#include <time.h>\n\n#define PAYMENT_AUTH_VERSION 1\n\ntypedef struct ep_payment_auth_t {\n    uint8_t  version;          /* schema version                     */\n    char     id[37];           /* UUID v4 string                     */\n    char     student_id[16];   /* canonical SID                      */\n    char     invoice_id[37];   /* payment belongs to which invoice   */\n    char     currency[4];      /* ISO-4217                           */\n    int64_t  amount_minor;     /* 1099 = $10.99 ( if cents )         */\n    char     auth_code[12];    /* provided by payment processor      */\n    time_t   occurred_at_utc;  /* RFC 3339 ts                        */\n} ep_payment_auth_t;\n\n#endif /* EP_EVENTS_PAYMENT_AUTH_H */\n```\n\n## Service Skeleton: `ledger-core`\n\nThe canonical micro-service exposes gRPC, publishes events on NATS,\nand persists to PostgreSQL.  Internally, Clean Architecture layers are\nsegregated into `domain`, `usecase`, and `infra` directories.\n\n### `domain/money.h`\n\n```c\n/* domain/money.h */\n#ifndef EP_DOMAIN_MONEY_H\n#define EP_DOMAIN_MONEY_H\n\n#include <stdint.h>\n\ntypedef struct ep_money_t {\n    int64_t amount_minor;  /* -922337203685477 */\n    char    currency[4];   /* \"USD\"            */\n} ep_money_t;\n\n/* Equality check without floating-point errors */\nstatic inline int ep_money_eq(const ep_money_t *a, const ep_money_t *b)\n{\n    return a && b &&\n           a->amount_minor == b->amount_minor &&\n           a->currency[0] == b->currency[0] &&\n           a->currency[1] == b->currency[1] &&\n           a->currency[2] == b->currency[2] &&\n           a->currency[3] == b->currency[3];\n}\n\n#endif /* EP_DOMAIN_MONEY_H */\n```\n\n### `usecase/post_payment.c`\n\n```c\n/* usecase/post_payment.c */\n#include \"domain/money.h\"\n#include \"events/payment_authorized.h\"\n#include <stdio.h>\n#include <string.h>\n\ntypedef struct ep_payment_repo ep_payment_repo_t;\ntypedef struct ep_event_bus   ep_event_bus_t;\n\n/* Dependency inversion via pure C interfaces */\nstruct ep_payment_repo {\n    int (*begin_tx)(ep_payment_repo_t *);\n    int (*commit_tx)(ep_payment_repo_t *);\n    int (*rollback_tx)(ep_payment_repo_t *);\n    int (*insert_ledger_entry)(ep_payment_repo_t *,\n                               const char  *invoice_id,\n                               const ep_money_t *amount,\n                               const char  *auth_code);\n};\n\nstruct ep_event_bus {\n    int (*publish)(ep_event_bus_t *, const void *evt, size_t sz);\n};\n\n/*\n  Post a payment to the double-entry ledger.\n\n  Returns 0 on success, < 0 on domain error, > 0 on infra error.\n*/\nint ep_uc_post_payment(ep_payment_repo_t *repo,\n                       ep_event_bus_t    *bus,\n                       const char        *invoice_id,\n                       const ep_money_t  *amount,\n                       const char        *auth_code)\n{\n    if (!repo || !bus || !invoice_id || !amount || !auth_code)\n        return -1; /* pre-condition failed */\n\n    if (repo->begin_tx(repo) != 0)\n        return  1; /* infra error */\n\n    if (repo->insert_ledger_entry(repo, invoice_id, amount, auth_code) != 0) {\n        repo->rollback_tx(repo);\n        return  2;\n    }\n\n    ep_payment_auth_t evt = {\n        .version        = PAYMENT_AUTH_VERSION,\n        .occurred_at_utc= time(NULL),\n    };\n    /* (Safe) string copy utilities omitted for brevity */\n    strncpy(evt.id,        \"UUID-GEN-STUB\", sizeof evt.id);\n    strncpy(evt.student_id,\"SID-LOOKUP-X\", sizeof evt.student_id);\n    strncpy(evt.invoice_id,invoice_id,     sizeof evt.invoice_id);\n    strncpy(evt.currency,  amount->currency, sizeof evt.currency);\n    evt.amount_minor = amount->amount_minor;\n    strncpy(evt.auth_code, auth_code, sizeof evt.auth_code);\n\n    if (bus->publish(bus, &evt, sizeof evt) != 0) {\n        repo->rollback_tx(repo);\n        return  3;\n    }\n\n    return repo->commit_tx(repo);\n}\n```\n\n### Saga Orchestrator (Distributed Tuition Payment)\n\n```c\n/* saga/tuition_payment_saga.h */\n#ifndef EP_SAGA_TUITION_PAYMENT_H\n#define EP_SAGA_TUITION_PAYMENT_H\n\n/*\n  TuitionPaymentSaga orchestrates:\n\n  1. Reserve Financial-Aid budget\n  2. Authorize card payment (Bursar)\n  3. Disburse stipends\n  4. Issue micro-credential revenue split\n\n  Compensation order is reverse.\n\n  State machine is stored in the EventStore; students step through\n  failures during outage simulations.\n*/\n\ntypedef enum {\n    SAGA_STEP_NONE = 0,\n    SAGA_STEP_AID_RESERVED,\n    SAGA_STEP_PAYMENT_AUTHORIZED,\n    SAGA_STEP_STIPEND_PAID,\n    SAGA_STEP_MICROCRED_SPLIT,\n    SAGA_STEP_DONE,\n    SAGA_STEP_FAILED\n} ep_saga_state_t;\n\n#endif /* EP_SAGA_TUITION_PAYMENT_H */\n```\n\n## Anti-corruption Layer (ACL) Sample\n\nAdmissions uses an external vendor for SIS and must translate its JSON\nschema into *StudentRegistered* events.\n\n```c\n/* acl/sis_adapter.c */\n#include <jansson.h>\n#include \"events/student_registered.h\"\n\n/* Returns 0 on success */\nint ep_acl_translate_sis_event(const char *json,\n                               ep_student_reg_t *out_evt)\n{\n    json_error_t err;\n    json_t *root = json_loads(json, 0, &err);\n    if (!root) return -1;\n\n    const char *sid   = json_string_value(json_object_get(root, \"sid\"));\n    const char *email = json_string_value(json_object_get(root, \"email\"));\n    if (!sid || !email) {\n        json_decref(root);\n        return -2;\n    }\n\n    strncpy(out_evt->student_id, sid, sizeof out_evt->student_id);\n    strncpy(out_evt->email,      email, sizeof out_evt->email);\n    out_evt->occurred_at_utc = time(NULL);\n\n    json_decref(root);\n    return 0;\n}\n```\n\n## Data Ownership & ACID Boundaries\n\n* **Admissions** owns `Student` aggregate; others reference via SID.\n* **Ledger-core** owns `Invoice`, `LedgerEntry`.\n* **Financial-Aid** owns `Award`, `AidDisbursement`.\n* Shared Kernel’s `AuditTrail` is append-only; no service can mutate.\n\n| Service        | DB Schema          | Tx Boundary (Local ACID)    |\n|----------------|--------------------|-----------------------------|\n| admissions     | `admissions.*`     | `Student`, `Applicant`      |\n| ledger-core    | `ledger.*`         | `Invoice`, `LedgerEntry`    |\n| aid-engine     | `aid.*`            | `Award`, `Disbursement`     |\n| micro-cred     | `microcred.*`      | `CourseRun`, `SplitPayout`  |\n\n## Resilience Policy\n\n1. Idempotent command handlers.\n2. At-least-once event delivery (NATS JetStream).\n3. Circuit-breaker around external PSP, open after 3 failures / 30 s.\n4. Saga compensation for inter-context failure.\n\n```c\n/* infra/circuit_breaker.h */\n#ifndef EP_INFRA_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_H\n#define EP_INFRA_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_H\n#include <time.h>\ntypedef enum { CB_CLOSED, CB_OPEN, CB_HALF_OPEN } cb_state_t;\n\ntypedef struct circuit_breaker {\n    cb_state_t state;\n    unsigned   failure_count;\n    time_t     last_failure;\n    unsigned   open_timeout_sec;\n    unsigned   threshold;\n} circuit_breaker_t;\n\nvoid cb_init(circuit_breaker_t *cb,\n             unsigned threshold,\n             unsigned open_timeout_sec);\n\nint  cb_before_request(circuit_breaker_t *cb);\nvoid cb_after_success(circuit_breaker_t *cb);\nvoid cb_after_failure(circuit_breaker_t *cb);\n\n#endif /* EP_INFRA_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_H */\n```\n\n## Build & Deployment Standards\n\n* `Dockerfile` uses scratch + `musl-clang` static linking ⇒ 6 MB images.\n* Each service exports `/livez` and `/readyz` over HTTP on port 8080.\n* Kubernetes Helm charts pin CPU/memory, configure HPA on NATS lag.\n\n---\n\n> “Architecture is about the important stuff—whatever that is.”  \n> — Ralph Johnson\n\nLearners should fork this repository, enable **Saga Pattern\nDemonstration Mode** (`make chaos-on`), and trigger the `tuition\ncheckout` flow.  When the intentional fault in step 2 forces a\nrollback, open the **AuditTrail dashboard** and correlate the saga\ncompensating transactions.\n\nHappy hacking!","numLines":326,"startLine":1,"totalLines":326}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01LXpX5NovVJKL1BD5NbtdLX","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→```markdown\n     2→---\n     3→title: EduPay Ledger Academy – CQRS & Event Sourcing Architecture\n     4→description: Technical deep-dive into how the EduPay Ledger Academy executes Command-Query Responsibility Segregation (CQRS) and Event Sourcing in C while remaining faithful to Clean Architecture.\n     5→slug: /architecture/cqrs-and-event-sourcing\n     6→---\n     7→\n     8→# 03 – CQRS & Event Sourcing\n     9→\n    10→> “Separate **what** the system does from **what** the system knows.”  \n    11→> — EduPay Core Team\n    12→\n    13→EduPay Ledger Academy employs **CQRS** to divide **mutations** (Commands) from **reads** (Queries) so that educators can swap persistence technologies, message buses, or reporting databases without touching domain logic.   \n    14→All state-changing requests are persisted as **immutable domain events**, enabling deterministic replay, powerful audit trails, and student-friendly time-travel debugging.\n    15→\n    16→---\n    17→\n    18→## Table of Contents\n    19→\n    20→1. Domain Vocabulary  \n    21→2. High Level Flow Diagram  \n    22→3. Command Pipeline (Write Model)  \n    23→4. Event Store Implementation (C)  \n    24→5. Query Model & Projections  \n    25→6. Consistency, Concurrency, and Idempotency  \n    26→7. Failure Injection Labs  \n    27→8. Further Reading\n    28→\n    29→---\n    30→\n    31→## 1. Domain Vocabulary\n    32→\n    33→Term | Definition\n    34→---- | ----------\n    35→**Command** | An intent _to do_ something. Must either succeed or return an error.\n    36→**Event** | A fact _that something happened_. Can never change or be removed.\n    37→**Aggregate** | A transactional consistency boundary, e.g., `LedgerAccount`.\n    38→**Projection / Read Model** | A denormalized view optimized for reads: dashboards, ledgers, etc.\n    39→\n    40→---\n    41→\n    42→## 2. High Level Flow Diagram\n    43→\n    44→```mermaid\n    45→sequenceDiagram\n    46→    actor Student\n    47→    participant CMD as Command API\n    48→    participant CH as Command Handler\n    49→    participant ES as Event Store (append-only)\n    50→    participant PR as Projector\n    51→    participant RM as Read Model (SQLite)\n    52→    Student->>CMD: POST /accounts/deposit\n    53→    CMD->>CH: DepositFundsCommand\n    54→    CH->>ES: Append(EventDeposited)\n    55→    ES->>PR: Emit(EventDeposited)\n    56→    PR->>RM: UPDATE balances\n    57→    Student->>RM: GET /dashboard\n    58→```\n    59→\n    60→---\n    61→\n    62→## 3. Command Pipeline (Write Model)\n    63→\n    64→Below is a **compile-ready** excerpt (C17) showing how a `CommandBus` validates and dispatches commands to their handlers.  \n    65→It purposefully decouples IO so professors can mock the bus during unit tests.\n    66→\n    67→```c\n    68→/* File: src/cqrs/command_bus.h */\n    69→#ifndef EDU_PAY_COMMAND_BUS_H\n    70→#define EDU_PAY_COMMAND_BUS_H\n    71→\n    72→#include <stdint.h>\n    73→#include <stddef.h>\n    74→#include \"utils/uuid.h\"\n    75→\n    76→/* ---------- Generic Command Definition ---------- */\n    77→typedef struct {\n    78→    uuid_t   id;         /* Correlation ID for tracing */\n    79→    uint64_t timestamp;  /* epoch millis (monotonic)   */\n    80→    const char *type;    /* \"DepositFundsCommand\" etc  */\n    81→    void     *payload;   /* domain-specific struct ptr */\n    82→} command_t;\n    83→\n    84→/* Error codes follow POSIX semantics for reuse in shell scripts */\n    85→typedef enum {\n    86→    CMD_OK          = 0,\n    87→    CMD_ERR_INPUT   = 22,  /* EINVAL */\n    88→    CMD_ERR_HANDLER = 38,  /* ENOSYS */\n    89→    CMD_ERR_REJECT  = 35   /* EDEADLK – business rule violated */\n    90→} command_status_e;\n    91→\n    92→/* Opaque route handle resolves to specific handler at runtime */\n    93→typedef command_status_e (*command_handler_f)(const command_t *cmd);\n    94→\n    95→/* Register handler */\n    96→int command_bus_register(const char *type, command_handler_f fn);\n    97→\n    98→/* Dispatch (synchronous) */\n    99→command_status_e command_bus_execute(const command_t *cmd);\n   100→\n   101→#endif /* EDU_PAY_COMMAND_BUS_H */\n   102→```\n   103→\n   104→```c\n   105→/* File: src/cqrs/command_bus.c */\n   106→#include \"cqrs/command_bus.h\"\n   107→#include <stdlib.h>\n   108→#include <string.h>\n   109→#include \"utils/hashtable.h\"\n   110→\n   111→/* --- Very light associative table (string → function ptr) --- */\n   112→static hash_table_t *router = NULL;\n   113→\n   114→int command_bus_register(const char *type, command_handler_f fn) {\n   115→    if (!type || !fn) return -CMD_ERR_INPUT;\n   116→    if (!router) router = ht_create(32);\n   117→    return ht_put(router, type, fn);\n   118→}\n   119→\n   120→command_status_e command_bus_execute(const command_t *cmd) {\n   121→    if (!cmd || !cmd->type) return CMD_ERR_INPUT;\n   122→    command_handler_f fn = ht_get(router, cmd->type);\n   123→    if (!fn) return CMD_ERR_HANDLER;\n   124→    return fn(cmd);\n   125→}\n   126→```\n   127→\n   128→Example Domain Command + Handler:\n   129→\n   130→```c\n   131→/* File: src/payments/commands/deposit_funds.h */\n   132→#ifndef EDU_PAY_COMMAND_DEPOSIT_H\n   133→#define EDU_PAY_COMMAND_DEPOSIT_H\n   134→#include <stdint.h>\n   135→\n   136→typedef struct {\n   137→    char     account_id[36];\n   138→    uint64_t amount_cents;\n   139→    char     currency[4];        /* \"USD\" | \"EUR\" | etc. */\n   140→} deposit_funds_payload_t;\n   141→\n   142→#endif\n   143→```\n   144→\n   145→```c\n   146→/* File: src/payments/handlers/deposit_funds_handler.c */\n   147→#include \"cqrs/command_bus.h\"\n   148→#include \"events/event_bus.h\"\n   149→#include \"payments/commands/deposit_funds.h\"\n   150→#include \"payments/events/funds_deposited.h\"\n   151→#include \"domain/accounts_repository.h\"\n   152→#include \"utils/clock.h\"\n   153→#include \"utils/currency.h\"\n   154→\n   155→static command_status_e handle_deposit(const command_t *cmd)\n   156→{\n   157→    if (!cmd || !cmd->payload) return CMD_ERR_INPUT;\n   158→    const deposit_funds_payload_t *pl = cmd->payload;\n   159→\n   160→    /* --- Business Rule Enforcement --- */\n   161→    if (!currency_is_supported(pl->currency) || pl->amount_cents == 0)\n   162→        return CMD_ERR_REJECT;\n   163→\n   164→    /* Optimistic lock fetch */\n   165→    ledger_account_t acct;\n   166→    if (accounts_repo_load(pl->account_id, &acct) != 0)\n   167→        return CMD_ERR_REJECT; /* Unknown account */\n   168→\n   169→    /* Domain invariants can run here (e.g., AML limits) */\n   170→    /* ... */\n   171→\n   172→    /* --- Produce Event (immutably) --- */\n   173→    funds_deposited_event_t ev = {\n   174→        .base.id        = uuid_v4(),\n   175→        .base.timestamp = clock_epoch_ms(),\n   176→        .base.type      = \"FundsDepositedEvent\",\n   177→        .account_id     = {0},\n   178→        .amount_cents   = pl->amount_cents,\n   179→        .currency       = {0}\n   180→    };\n   181→    strncpy(ev.account_id, pl->account_id, sizeof(ev.account_id)-1);\n   182→    strncpy(ev.currency, pl->currency,   sizeof(ev.currency)-1);\n   183→\n   184→    /* Persist via Event Bus (which writes through to Event Store) */\n   185→    if (event_bus_publish((event_t *)&ev) != EVT_OK)\n   186→        return CMD_ERR_HANDLER;\n   187→\n   188→    return CMD_OK;\n   189→}\n   190→\n   191→/* Auto-register at startup */\n   192→__attribute__((constructor))\n   193→static void _deposit_register(void) {\n   194→    command_bus_register(\"DepositFundsCommand\", handle_deposit);\n   195→}\n   196→```\n   197→\n   198→---\n   199→\n   200→## 4. Event Store Implementation (C)\n   201→\n   202→The **Event Store** is append-only, leverages `mmap(2)` for re-play speed, and stores each record as length-prefixed JSON so that students can grep logs with *jq*.\n   203→\n   204→```c\n   205→/* File: src/event_store/event_store.h */\n   206→#ifndef EDU_PAY_EVENT_STORE_H\n   207→#define EDU_PAY_EVENT_STORE_H\n   208→\n   209→#include <stddef.h>\n   210→#include <stdint.h>\n   211→#include \"events/event.h\"\n   212→\n   213→#define EVT_STORE_OK          0\n   214→#define EVT_STORE_ERR_IO    (-5)\n   215→#define EVT_STORE_ERR_FULL (-28)    /* ENOSPC */\n   216→\n   217→/* Append single event (thread-safe, fsync’ed) */\n   218→int event_store_append(const event_t *ev);\n   219→\n   220→/* Stream all events for replay (blocking). \n   221→   Calls `callback` for each event; stop when it returns non-zero. */\n   222→typedef int (*event_stream_cb)(const event_t *ev, void *ctx);\n   223→int event_store_stream(event_stream_cb callback, void *ctx);\n   224→\n   225→#endif\n   226→```\n   227→\n   228→```c\n   229→/* File: src/event_store/event_store.c */\n   230→#define _POSIX_C_SOURCE 200809L\n   231→#include \"event_store/event_store.h\"\n   232→#include <fcntl.h>\n   233→#include <sys/mman.h>\n   234→#include <sys/stat.h>\n   235→#include <unistd.h>\n   236→#include <pthread.h>\n   237→#include <stdio.h>\n   238→#include <string.h>\n   239→#include \"utils/serialize_json.h\"\n   240→#include \"utils/spinlock.h\"\n   241→\n   242→#define EVENT_STORE_FILE \"./var/event_store.log\"\n   243→#define MAX_EVENT_SIZE   4096   /* hard cap to aid undergrad sanity */\n   244→\n   245→static int fd = -1;\n   246→static pthread_spinlock_t lock;\n   247→\n   248→__attribute__((constructor))\n   249→static void _init_store(void) {\n   250→    fd = open(EVENT_STORE_FILE, O_RDWR | O_CREAT | O_APPEND, 0640);\n   251→    pthread_spin_init(&lock, PTHREAD_PROCESS_PRIVATE);\n   252→}\n   253→\n   254→__attribute__((destructor))\n   255→static void _close_store(void) {\n   256→    if (fd != -1) close(fd);\n   257→    pthread_spin_destroy(&lock);\n   258→}\n   259→\n   260→int event_store_append(const event_t *ev)\n   261→{\n   262→    if (!ev) return EVT_STORE_ERR_IO;\n   263→\n   264→    char buffer[MAX_EVENT_SIZE];\n   265→    ssize_t len = json_serialize_event(ev, buffer, sizeof(buffer));\n   266→    if (len < 0) return EVT_STORE_ERR_IO;\n   267→\n   268→    /* Length-prefixed frame */\n   269→    uint32_t nlen = (uint32_t)len;\n   270→    pthread_spin_lock(&lock);\n   271→    ssize_t w = write(fd, &nlen, sizeof(nlen));\n   272→    w += write(fd, buffer, len);\n   273→    fsync(fd);\n   274→    pthread_spin_unlock(&lock);\n   275→\n   276→    return (w == (ssize_t)(sizeof(nlen) + len)) ? EVT_STORE_OK\n   277→                                                : EVT_STORE_ERR_IO;\n   278→}\n   279→\n   280→int event_store_stream(event_stream_cb cb, void *ctx)\n   281→{\n   282→    if (!cb) return EVT_STORE_ERR_IO;\n   283→\n   284→    int sfd = open(EVENT_STORE_FILE, O_RDONLY);\n   285→    if (sfd < 0) return EVT_STORE_ERR_IO;\n   286→\n   287→    uint32_t nlen;\n   288→    while (read(sfd, &nlen, sizeof(nlen)) == sizeof(nlen)) {\n   289→        if (nlen > MAX_EVENT_SIZE) { close(sfd); return EVT_STORE_ERR_IO; }\n   290→        char buffer[MAX_EVENT_SIZE];\n   291→        if (read(sfd, buffer, nlen) != (ssize_t)nlen) break;\n   292→\n   293→        event_t *ev = json_deserialize_event(buffer, nlen);\n   294→        if (!ev) continue;\n   295→        if (cb(ev, ctx) != 0) { free(ev); break; }\n   296→        free(ev);\n   297→    }\n   298→    close(sfd);\n   299→    return EVT_STORE_OK;\n   300→}\n   301→```\n   302→\n   303→---\n   304→\n   305→## 5. Query Model & Projections\n   306→\n   307→Read models are **eventually consistent**. Every event published above is fanned-out to one or more *Projectors* that transform it into query-optimized databases (SQLite, Redis, CSV for Excel labs).\n   308→\n   309→```c\n   310→/* File: src/projections/balance_projection.c */\n   311→#include \"events/event_bus.h\"\n   312→#include \"payments/events/funds_deposited.h\"\n   313→#include \"database/sqlite.h\"\n   314→\n   315→static int on_funds_deposited(const event_t *ev)\n   316→{\n   317→    const funds_deposited_event_t *e = (const funds_deposited_event_t *)ev;\n   318→    return sqlite_exec(\n   319→        \"INSERT INTO account_balance(account_id, currency, balance_cents) \"\n   320→        \"VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3) \"\n   321→        \"ON CONFLICT(account_id, currency) \"\n   322→        \"DO UPDATE SET balance_cents = balance_cents + excluded.balance_cents;\",\n   323→        e->account_id, e->currency, (int64_t)e->amount_cents);\n   324→}\n   325→\n   326→__attribute__((constructor))\n   327→static void _register_projector(void) {\n   328→    event_bus_subscribe(\"FundsDepositedEvent\", on_funds_deposited);\n   329→}\n   330→```\n   331→\n   332→Students can query via the REST shell or `sqlite3 var/read_models.db`.\n   333→\n   334→---\n   335→\n   336→## 6. Consistency, Concurrency, and Idempotency\n   337→\n   338→• **Concurrency Control** – Each aggregate carries a monotonically increasing `version`. Handlers pass the expected `version` into `event_store_append()`.  \n   339→• **At-Least-Once Delivery** – Projectors keep an `offset` table so they can re-play events idempotently.  \n   340→• **Snap-Shots** – Long-lived aggregates (>2 000 events) periodically persist snapshots to avoid hot-loop rehydration in class.\n   341→\n   342→---\n   343→\n   344→## 7. Failure Injection Labs\n   345→\n   346→Activate with:\n   347→\n   348→```bash\n   349→# Simulate projector downtime\n   350→$ make simulate_projector_outage DURATION=30s\n   351→\n   352→# Induce write-model crash mid-transaction\n   353→$ make chaos_saga MODULE=payments\n   354→```\n   355→\n   356→Students then debug replay & compensating actions per the **Saga Pattern**.\n   357→\n   358→---\n   359→\n   360→## 8. Domain Vocabulary  \n2. High Level Flow Diagram  \n3. Command Pipeline (Write Model)  \n4. Event Store Implementation (C)  \n5. Query Model & Projections  \n6. Consistency, Concurrency, and Idempotency  \n7. Failure Injection Labs  \n8. Further Reading\n\n---\n\n## 1. Domain Vocabulary\n\nTerm | Definition\n---- | ----------\n**Command** | An intent _to do_ something. Must either succeed or return an error.\n**Event** | A fact _that something happened_. Can never change or be removed.\n**Aggregate** | A transactional consistency boundary, e.g., `LedgerAccount`.\n**Projection / Read Model** | A denormalized view optimized for reads: dashboards, ledgers, etc.\n\n---\n\n## 2. High Level Flow Diagram\n\n```mermaid\nsequenceDiagram\n    actor Student\n    participant CMD as Command API\n    participant CH as Command Handler\n    participant ES as Event Store (append-only)\n    participant PR as Projector\n    participant RM as Read Model (SQLite)\n    Student->>CMD: POST /accounts/deposit\n    CMD->>CH: DepositFundsCommand\n    CH->>ES: Append(EventDeposited)\n    ES->>PR: Emit(EventDeposited)\n    PR->>RM: UPDATE balances\n    Student->>RM: GET /dashboard\n```\n\n---\n\n## 3. Command Pipeline (Write Model)\n\nBelow is a **compile-ready** excerpt (C17) showing how a `CommandBus` validates and dispatches commands to their handlers.  \nIt purposefully decouples IO so professors can mock the bus during unit tests.\n\n```c\n/* File: src/cqrs/command_bus.h */\n#ifndef EDU_PAY_COMMAND_BUS_H\n#define EDU_PAY_COMMAND_BUS_H\n\n#include <stdint.h>\n#include <stddef.h>\n#include \"utils/uuid.h\"\n\n/* ---------- Generic Command Definition ---------- */\ntypedef struct {\n    uuid_t   id;         /* Correlation ID for tracing */\n    uint64_t timestamp;  /* epoch millis (monotonic)   */\n    const char *type;    /* \"DepositFundsCommand\" etc  */\n    void     *payload;   /* domain-specific struct ptr */\n} command_t;\n\n/* Error codes follow POSIX semantics for reuse in shell scripts */\ntypedef enum {\n    CMD_OK          = 0,\n    CMD_ERR_INPUT   = 22,  /* EINVAL */\n    CMD_ERR_HANDLER = 38,  /* ENOSYS */\n    CMD_ERR_REJECT  = 35   /* EDEADLK – business rule violated */\n} command_status_e;\n\n/* Opaque route handle resolves to specific handler at runtime */\ntypedef command_status_e (*command_handler_f)(const command_t *cmd);\n\n/* Register handler */\nint command_bus_register(const char *type, command_handler_f fn);\n\n/* Dispatch (synchronous) */\ncommand_status_e command_bus_execute(const command_t *cmd);\n\n#endif /* EDU_PAY_COMMAND_BUS_H */\n```\n\n```c\n/* File: src/cqrs/command_bus.c */\n#include \"cqrs/command_bus.h\"\n#include <stdlib.h>\n#include <string.h>\n#include \"utils/hashtable.h\"\n\n/* --- Very light associative table (string → function ptr) --- */\nstatic hash_table_t *router = NULL;\n\nint command_bus_register(const char *type, command_handler_f fn) {\n    if (!type || !fn) return -CMD_ERR_INPUT;\n    if (!router) router = ht_create(32);\n    return ht_put(router, type, fn);\n}\n\ncommand_status_e command_bus_execute(const command_t *cmd) {\n    if (!cmd || !cmd->type) return CMD_ERR_INPUT;\n    command_handler_f fn = ht_get(router, cmd->type);\n    if (!fn) return CMD_ERR_HANDLER;\n    return fn(cmd);\n}\n```\n\nExample Domain Command + Handler:\n\n```c\n/* File: src/payments/commands/deposit_funds.h */\n#ifndef EDU_PAY_COMMAND_DEPOSIT_H\n#define EDU_PAY_COMMAND_DEPOSIT_H\n#include <stdint.h>\n\ntypedef struct {\n    char     account_id[36];\n    uint64_t amount_cents;\n    char     currency[4];        /* \"USD\" | \"EUR\" | etc. */\n} deposit_funds_payload_t;\n\n#endif\n```\n\n```c\n/* File: src/payments/handlers/deposit_funds_handler.c */\n#include \"cqrs/command_bus.h\"\n#include \"events/event_bus.h\"\n#include \"payments/commands/deposit_funds.h\"\n#include \"payments/events/funds_deposited.h\"\n#include \"domain/accounts_repository.h\"\n#include \"utils/clock.h\"\n#include \"utils/currency.h\"\n\nstatic command_status_e handle_deposit(const command_t *cmd)\n{\n    if (!cmd || !cmd->payload) return CMD_ERR_INPUT;\n    const deposit_funds_payload_t *pl = cmd->payload;\n\n    /* --- Business Rule Enforcement --- */\n    if (!currency_is_supported(pl->currency) || pl->amount_cents == 0)\n        return CMD_ERR_REJECT;\n\n    /* Optimistic lock fetch */\n    ledger_account_t acct;\n    if (accounts_repo_load(pl->account_id, &acct) != 0)\n        return CMD_ERR_REJECT; /* Unknown account */\n\n    /* Domain invariants can run here (e.g., AML limits) */\n    /* ... */\n\n    /* --- Produce Event (immutably) --- */\n    funds_deposited_event_t ev = {\n        .base.id        = uuid_v4(),\n        .base.timestamp = clock_epoch_ms(),\n        .base.type      = \"FundsDepositedEvent\",\n        .account_id     = {0},\n        .amount_cents   = pl->amount_cents,\n        .currency       = {0}\n    };\n    strncpy(ev.account_id, pl->account_id, sizeof(ev.account_id)-1);\n    strncpy(ev.currency, pl->currency,   sizeof(ev.currency)-1);\n\n    /* Persist via Event Bus (which writes through to Event Store) */\n    if (event_bus_publish((event_t *)&ev) != EVT_OK)\n        return CMD_ERR_HANDLER;\n\n    return CMD_OK;\n}\n\n/* Auto-register at startup */\n__attribute__((constructor))\nstatic void _deposit_register(void) {\n    command_bus_register(\"DepositFundsCommand\", handle_deposit);\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. Event Store Implementation (C)\n\nThe **Event Store** is append-only, leverages `mmap(2)` for re-play speed, and stores each record as length-prefixed JSON so that students can grep logs with *jq*.\n\n```c\n/* File: src/event_store/event_store.h */\n#ifndef EDU_PAY_EVENT_STORE_H\n#define EDU_PAY_EVENT_STORE_H\n\n#include <stddef.h>\n#include <stdint.h>\n#include \"events/event.h\"\n\n#define EVT_STORE_OK          0\n#define EVT_STORE_ERR_IO    (-5)\n#define EVT_STORE_ERR_FULL (-28)    /* ENOSPC */\n\n/* Append single event (thread-safe, fsync’ed) */\nint event_store_append(const event_t *ev);\n\n/* Stream all events for replay (blocking). \n   Calls `callback` for each event; stop when it returns non-zero. */\ntypedef int (*event_stream_cb)(const event_t *ev, void *ctx);\nint event_store_stream(event_stream_cb callback, void *ctx);\n\n#endif\n```\n\n```c\n/* File: src/event_store/event_store.c */\n#define _POSIX_C_SOURCE 200809L\n#include \"event_store/event_store.h\"\n#include <fcntl.h>\n#include <sys/mman.h>\n#include <sys/stat.h>\n#include <unistd.h>\n#include <pthread.h>\n#include <stdio.h>\n#include <string.h>\n#include \"utils/serialize_json.h\"\n#include \"utils/spinlock.h\"\n\n#define EVENT_STORE_FILE \"./var/event_store.log\"\n#define MAX_EVENT_SIZE   4096   /* hard cap to aid undergrad sanity */\n\nstatic int fd = -1;\nstatic pthread_spinlock_t lock;\n\n__attribute__((constructor))\nstatic void _init_store(void) {\n    fd = open(EVENT_STORE_FILE, O_RDWR | O_CREAT | O_APPEND, 0640);\n    pthread_spin_init(&lock, PTHREAD_PROCESS_PRIVATE);\n}\n\n__attribute__((destructor))\nstatic void _close_store(void) {\n    if (fd != -1) close(fd);\n    pthread_spin_destroy(&lock);\n}\n\nint event_store_append(const event_t *ev)\n{\n    if (!ev) return EVT_STORE_ERR_IO;\n\n    char buffer[MAX_EVENT_SIZE];\n    ssize_t len = json_serialize_event(ev, buffer, sizeof(buffer));\n    if (len < 0) return EVT_STORE_ERR_IO;\n\n    /* Length-prefixed frame */\n    uint32_t nlen = (uint32_t)len;\n    pthread_spin_lock(&lock);\n    ssize_t w = write(fd, &nlen, sizeof(nlen));\n    w += write(fd, buffer, len);\n    fsync(fd);\n    pthread_spin_unlock(&lock);\n\n    return (w == (ssize_t)(sizeof(nlen) + len)) ? EVT_STORE_OK\n                                                : EVT_STORE_ERR_IO;\n}\n\nint event_store_stream(event_stream_cb cb, void *ctx)\n{\n    if (!cb) return EVT_STORE_ERR_IO;\n\n    int sfd = open(EVENT_STORE_FILE, O_RDONLY);\n    if (sfd < 0) return EVT_STORE_ERR_IO;\n\n    uint32_t nlen;\n    while (read(sfd, &nlen, sizeof(nlen)) == sizeof(nlen)) {\n        if (nlen > MAX_EVENT_SIZE) { close(sfd); return EVT_STORE_ERR_IO; }\n        char buffer[MAX_EVENT_SIZE];\n        if (read(sfd, buffer, nlen) != (ssize_t)nlen) break;\n\n        event_t *ev = json_deserialize_event(buffer, nlen);\n        if (!ev) continue;\n        if (cb(ev, ctx) != 0) { free(ev); break; }\n        free(ev);\n    }\n    close(sfd);\n    return EVT_STORE_OK;\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. Query Model & Projections\n\nRead models are **eventually consistent**. Every event published above is fanned-out to one or more *Projectors* that transform it into query-optimized databases (SQLite, Redis, CSV for Excel labs).\n\n```c\n/* File: src/projections/balance_projection.c */\n#include \"events/event_bus.h\"\n#include \"payments/events/funds_deposited.h\"\n#include \"database/sqlite.h\"\n\nstatic int on_funds_deposited(const event_t *ev)\n{\n    const funds_deposited_event_t *e = (const funds_deposited_event_t *)ev;\n    return sqlite_exec(\n        \"INSERT INTO account_balance(account_id, currency, balance_cents) \"\n        \"VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3) \"\n        \"ON CONFLICT(account_id, currency) \"\n        \"DO UPDATE SET balance_cents = balance_cents + excluded.balance_cents;\",\n        e->account_id, e->currency, (int64_t)e->amount_cents);\n}\n\n__attribute__((constructor))\nstatic void _register_projector(void) {\n    event_bus_subscribe(\"FundsDepositedEvent\", on_funds_deposited);\n}\n```\n\nStudents can query via the REST shell or `sqlite3 var/read_models.db`.\n\n---\n\n## 6. Consistency, Concurrency, and Idempotency\n\n• **Concurrency Control** – Each aggregate carries a monotonically increasing `version`. Handlers pass the expected `version` into `event_store_append()`.  \n• **At-Least-Once Delivery** – Projectors keep an `offset` table so they can re-play events idempotently.  \n• **Snap-Shots** – Long-lived aggregates (>2 000 events) periodically persist snapshots to avoid hot-loop rehydration in class.\n\n---\n\n## 7. Failure Injection Labs\n\nActivate with:\n\n```bash\n# Simulate projector downtime\n$ make simulate_projector_outage DURATION=30s\n\n# Induce write-model crash mid-transaction\n$ make chaos_saga MODULE=payments\n```\n\nStudents then debug replay & compensating actions per the **Saga Pattern**.\n\n---\n\n## 8. {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01HJ8Dt2J5BqArbGPhLrv8br","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→```markdown\n     2→# EduPay Ledger Academy  \n     3→### docs/architecture/04_saga_pattern.md\n     4→---\n     5→> Version: 1.3  \n     6→> Last Reviewed: 2024-05-09  \n     7→> Authors: Core Platform Team (@edupay-ledger)  \n     8→> Status: ✅ Production  \n     9→---\n    10→\n    11→## 1. Why EduPay Uses the Saga Pattern\n    12→\n    13→EduPay processes **millions of distributed, financial micro-transactions** each semester.  \n    14→The platform deliberately avoids *single-phase* database transactions because:\n    15→\n    16→* **Micro-services** compose payments from multiple bounded contexts (Admissions, Bursar, Treasury, FX, Fraud).  \n    17→* **Heterogeneous data stores** (PostgreSQL, Redis, EventStoreDB) participate in a single business flow.  \n    18→* **Resiliency labs** in coursework require controlled failure injection and roll-back.  \n    19→\n    20→A **Saga** slices a long-running business process into a series of *local* ACID transactions plus **compensating steps**.  \n    21→If any step fails, the orchestrator triggers rollback actions in **reverse order**, yielding *eventual consistency* without locking global resources.\n    22→\n    23→```\n    24→Enrollment_Fee → FX_Conversion → Treasury_Posting → Fraud_Scoring → Receipt_Email\n    25→         │                │                │              │\n    26→         └────────────────┴────────────────┴──────────────┴─────┐\n    27→                       Saga Orchestrator (payment_saga.c)       │\n    28→                       • persists state in Audit_Trail          │\n    29→                       • broadcasts events over EventBus        │\n    30→                       • drives rollbacks on failure ◄──────────┘\n    31→```\n    32→\n    33→---\n    34→\n    35→## 2. High-Level Flow\n    36→\n    37→1. **Create** a `payment_saga_t` record in the `audit_trail` stream.  \n    38→2. **Send** `SAGA_STEP_BEGIN` for *FX_Conversion* (`fx.c`).  \n    39→3. **Local commit** in FX; publish `SAGA_STEP_DONE`.  \n    40→4. Orchestrator continues to *Treasury_Posting* (`ledger.c`).  \n    41→5. If *Fraud_Scoring* fails, **publish** `SAGA_ABORT`.  \n    42→6. Participants receiving `SAGA_ABORT` invoke *compensating* handlers.  \n    43→7. Saga ends with `SAGA_ROLLBACK_DONE` or `SAGA_SUCCESS`.\n    44→\n    45→---\n    46→\n    47→## 3. Reference Implementation (C)\n    48→\n    49→All code is kept framework-agnostic, consistent with **Clean Architecture**.  \n    50→Dependencies limited to the standard C library (`pthread` for concurrency).\n    51→\n    52→### 3.1. Public Interface – `include/saga.h`\n    53→\n    54→```c\n    55→#ifndef EDUPAY_SAGA_H\n    56→#define EDUPAY_SAGA_H\n    57→\n    58→#include <stdint.h>\n    59→#include <stdbool.h>\n    60→\n    61→/* ---------- Event Bus ---------------------------------------------------- */\n    62→\n    63→typedef enum {\n    64→    EVT_SAGA_STEP_BEGIN,\n    65→    EVT_SAGA_STEP_DONE,\n    66→    EVT_SAGA_ABORT,\n    67→    EVT_SAGA_ROLLBACK_DONE,\n    68→    EVT_SAGA_SUCCESS\n    69→} saga_event_type_t;\n    70→\n    71→typedef struct {\n    72→    saga_event_type_t type;\n    73→    uint64_t          saga_id;\n    74→    const char       *step_name;   /* e.g., \"FX_Conversion\" */\n    75→    void             *payload;     /* opaque domain state   */\n    76→} saga_event_t;\n    77→\n    78→/* Subscriber callback signature */\n    79→typedef void (*event_handler_f)(const saga_event_t *evt, void *ctx);\n    80→\n    81→/* Registers a handler; returns 0 on success, -1 on error */\n    82→int eventbus_subscribe(event_handler_f cb, void *ctx);\n    83→\n    84→/* Broadcasts event to all subscribers; thread-safe */\n    85→int eventbus_publish(const saga_event_t *evt);\n    86→\n    87→/* ---------- Saga Orchestrator ------------------------------------------- */\n    88→\n    89→#define SAGA_MAX_STEPS 8\n    90→#define SAGA_STEP_NAME_MAX 32\n    91→\n    92→typedef int (*saga_step_f)(uint64_t saga_id, void **payload);\n    93→typedef int (*saga_comp_f)(uint64_t saga_id, void *payload);\n    94→\n    95→typedef struct {\n    96→    char         name[SAGA_STEP_NAME_MAX];\n    97→    saga_step_f  action;\n    98→    saga_comp_f  compensate;\n    99→} saga_step_def_t;\n   100→\n   101→typedef struct {\n   102→    uint64_t        id;\n   103→    uint8_t         step_count;\n   104→    uint8_t         current;\n   105→    saga_step_def_t steps[SAGA_MAX_STEPS];\n   106→    bool            aborted;\n   107→} payment_saga_t;\n   108→\n   109→/* Initializes a saga with the supplied steps */\n   110→void saga_init(payment_saga_t *saga, uint64_t id,\n   111→               const saga_step_def_t *steps,\n   112→               uint8_t step_count);\n   113→\n   114→/* Executes saga synchronously; returns 0 on success, -1 on failure */\n   115→int saga_execute(payment_saga_t *saga);\n   116→\n   117→#endif /* EDUPAY_SAGA_H */\n   118→```\n   119→\n   120→### 3.2. Core Implementation – `src/saga.c`\n   121→\n   122→```c\n   123→#include \"saga.h\"\n   124→#include <pthread.h>\n   125→#include <stdio.h>\n   126→#include <stdlib.h>\n   127→#include <string.h>\n   128→\n   129→#define BUS_MAX_HANDLERS 16\n   130→\n   131→/* ----------------- Simple In-Process Event Bus -------------------------- */\n   132→\n   133→typedef struct {\n   134→    event_handler_f cb;\n   135→    void           *ctx;\n   136→} handler_entry_t;\n   137→\n   138→static handler_entry_t BUS[BUS_MAX_HANDLERS];\n   139→static pthread_mutex_t BUS_LOCK = PTHREAD_MUTEX_INITIALIZER;\n   140→\n   141→int eventbus_subscribe(event_handler_f cb, void *ctx)\n   142→{\n   143→    pthread_mutex_lock(&BUS_LOCK);\n   144→    for (int i = 0; i < BUS_MAX_HANDLERS; ++i) {\n   145→        if (BUS[i].cb == NULL) {\n   146→            BUS[i].cb  = cb;\n   147→            BUS[i].ctx = ctx;\n   148→            pthread_mutex_unlock(&BUS_LOCK);\n   149→            return 0;\n   150→        }\n   151→    }\n   152→    pthread_mutex_unlock(&BUS_LOCK);\n   153→    return -1; /* No slots */\n   154→}\n   155→\n   156→int eventbus_publish(const saga_event_t *evt)\n   157→{\n   158→    pthread_mutex_lock(&BUS_LOCK);\n   159→    handler_entry_t handlers[BUS_MAX_HANDLERS];\n   160→    memcpy(handlers, BUS, sizeof(handlers));\n   161→    pthread_mutex_unlock(&BUS_LOCK);\n   162→\n   163→    for (int i = 0; i < BUS_MAX_HANDLERS; ++i) {\n   164→        if (handlers[i].cb)\n   165→            handlers[i].cb(evt, handlers[i].ctx);\n   166→    }\n   167→    return 0;\n   168→}\n   169→\n   170→/* ----------------- Saga Orchestration ---------------------------------- */\n   171→\n   172→static void publish_step_event(saga_event_type_t type,\n   173→                               const payment_saga_t *saga,\n   174→                               const char *step_name,\n   175→                               void *payload)\n   176→{\n   177→    saga_event_t evt = {\n   178→        .type      = type,\n   179→        .saga_id   = saga->id,\n   180→        .step_name = step_name,\n   181→        .payload   = payload\n   182→    };\n   183→    eventbus_publish(&evt);\n   184→}\n   185→\n   186→void saga_init(payment_saga_t *s, uint64_t id,\n   187→               const saga_step_def_t *steps,\n   188→               uint8_t step_count)\n   189→{\n   190→    memset(s, 0, sizeof(*s));\n   191→    s->id         = id;\n   192→    s->step_count = step_count > SAGA_MAX_STEPS ? SAGA_MAX_STEPS : step_count;\n   193→    memcpy(s->steps, steps, s->step_count * sizeof(saga_step_def_t));\n   194→}\n   195→\n   196→static int rollback(payment_saga_t *saga)\n   197→{\n   198→    for (int i = saga->current; i >= 0; --i) {\n   199→        saga_step_def_t *step = &saga->steps[i];\n   200→        if (step->compensate) {\n   201→            publish_step_event(EVT_SAGA_ABORT, saga, step->name, NULL);\n   202→            if (step->compensate(saga->id, NULL) != 0)\n   203→                fprintf(stderr, \"⚠  Compensation failed at %s\\n\", step->name);\n   204→        }\n   205→    }\n   206→    saga_event_t done = {\n   207→        .type      = EVT_SAGA_ROLLBACK_DONE,\n   208→        .saga_id   = saga->id,\n   209→        .step_name = \"ROLLBACK\"\n   210→    };\n   211→    eventbus_publish(&done);\n   212→    return -1;\n   213→}\n   214→\n   215→int saga_execute(payment_saga_t *saga)\n   216→{\n   217→    for (saga->current = 0; saga->current < saga->step_count; ++saga->current) {\n   218→        saga_step_def_t *step = &saga->steps[saga->current];\n   219→\n   220→        publish_step_event(EVT_SAGA_STEP_BEGIN, saga, step->name, NULL);\n   221→\n   222→        void *payload = NULL;\n   223→        if (step->action(saga->id, &payload) != 0) {\n   224→            fprintf(stderr, \"❌  Saga %lu failed at step %s\\n\",\n   225→                    saga->id, step->name);\n   226→            saga->aborted = true;\n   227→            return rollback(saga);\n   228→        }\n   229→\n   230→        publish_step_event(EVT_SAGA_STEP_DONE, saga, step->name, payload);\n   231→    }\n   232→\n   233→    saga_event_t ok = {\n   234→        .type      = EVT_SAGA_SUCCESS,\n   235→        .saga_id   = saga->id,\n   236→        .step_name = \"COMPLETED\"\n   237→    };\n   238→    eventbus_publish(&ok);\n   239→    return 0;\n   240→}\n   241→```\n   242→\n   243→### 3.3. Example Steps – `src/steps_fx.c`, `src/steps_ledger.c`\n   244→\n   245→```c\n   246→/* src/steps_fx.c */\n   247→#include \"saga.h\"\n   248→#include <stdio.h>\n   249→\n   250→int fx_convert(uint64_t id, void **payload)\n   251→{\n   252→    (void)payload; /* Not needed for demo */\n   253→    printf(\"💱  FX_Conversion performed for saga %lu\\n\", id);\n   254→    /* simulate success */\n   255→    return 0;\n   256→}\n   257→\n   258→int fx_reverse(uint64_t id, void *payload)\n   259→{\n   260→    (void)payload;\n   261→    printf(\"↩  FX_Conversion reversed for saga %lu\\n\", id);\n   262→    return 0;\n   263→}\n   264→```\n   265→\n   266→```c\n   267→/* src/steps_ledger.c */\n   268→#include \"saga.h\"\n   269→#include <stdio.h>\n   270→\n   271→int ledger_post(uint64_t id, void **payload)\n   272→{\n   273→    (void)payload;\n   274→    printf(\"📒  Ledger posting OK for saga %lu\\n\", id);\n   275→    return 0;\n   276→}\n   277→\n   278→int ledger_reverse(uint64_t id, void *payload)\n   279→{\n   280→    (void)payload;\n   281→    printf(\"↩  Ledger posting reversed for saga %lu\\n\", id);\n   282→    return 0;\n   283→}\n   284→```\n   285→\n   286→### 3.4. Why EduPay Uses the Saga Pattern\n\nEduPay processes **millions of distributed, financial micro-transactions** each semester.  \nThe platform deliberately avoids *single-phase* database transactions because:\n\n* **Micro-services** compose payments from multiple bounded contexts (Admissions, Bursar, Treasury, FX, Fraud).  \n* **Heterogeneous data stores** (PostgreSQL, Redis, EventStoreDB) participate in a single business flow.  \n* **Resiliency labs** in coursework require controlled failure injection and roll-back.  \n\nA **Saga** slices a long-running business process into a series of *local* ACID transactions plus **compensating steps**.  \nIf any step fails, the orchestrator triggers rollback actions in **reverse order**, yielding *eventual consistency* without locking global resources.\n\n```\nEnrollment_Fee → FX_Conversion → Treasury_Posting → Fraud_Scoring → Receipt_Email\n         │                │                │              │\n         └────────────────┴────────────────┴──────────────┴─────┐\n                       Saga Orchestrator (payment_saga.c)       │\n                       • persists state in Audit_Trail          │\n                       • broadcasts events over EventBus        │\n                       • drives rollbacks on failure ◄──────────┘\n```\n\n---\n\n## 2. High-Level Flow\n\n1. **Create** a `payment_saga_t` record in the `audit_trail` stream.  \n2. **Send** `SAGA_STEP_BEGIN` for *FX_Conversion* (`fx.c`).  \n3. **Local commit** in FX; publish `SAGA_STEP_DONE`.  \n4. Orchestrator continues to *Treasury_Posting* (`ledger.c`).  \n5. If *Fraud_Scoring* fails, **publish** `SAGA_ABORT`.  \n6. Participants receiving `SAGA_ABORT` invoke *compensating* handlers.  \n7. Saga ends with `SAGA_ROLLBACK_DONE` or `SAGA_SUCCESS`.\n\n---\n\n## 3. Reference Implementation (C)\n\nAll code is kept framework-agnostic, consistent with **Clean Architecture**.  \nDependencies limited to the standard C library (`pthread` for concurrency).\n\n### 3.1. Public Interface – `include/saga.h`\n\n```c\n#ifndef EDUPAY_SAGA_H\n#define EDUPAY_SAGA_H\n\n#include <stdint.h>\n#include <stdbool.h>\n\n/* ---------- Event Bus ---------------------------------------------------- */\n\ntypedef enum {\n    EVT_SAGA_STEP_BEGIN,\n    EVT_SAGA_STEP_DONE,\n    EVT_SAGA_ABORT,\n    EVT_SAGA_ROLLBACK_DONE,\n    EVT_SAGA_SUCCESS\n} saga_event_type_t;\n\ntypedef struct {\n    saga_event_type_t type;\n    uint64_t          saga_id;\n    const char       *step_name;   /* e.g., \"FX_Conversion\" */\n    void             *payload;     /* opaque domain state   */\n} saga_event_t;\n\n/* Subscriber callback signature */\ntypedef void (*event_handler_f)(const saga_event_t *evt, void *ctx);\n\n/* Registers a handler; returns 0 on success, -1 on error */\nint eventbus_subscribe(event_handler_f cb, void *ctx);\n\n/* Broadcasts event to all subscribers; thread-safe */\nint eventbus_publish(const saga_event_t *evt);\n\n/* ---------- Saga Orchestrator ------------------------------------------- */\n\n#define SAGA_MAX_STEPS 8\n#define SAGA_STEP_NAME_MAX 32\n\ntypedef int (*saga_step_f)(uint64_t saga_id, void **payload);\ntypedef int (*saga_comp_f)(uint64_t saga_id, void *payload);\n\ntypedef struct {\n    char         name[SAGA_STEP_NAME_MAX];\n    saga_step_f  action;\n    saga_comp_f  compensate;\n} saga_step_def_t;\n\ntypedef struct {\n    uint64_t        id;\n    uint8_t         step_count;\n    uint8_t         current;\n    saga_step_def_t steps[SAGA_MAX_STEPS];\n    bool            aborted;\n} payment_saga_t;\n\n/* Initializes a saga with the supplied steps */\nvoid saga_init(payment_saga_t *saga, uint64_t id,\n               const saga_step_def_t *steps,\n               uint8_t step_count);\n\n/* Executes saga synchronously; returns 0 on success, -1 on failure */\nint saga_execute(payment_saga_t *saga);\n\n#endif /* EDUPAY_SAGA_H */\n```\n\n### 3.2. Core Implementation – `src/saga.c`\n\n```c\n#include \"saga.h\"\n#include <pthread.h>\n#include <stdio.h>\n#include <stdlib.h>\n#include <string.h>\n\n#define BUS_MAX_HANDLERS 16\n\n/* ----------------- Simple In-Process Event Bus -------------------------- */\n\ntypedef struct {\n    event_handler_f cb;\n    void           *ctx;\n} handler_entry_t;\n\nstatic handler_entry_t BUS[BUS_MAX_HANDLERS];\nstatic pthread_mutex_t BUS_LOCK = PTHREAD_MUTEX_INITIALIZER;\n\nint eventbus_subscribe(event_handler_f cb, void *ctx)\n{\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&BUS_LOCK);\n    for (int i = 0; i < BUS_MAX_HANDLERS; ++i) {\n        if (BUS[i].cb == NULL) {\n            BUS[i].cb  = cb;\n            BUS[i].ctx = ctx;\n            pthread_mutex_unlock(&BUS_LOCK);\n            return 0;\n        }\n    }\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&BUS_LOCK);\n    return -1; /* No slots */\n}\n\nint eventbus_publish(const saga_event_t *evt)\n{\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&BUS_LOCK);\n    handler_entry_t handlers[BUS_MAX_HANDLERS];\n    memcpy(handlers, BUS, sizeof(handlers));\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&BUS_LOCK);\n\n    for (int i = 0; i < BUS_MAX_HANDLERS; ++i) {\n        if (handlers[i].cb)\n            handlers[i].cb(evt, handlers[i].ctx);\n    }\n    return 0;\n}\n\n/* ----------------- Saga Orchestration ---------------------------------- */\n\nstatic void publish_step_event(saga_event_type_t type,\n                               const payment_saga_t *saga,\n                               const char *step_name,\n                               void *payload)\n{\n    saga_event_t evt = {\n        .type      = type,\n        .saga_id   = saga->id,\n        .step_name = step_name,\n        .payload   = payload\n    };\n    eventbus_publish(&evt);\n}\n\nvoid saga_init(payment_saga_t *s, uint64_t id,\n               const saga_step_def_t *steps,\n               uint8_t step_count)\n{\n    memset(s, 0, sizeof(*s));\n    s->id         = id;\n    s->step_count = step_count > SAGA_MAX_STEPS ? SAGA_MAX_STEPS : step_count;\n    memcpy(s->steps, steps, s->step_count * sizeof(saga_step_def_t));\n}\n\nstatic int rollback(payment_saga_t *saga)\n{\n    for (int i = saga->current; i >= 0; --i) {\n        saga_step_def_t *step = &saga->steps[i];\n        if (step->compensate) {\n            publish_step_event(EVT_SAGA_ABORT, saga, step->name, NULL);\n            if (step->compensate(saga->id, NULL) != 0)\n                fprintf(stderr, \"⚠  Compensation failed at %s\\n\", step->name);\n        }\n    }\n    saga_event_t done = {\n        .type      = EVT_SAGA_ROLLBACK_DONE,\n        .saga_id   = saga->id,\n        .step_name = \"ROLLBACK\"\n    };\n    eventbus_publish(&done);\n    return -1;\n}\n\nint saga_execute(payment_saga_t *saga)\n{\n    for (saga->current = 0; saga->current < saga->step_count; ++saga->current) {\n        saga_step_def_t *step = &saga->steps[saga->current];\n\n        publish_step_event(EVT_SAGA_STEP_BEGIN, saga, step->name, NULL);\n\n        void *payload = NULL;\n        if (step->action(saga->id, &payload) != 0) {\n            fprintf(stderr, \"❌  Saga %lu failed at step %s\\n\",\n                    saga->id, step->name);\n            saga->aborted = true;\n            return rollback(saga);\n        }\n\n        publish_step_event(EVT_SAGA_STEP_DONE, saga, step->name, payload);\n    }\n\n    saga_event_t ok = {\n        .type      = EVT_SAGA_SUCCESS,\n        .saga_id   = saga->id,\n        .step_name = \"COMPLETED\"\n    };\n    eventbus_publish(&ok);\n    return 0;\n}\n```\n\n### 3.3. Example Steps – `src/steps_fx.c`, `src/steps_ledger.c`\n\n```c\n/* src/steps_fx.c */\n#include \"saga.h\"\n#include <stdio.h>\n\nint fx_convert(uint64_t id, void **payload)\n{\n    (void)payload; /* Not needed for demo */\n    printf(\"💱  FX_Conversion performed for saga %lu\\n\", id);\n    /* simulate success */\n    return 0;\n}\n\nint fx_reverse(uint64_t id, void *payload)\n{\n    (void)payload;\n    printf(\"↩  FX_Conversion reversed for saga %lu\\n\", id);\n    return 0;\n}\n```\n\n```c\n/* src/steps_ledger.c */\n#include \"saga.h\"\n#include <stdio.h>\n\nint ledger_post(uint64_t id, void **payload)\n{\n    (void)payload;\n    printf(\"📒  Ledger posting OK for saga %lu\\n\", id);\n    return 0;\n}\n\nint ledger_reverse(uint64_t id, void *payload)\n{\n    (void)payload;\n    printf(\"↩  Ledger posting reversed for saga %lu\\n\", id);\n    return 0;\n}\n```\n\n### 3.4. Demonstration Driver – `samples/payment_saga.c`\n\n```c\n#include \"saga.h\"\n#include <stdio.h>\n#include <stdlib.h>\n#include <time.h>\n\n/* ---------- Step Implementations --------------------------------------- */\nextern int fx_convert(uint64_t, void **);\nextern int fx_reverse(uint64_t, void *);\n\nextern int ledger_post(uint64_t, void **);\nextern int ledger_reverse(uint64_t, void *);\n\nstatic int fraud_score(uint64_t id, void **payload)\n{\n    (void)payload;\n    printf(\"🕵  Fraud scoring started for saga %lu\\n\", id);\n\n    /* Simulate 30% failure rate for classroom chaos monkeys */\n    if (rand() % 10 < 3) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"🚨  Fraud scoring FAILED (suspicious)\\n\");\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"✅  Fraud scoring passed\\n\");\n    return 0;\n}\n\nstatic int fraud_reverse(uint64_t id, void *payload)\n{\n    (void)payload;\n    printf(\"↩  Fraud scoring compensation (unflag) for saga %lu\\n\", id);\n    return 0;\n}\n\n/* ---------- Audit Trail Console Subscriber ----------------------------- */\nstatic void audit_logger(const saga_event_t *evt, void *ctx)\n{\n    (void)ctx;\n    const char *type_str[] = {\n        \"STEP_BEGIN\", \"STEP_DONE\", \"ABORT\", \"ROLLBACK_DONE\", \"SUCCESS\"\n    };\n    printf(\"📝  Audit: %s — saga=%lu step=%s\\n\",\n           type_str[evt->type], evt->saga_id, evt->step_name);\n}\n\nint main(void)\n{\n    srand((unsigned)time(NULL));\n    eventbus_subscribe(audit_logger, NULL);\n\n    saga_step_def_t steps[] = {\n        { \"FX_Conversion\",   fx_convert,   fx_reverse   },\n        { \"Ledger_Posting\",  ledger_post,  ledger_reverse },\n        { \"Fraud_Scoring\",   fraud_score,  fraud_reverse }\n    };\n\n    payment_saga_t saga;\n    saga_init(&saga, 42, steps, 3);\n\n    int rc = saga_execute(&saga);\n    printf(rc == 0 ? \"🎉  Saga completed successfully!\\n\"\n                   : \"💔  Saga rolled back.\\n\");\n    return rc;\n}\n```\n\nCompile & Run:\n\n```bash\ngcc -Iinclude src/saga.c src/steps_fx.c src/steps_ledger.c samples/payment_saga.c -lpthread -o bin/payment_saga\n./bin/payment_saga\n```\n\nExpected terminal output (with occasional induced failure):\n\n```\n💱  FX_Conversion performed for saga 42\n📒  Ledger posting OK for saga 42\n🕵  Fraud scoring started for saga 42\n🚨  Fraud scoring FAILED (suspicious)\n❌  Saga 42 failed at step Fraud_Scoring\n↩  Fraud scoring compensation (unflag) for saga 42\n↩  Ledger posting reversed for saga 42\n↩  FX_Conversion reversed for saga 42\n💔  Saga rolled back.\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. Teaching Notes\n\n* The orchestrator resides **inside** the Payment bounded context, not in an external workflow engine.  \n  Code can therefore be debugged with `gdb` by novices.\n* Because the event bus is *in-process*, instructors can swap it for a\n  real Kafka / NATS adapter during advanced labs without touching business logic.\n* Compensations are **idempotent**: every reverse handler can run twice safely,\n  a mandatory property in at-least-once delivery environments.\n\n---\n\n## 5. Production Hardening Checklist\n\n- [x] Persist `payment_saga_t` progress to EventStoreDB → replay after crash.  \n- [x] Encrypt payloads (`payload_encrypt()`) for PCI-DSS compliance.  \n- [x] Emit OpenTelemetry spans for distributed tracing.  \n- [ ] Add circuit-breakers around downstream services (coming Q3).  \n\n---\n\nHappy hacking!  \nEduPay Ledger Team ✨\n```","numLines":400,"startLine":1,"totalLines":400}}}

Because the platform is intended both for **production payment flows** and for\n     5→**pedagogical deep-dives**, every security control is written in C with pristine readability,\n     6→strong encapsulation, and complete unit-test coverage.\n     7→\n     8→This document serves three audiences:\n     9→\n    10→1. **C-level security engineers** hardening live campus deployments.  \n    11→2. **Professors & students** dissecting real-world fintech counter-measures.  \n    12→3. **Auditors (PCI-DSS, SOC 2, FERPA, PSD2)** reviewing our design artifacts.\n    13→\n    14→---\n    15→\n    16→## 1. Threat Model\n    17→\n    18→| Asset                          | Adversary Goal                          | Mitigation                                                                             |\n    19→| ------------------------------ | --------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n    20→| Cardholder data (CHD)          | Exfiltrate PANs, CVVs, expiry dates     | Tokenization, AES-256-GCM at rest, TLS 1.3 in flight, mlock/munlock secrets            |\n    21→| Tuition escrow accounts        | Unauthorized payout or diversion        | Dual-signature ledger, role-based ACL, multi-factor TOTP                               |\n    22→| Student PII (FERPA)            | Deanonymize academic records            | Record-level field encryption, Attribute-based access control (ABAC)                   |\n    23→| Audit logs                     | Tamper with or delete evidence          | Append-only Event Store, HMAC-SHA-256 chain-linking                                    |\n    24→| Sagas (distributed txns)       | Replay or reorder compensating actions  | Idempotent command IDs, monotonic vector clocks                                        |\n    25→\n    26→---\n    27→\n    28→## 2. Reference Implementation: PCI Tokenization Service\n    40→The code below is **production-ready** and compiles as a standalone library.  It demonstrates:\n    41→\n    42→* FIPS-compatible AES-256-GCM encryption\n    43→* High-entropy random token generation\n    44→* Memory-safe key handling with `mlock` + `OPENSSL_cleanse`\n    45→*
