libpci_tokenizer.so -fPIC -shared -lcrypto\n    58→ *\n    59→ * Author: EduPay Ledger Academy Security Team\n    60→ */\n    61→\n    62→#include <errno.h>\n    63→#include <inttypes.h>\n    64→#include <stdbool.h>\n    65→#include <stddef.h>\n    66→#include <stdint.h>\n    67→#include <stdio.h>\n    68→#include <stdlib.h>\n    69→#include <string.h>\n    70→#include <sys/mman.h>\n    71→#include <unistd.h>\n    72→\n    73→#include <openssl/evp.h>\n    74→#include <openssl/rand.h>\n    75→#include <openssl/err.h>\n    76→\n    77→/* ——————————————————— Public Constants ——————————————————— */\n    78→#define PCI_TOKENIZER_OK                 (0)\n    79→#define PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_CRYPTO        (-1)\n    80→#define PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_RNG           (-2)\n    81→#define PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_PARAM         (-3)\n    82→#define PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_INTERNAL      (-4)\n    83→\n    84→/* Always tokenise PAN down to 16 random hex chars */\n    85→#define TOKEN_LEN_HEX                   (16)\n    86→\n    87→/* AES-256-GCM parameters */\n    88→#define AES_GCM_KEY_BYTES               (32)\n    89→#define AES_GCM_IV_BYTES                (12)\n    90→#define AES_GCM_TAG_BYTES               (16)\n    91→\n    92→/* Compile-time checks */\n    93→#if TOKEN_LEN_HEX % 2 != 0\n    94→#   error \"TOKEN_LEN_HEX must be divisible by 2\"\n    95→#endif\n    96→\n    97→/* ——————————————————— Secure Utilities ——————————————————— */\n    98→\n    99→/**\n   100→ * Allocate memory that is locked into RAM, never swapped to disk.\n   101→ * Returns NULL on failure.\n   102→ */\n   103→static void *secure_calloc(size_t n, size_t size) {\n   104→    size_t total = n * size;\n   105→    void *ptr = calloc(1, total);\n   106→    if (!ptr) return NULL;\n   107→\n   108→    if (mlock(ptr, total) != 0) {\n   109→        /* Best-effort fallback: continue even if mlock fails on some OS */\n   110→        perror(\"mlock\");\n   111→    }\n   112→    return ptr;\n   113→}\n   114→\n   115→/**\n   116→ * Frees memory allocated by secure_calloc, wiping contents first.\n   117→ */\n   118→static void secure_free(void *ptr, size_t bytes) {\n   119→    if (!ptr) return;\n   120→    OPENSSL_cleanse(ptr, bytes);\n   121→    munlock(ptr, bytes);\n   122→    free(ptr);\n   123→}\n   124→\n   125→/* ——————————————————— Core API ——————————————————— */\n   126→\n   127→/**\n   128→ * Generates a cryptographically strong random hex string of TOKEN_LEN_HEX.\n   129→ */\n   130→static int generate_token(char token_out[TOKEN_LEN_HEX + 1]) {\n   131→    uint8_t raw[TOKEN_LEN_HEX / 2];\n   132→    if (RAND_bytes(raw, sizeof(raw)) != 1) {\n   133→        return PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_RNG;\n   134→    }\n   135→\n   136→    for (size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(raw); ++i)\n   137→        sprintf(&token_out[i * 2], \"%02x\", raw[i]);\n   138→\n   139→    token_out[TOKEN_LEN_HEX] = '\\0';\n   140→    return PCI_TOKENIZER_OK;\n   141→}\n   142→\n   143→/**\n   144→ * Encrypts plaintext using AES-256-GCM.\n   145→ *\n   146→ * Inputs:\n   147→ *   key:        32-byte key\n   148→ *   iv:         12-byte IV\n   149→ *   plaintext:  PAN string\n   150→ *   pt_len:     plaintext length in bytes\n   151→ *\n   152→ * Outputs:\n   153→ *   ciphertext: caller-allocated buffer at least pt_len bytes\n   154→ *   tag:        16-byte authentication tag\n   155→ *   ct_len:     actual number of ciphertext bytes written\n   156→ *\n   157→ * Returns PCI_TOKENIZER_* status code.\n   158→ */\n   159→static int aes256_gcm_encrypt(const uint8_t key[AES_GCM_KEY_BYTES],\n   160→                              const uint8_t iv[AES_GCM_IV_BYTES],\n   161→                              const uint8_t *plaintext,\n   162→                              size_t pt_len,\n   163→                              uint8_t *ciphertext,\n   164→                              uint8_t tag[AES_GCM_TAG_BYTES],\n   165→                              size_t *ct_len) {\n   166→\n   167→    EVP_CIPHER_CTX *ctx = EVP_CIPHER_CTX_new();\n   168→    if (!ctx) return PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_CRYPTO;\n   169→\n   170→    int len = 0;\n   171→    int ciphertext_len = 0;\n   172→    int ret_code = PCI_TOKENIZER_OK;\n   173→\n   174→    do {\n   175→        if (EVP_EncryptInit_ex(ctx, EVP_aes_256_gcm(), NULL, NULL, NULL) != 1) {\n   176→            ret_code = PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_CRYPTO; break;\n   177→        }\n   178→        if (EVP_CIPHER_CTX_ctrl(ctx, EVP_CTRL_GCM_SET_IVLEN, AES_GCM_IV_BYTES, NULL) != 1) {\n   179→            ret_code = PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_CRYPTO; break;\n   180→        }\n   181→        if (EVP_EncryptInit_ex(ctx, NULL, NULL, key, iv) != 1) {\n   182→            ret_code = PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_CRYPTO; break;\n   183→        }\n   184→        if (EVP_EncryptUpdate(ctx, ciphertext, &len, plaintext, (int)pt_len) != 1) {\n   185→            ret_code = PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_CRYPTO; break;\n   186→        }\n   187→        ciphertext_len = len;\n   188→        if (EVP_EncryptFinal_ex(ctx, ciphertext + len, &len) != 1) {\n   189→            ret_code = PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_CRYPTO; break;\n   190→        }\n   191→        ciphertext_len += len;\n   192→        if (EVP_CIPHER_CTX_ctrl(ctx, EVP_CTRL_GCM_GET_TAG, AES_GCM_TAG_BYTES, tag) != 1) {\n   193→            ret_code = PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_CRYPTO; break;\n   194→        }\n   195→        *ct_len = (size_t)ciphertext_len;\n   196→    } while (0);\n   197→\n   198→    EVP_CIPHER_CTX_free(ctx);\n   199→    if (ret_code != PCI_TOKENIZER_OK)\n   200→        ERR_print_errors_fp(stderr);\n   201→\n   202→    return ret_code;\n   203→}\n   204→\n   205→/* ——————————————————— Public Facade ——————————————————— */\n   206→\n   207→/**\n   208→ * Tokenises a Primary Account Number (PAN).\n   209→ *\n   210→ * The caller supplies:\n   211→ *   pan                 PAN string (digits only)\n   212→ *\n   213→ * The library returns:\n   214→ *   out_token           TOKEN_LEN_HEX-char hex string\n   215→ *   out_ciphertext      Encrypted PAN (malloc'd, must be free()'d by caller)\n   216→ *   out_ciphertext_len  Length of encrypted PAN\n   217→ *   out_iv              12-byte IV\n   218→ *   out_tag             16-byte GCM tag\n   219→ *\n   220→ * Note: the encryption key is managed by the HSM module; for demo purposes\n   221→ *       we generate a random key here and return it to the caller.\n   222→ */\n   223→int pci_tokenize_pan(const char  *pan,\n   224→                     char        out_token[TOKEN_LEN_HEX + 1],\n   225→                     uint8_t   **out_ciphertext,\n   226→                     size_t     *out_ciphertext_len,\n   227→                     uint8_t     out_iv[AES_GCM_IV_BYTES],\n   228→                     uint8_t     out_tag[AES_GCM_TAG_BYTES],\n   229→                     uint8_t     out_key[AES_GCM_KEY_BYTES]) {\n   230→\n   231→    if (!pan || !out_token || !out_ciphertext || !out_ciphertext_len ||\n   232→        !out_iv || !out_tag || !out_key) {\n   233→        return PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_PARAM;\n   234→    }\n   235→\n   236→    size_t pan_len = strlen(pan);\n   237→    if (pan_len == 0 || pan_len > 32) { /* 19 digits + possible separators */\n   238→        return PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_PARAM;\n   239→    }\n   240→\n   241→    /* Step 1: generate random token */\n   242→    int rc = generate_token(out_token);\n   243→    if (rc != PCI_TOKENIZER_OK) return rc;\n   244→\n   245→    /* Step 2: generate fresh AES GCM key & IV */\n   246→    if (RAND_bytes(out_key, AES_GCM_KEY_BYTES) != 1) {\n   247→        return PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_RNG;\n   248→    }\n   249→    if (RAND_bytes(out_iv, AES_GCM_IV_BYTES) != 1) {\n   250→        return PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_RNG;\n   251→    }\n   252→\n   253→    /* Step 3: secure memory allocation for ciphertext */\n   254→    *out_ciphertext = malloc(pan_len); /* ciphertext length equals plaintext */\n   255→    if (!*out_ciphertext) {\n   256→        return PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_INTERNAL;\n   257→    }\n   258→\n   259→    /* Step 4: encrypt PAN */\n   260→    rc = aes256_gcm_encrypt(out_key,\n   261→                            out_iv,\n   262→                            (const uint8_t *)pan,\n   263→                            pan_len,\n   264→                            *out_ciphertext,\n   265→                            out_tag,\n   266→                            out_ciphertext_len);\n   267→\n   268→    if (rc != PCI_TOKENIZER_OK) {\n   269→        OPENSSL_cleanse(*out_ciphertext, pan_len);\n   270→        free(*out_ciphertext);\n   271→        *out_ciphertext = NULL;\n   272→        *out_ciphertext_len = 0;\n   273→        return rc;\n   274→    }\n   275→\n   276→    return PCI_TOKENIZER_OK;\n   277→}\n   278→\n   279→/**\n   280→ * Wipes and frees the ciphertext buffer returned by pci_tokenize_pan().\n   281→ */\n   282→void pci_tokenizer_free_ciphertext(uint8_t *ciphertext, size_t len) {\n   283→    if (!ciphertext) return;\n   284→    OPENSSL_cleanse(ciphertext, len);\n   285→    free(ciphertext);\n   286→}\n   287→\n   288→/* ——————————————————— Example Usage ——————————————————— */\n   289→#ifdef PCI_TOKENIZER_DEMO_MAIN\n   290→int main(void) {\n   291→    const char *pan = \"4111111111111111\";\n   292→    char token[TOKEN_LEN_HEX + 1];\n   293→\n   294→    uint8_t *ciphertext = NULL;\n   295→    size_t   ciphertext_len = 0;\n   296→    uint8_t  iv[AES_GCM_IV_BYTES];\n   297→    uint8_t  tag[AES_GCM_TAG_BYTES];\n   298→    uint8_t  key[AES_GCM_KEY_BYTES];\n   299→\n   300→    int rc = pci_tokenize_pan(pan, token, &ciphertext, &ciphertext_len,\n   301→                              iv, tag, key);\n   302→    if (rc != PCI_TOKENIZER_OK) {\n   303→        fprintf(stderr, \"Tokenization failed (err=%d)\\n\", rc);\n   304→        return EXIT_FAILURE;\n   305→    }\n   306→\n   307→    printf(\"PAN           : %s\\n\", pan);\n   308→    printf(\"Token         : %s\\n\", token);\n   309→    printf(\"Ciphertext    : \");\n   310→    for (size_t i = 0; i < ciphertext_len; ++i)\n   311→        printf(\"%02x\", ciphertext[i]);\n   312→    printf(\"\\n\");\n   313→\n   314→    /* Proper key handling omitted for brevity — would be escrowed in HSM. */\n   315→    pci_tokenizer_free_ciphertext(ciphertext, ciphertext_len);\n   316→    OPENSSL_cleanse(key, sizeof(key));\n   317→    return EXIT_SUCCESS;\n   318→}\n   319→#endif\n   320→```\n   321→\n Additional Controls & Roadmap\n   338→\n   339→1. **Runtime ASLR Enforcement** – `seccomp` + clang CFI flags.  \n   340→2. **Key Rotation Service** – Zero-downtime re-encryption of vault rows.  \n   341→3. **FIDO2 WebAuthn** – Replacing TOTP for privileged bursar operations.  \n   342→4. **Continuous Compliance** – Open Policy Agent (OPA) integration for FERPA audits.  \n   343→\n   344→---\n   345→\n   346→## 5. Because the platform is intended both for **production payment flows** and for\n**pedagogical deep-dives**, every security control is written in C with pristine readability,\nstrong encapsulation, and complete unit-test coverage.\n\nThis document serves three audiences:\n\n1. **C-level security engineers** hardening live campus deployments.  \n2. **Professors & students** dissecting real-world fintech counter-measures.  \n3. **Auditors (PCI-DSS, SOC 2, FERPA, PSD2)** reviewing our design artifacts.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Threat Model\n\n| Asset                          | Adversary Goal                          | Mitigation                                                                             |\n| ------------------------------ | --------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| Cardholder data (CHD)          | Exfiltrate PANs, CVVs, expiry dates     | Tokenization, AES-256-GCM at rest, TLS 1.3 in flight, mlock/munlock secrets            |\n| Tuition escrow accounts        | Unauthorized payout or diversion        | Dual-signature ledger, role-based ACL, multi-factor TOTP                               |\n| Student PII (FERPA)            | Deanonymize academic records            | Record-level field encryption, Attribute-based access control (ABAC)                   |\n| Audit logs                     | Tamper with or delete evidence          | Append-only Event Store, HMAC-SHA-256 chain-linking                                    |\n| Sagas (distributed txns)       | Replay or reorder compensating actions  | Idempotent command IDs, monotonic vector clocks                                        |\n\n---\n\n## 2. Regulatory Compliance Matrix\n\n| Regulation | Relevant ELA Module(s)                       | Key Controls Implemented                                                                     |\n| ---------- | -------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| PCI-DSS 4  | `pci_tokenizer`, `tls_terminator`, `vault`   | PAN truncation, secure memory, quarterly ASV scans, FIPS 140-2 crypto                        |\n| PSD2       | `strong_customer_auth`, `risk_engine`        | SCA 2-factor routing, dynamic linking of transaction data                                    |\n| SOC 2 TSC  | `audit_trail`, `config_service`              | Immutable logs, least-privilege secrets rotation                                             |\n| FERPA      | `pii_encryptor`, `reporting_gateway`         | Field-level AES, consent enforcement, purpose-based de-identification                        |\n\n---\n\n## 3. Reference Implementation: PCI Tokenization Service\nThe code below is **production-ready** and compiles as a standalone library.  It demonstrates:\n\n* FIPS-compatible AES-256-GCM encryption\n* High-entropy random token generation\n* Memory-safe key handling with `mlock` + `OPENSSL_cleanse`\n* Audit-ready error codes\n\n> File location in repo: `src/security/pci_tokenizer.c`\n\n```c\n/**\n * @file pci_tokenizer.c\n *\n * Production-grade PAN tokenization compliant with PCI-DSS 4.\n *\n * Build:\n *   gcc -Wall -Wextra -Werror -pedantic -std=c11 \\\n *       pci_tokenizer.c -o libpci_tokenizer.so -fPIC -shared -lcrypto\n *\n * Author: EduPay Ledger Academy Security Team\n */\n\n#include <errno.h>\n#include <inttypes.h>\n#include <stdbool.h>\n#include <stddef.h>\n#include <stdint.h>\n#include <stdio.h>\n#include <stdlib.h>\n#include <string.h>\n#include <sys/mman.h>\n#include <unistd.h>\n\n#include <openssl/evp.h>\n#include <openssl/rand.h>\n#include <openssl/err.h>\n\n/* ——————————————————— Public Constants ——————————————————— */\n#define PCI_TOKENIZER_OK                 (0)\n#define PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_CRYPTO        (-1)\n#define PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_RNG           (-2)\n#define PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_PARAM         (-3)\n#define PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_INTERNAL      (-4)\n\n/* Always tokenise PAN down to 16 random hex chars */\n#define TOKEN_LEN_HEX                   (16)\n\n/* AES-256-GCM parameters */\n#define AES_GCM_KEY_BYTES               (32)\n#define AES_GCM_IV_BYTES                (12)\n#define AES_GCM_TAG_BYTES               (16)\n\n/* Compile-time checks */\n#if TOKEN_LEN_HEX % 2 != 0\n#   error \"TOKEN_LEN_HEX must be divisible by 2\"\n#endif\n\n/* ——————————————————— Secure Utilities ——————————————————— */\n\n/**\n * Allocate memory that is locked into RAM, never swapped to disk.\n * Returns NULL on failure.\n */\nstatic void *secure_calloc(size_t n, size_t size) {\n    size_t total = n * size;\n    void *ptr = calloc(1, total);\n    if (!ptr) return NULL;\n\n    if (mlock(ptr, total) != 0) {\n        /* Best-effort fallback: continue even if mlock fails on some OS */\n        perror(\"mlock\");\n    }\n    return ptr;\n}\n\n/**\n * Frees memory allocated by secure_calloc, wiping contents first.\n */\nstatic void secure_free(void *ptr, size_t bytes) {\n    if (!ptr) return;\n    OPENSSL_cleanse(ptr, bytes);\n    munlock(ptr, bytes);\n    free(ptr);\n}\n\n/* ——————————————————— Core API ——————————————————— */\n\n/**\n * Generates a cryptographically strong random hex string of TOKEN_LEN_HEX.\n */\nstatic int generate_token(char token_out[TOKEN_LEN_HEX + 1]) {\n    uint8_t raw[TOKEN_LEN_HEX / 2];\n    if (RAND_bytes(raw, sizeof(raw)) != 1) {\n        return PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_RNG;\n    }\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(raw); ++i)\n        sprintf(&token_out[i * 2], \"%02x\", raw[i]);\n\n    token_out[TOKEN_LEN_HEX] = '\\0';\n    return PCI_TOKENIZER_OK;\n}\n\n/**\n * Encrypts plaintext using AES-256-GCM.\n *\n * Inputs:\n *   key:        32-byte key\n *   iv:         12-byte IV\n *   plaintext:  PAN string\n *   pt_len:     plaintext length in bytes\n *\n * Outputs:\n *   ciphertext: caller-allocated buffer at least pt_len bytes\n *   tag:        16-byte authentication tag\n *   ct_len:     actual number of ciphertext bytes written\n *\n * Returns PCI_TOKENIZER_* status code.\n */\nstatic int aes256_gcm_encrypt(const uint8_t key[AES_GCM_KEY_BYTES],\n                              const uint8_t iv[AES_GCM_IV_BYTES],\n                              const uint8_t *plaintext,\n                              size_t pt_len,\n                              uint8_t *ciphertext,\n                              uint8_t tag[AES_GCM_TAG_BYTES],\n                              size_t *ct_len) {\n\n    EVP_CIPHER_CTX *ctx = EVP_CIPHER_CTX_new();\n    if (!ctx) return PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_CRYPTO;\n\n    int len = 0;\n    int ciphertext_len = 0;\n    int ret_code = PCI_TOKENIZER_OK;\n\n    do {\n        if (EVP_EncryptInit_ex(ctx, EVP_aes_256_gcm(), NULL, NULL, NULL) != 1) {\n            ret_code = PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_CRYPTO; break;\n        }\n        if (EVP_CIPHER_CTX_ctrl(ctx, EVP_CTRL_GCM_SET_IVLEN, AES_GCM_IV_BYTES, NULL) != 1) {\n            ret_code = PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_CRYPTO; break;\n        }\n        if (EVP_EncryptInit_ex(ctx, NULL, NULL, key, iv) != 1) {\n            ret_code = PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_CRYPTO; break;\n        }\n        if (EVP_EncryptUpdate(ctx, ciphertext, &len, plaintext, (int)pt_len) != 1) {\n            ret_code = PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_CRYPTO; break;\n        }\n        ciphertext_len = len;\n        if (EVP_EncryptFinal_ex(ctx, ciphertext + len, &len) != 1) {\n            ret_code = PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_CRYPTO; break;\n        }\n        ciphertext_len += len;\n        if (EVP_CIPHER_CTX_ctrl(ctx, EVP_CTRL_GCM_GET_TAG, AES_GCM_TAG_BYTES, tag) != 1) {\n            ret_code = PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_CRYPTO; break;\n        }\n        *ct_len = (size_t)ciphertext_len;\n    } while (0);\n\n    EVP_CIPHER_CTX_free(ctx);\n    if (ret_code != PCI_TOKENIZER_OK)\n        ERR_print_errors_fp(stderr);\n\n    return ret_code;\n}\n\n/* ——————————————————— Public Facade ——————————————————— */\n\n/**\n * Tokenises a Primary Account Number (PAN).\n *\n * The caller supplies:\n *   pan                 PAN string (digits only)\n *\n * The library returns:\n *   out_token           TOKEN_LEN_HEX-char hex string\n *   out_ciphertext      Encrypted PAN (malloc'd, must be free()'d by caller)\n *   out_ciphertext_len  Length of encrypted PAN\n *   out_iv              12-byte IV\n *   out_tag             16-byte GCM tag\n *\n * Note: the encryption key is managed by the HSM module; for demo purposes\n *       we generate a random key here and return it to the caller.\n */\nint pci_tokenize_pan(const char  *pan,\n                     char        out_token[TOKEN_LEN_HEX + 1],\n                     uint8_t   **out_ciphertext,\n                     size_t     *out_ciphertext_len,\n                     uint8_t     out_iv[AES_GCM_IV_BYTES],\n                     uint8_t     out_tag[AES_GCM_TAG_BYTES],\n                     uint8_t     out_key[AES_GCM_KEY_BYTES]) {\n\n    if (!pan || !out_token || !out_ciphertext || !out_ciphertext_len ||\n        !out_iv || !out_tag || !out_key) {\n        return PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_PARAM;\n    }\n\n    size_t pan_len = strlen(pan);\n    if (pan_len == 0 || pan_len > 32) { /* 19 digits + possible separators */\n        return PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_PARAM;\n    }\n\n    /* Step 1: generate random token */\n    int rc = generate_token(out_token);\n    if (rc != PCI_TOKENIZER_OK) return rc;\n\n    /* Step 2: generate fresh AES GCM key & IV */\n    if (RAND_bytes(out_key, AES_GCM_KEY_BYTES) != 1) {\n        return PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_RNG;\n    }\n    if (RAND_bytes(out_iv, AES_GCM_IV_BYTES) != 1) {\n        return PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_RNG;\n    }\n\n    /* Step 3: secure memory allocation for ciphertext */\n    *out_ciphertext = malloc(pan_len); /* ciphertext length equals plaintext */\n    if (!*out_ciphertext) {\n        return PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_INTERNAL;\n    }\n\n    /* Step 4: encrypt PAN */\n    rc = aes256_gcm_encrypt(out_key,\n                            out_iv,\n                            (const uint8_t *)pan,\n                            pan_len,\n                            *out_ciphertext,\n                            out_tag,\n                            out_ciphertext_len);\n\n    if (rc != PCI_TOKENIZER_OK) {\n        OPENSSL_cleanse(*out_ciphertext, pan_len);\n        free(*out_ciphertext);\n        *out_ciphertext = NULL;\n        *out_ciphertext_len = 0;\n        return rc;\n    }\n\n    return PCI_TOKENIZER_OK;\n}\n\n/**\n * Wipes and frees the ciphertext buffer returned by pci_tokenize_pan().\n */\nvoid pci_tokenizer_free_ciphertext(uint8_t *ciphertext, size_t len) {\n    if (!ciphertext) return;\n    OPENSSL_cleanse(ciphertext, len);\n    free(ciphertext);\n}\n\n/* ——————————————————— Example Usage ——————————————————— */\n#ifdef PCI_TOKENIZER_DEMO_MAIN\nint main(void) {\n    const char *pan = \"4111111111111111\";\n    char token[TOKEN_LEN_HEX + 1];\n\n    uint8_t *ciphertext = NULL;\n    size_t   ciphertext_len = 0;\n    uint8_t  iv[AES_GCM_IV_BYTES];\n    uint8_t  tag[AES_GCM_TAG_BYTES];\n    uint8_t  key[AES_GCM_KEY_BYTES];\n\n    int rc = pci_tokenize_pan(pan, token, &ciphertext, &ciphertext_len,\n                              iv, tag, key);\n    if (rc != PCI_TOKENIZER_OK) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"Tokenization failed (err=%d)\\n\", rc);\n        return EXIT_FAILURE;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"PAN           : %s\\n\", pan);\n    printf(\"Token         : %s\\n\", token);\n    printf(\"Ciphertext    : \");\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < ciphertext_len; ++i)\n        printf(\"%02x\", ciphertext[i]);\n    printf(\"\\n\");\n\n    /* Proper key handling omitted for brevity — would be escrowed in HSM. */\n    pci_tokenizer_free_ciphertext(ciphertext, ciphertext_len);\n    OPENSSL_cleanse(key, sizeof(key));\n    return EXIT_SUCCESS;\n}\n#endif\n```\n\n### Unit Test Snippet\n\n```c\n#include \"pci_tokenizer.h\"\n#include <assert.h>\n\nvoid test_token_length(void) {\n    char token[TOKEN_LEN_HEX + 1];\n    /* ... invoke tokenizer ... */\n    assert(strlen(token) == TOKEN_LEN_HEX);\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. Additional Controls & Roadmap\n\n1. **Runtime ASLR Enforcement** – `seccomp` + clang CFI flags.  \n2. **Key Rotation Service** – Zero-downtime re-encryption of vault rows.  \n3. **FIDO2 WebAuthn** – Replacing TOTP for privileged bursar operations.  \n4. **Continuous Compliance** – Open Policy Agent (OPA) integration for FERPA audits.  \n\n---\n\n## 5. Appendix A — Compile & Run\n\n```bash\n# Build shared library\ngcc -Wall -Wextra -Werror -pedantic -std=c11 \\\n    -DPCI_TOKENIZER_DEMO_MAIN \\\n    pci_tokenizer.c -o pci_tokenizer_demo -lcrypto\n\n./pci_tokenizer_demo\n```\n\nThe tokenizer library is CI-vetted with **Clang Static Analyzer**, **Coverity**, and\n**ASan** instrumentation to guarantee memory safety and side-channel resistance.\n```","numLines":359,"startLine":1,"totalLines":359}}}

All functions are\n    28→  synchronous and fail-fast; wrap them in your platform’s preferred error\n    29→  handling and tracing facilities when deploying in production.\n    30→\n    31→  Build note: link with -lcrypto (OpenSSL 1.1.1+) or LibreSSL 3.x.\n    32→──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────*/\n    33→#ifndef EDU_COMPLIANCE_GUARD_H\n    34→#define EDU_COMPLIANCE_GUARD_H\n    35→\n    36→#include <stddef.h>   /* size_t   */\n    37→#include <stdint.h>   /* uint8_t  */\n    38→#include <stdbool.h>  /* bool     */\n    39→\n    40→#ifdef __cplusplus\n    41→extern \"C\" {\n    42→#endif\n    43→\n    44→/* Opaque symmetric-key handle ‑ see cg_key_acquire() */\n    45→typedef struct cg_key cg_key_t;\n    46→\n    47→/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    48→/*  PCI-DSS Safe Tokenisation                                                */\n    49→/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    50→\n    51→/**\n    52→ * Generates a numeric, Luhn-valid token that preserves the first six\n    53→ * digits (BIN/IIN) of the supplied Primary Account Number.\n    54→ *\n    55→ * The function is idempotent; identical input produces identical output\n    56→ * when `EDUPAY_FPE=0`.  Set `EDUPAY_FPE=1` to switch to format-preserving\n    57→ * encryption (left as an exercise).\n    58→ *\n    59→ * Memory safety: the caller owns both the input PAN and output buffer.\n    60→ * Call cg_secure_bzero() after use.\n    61→ *\n    62→ * @return 0 on success, ‑EINVAL on bad input, negative errno on failure.\n    63→ */\n    64→int cg_tokenise_pan(const char *pan,\n    65→                    char       *out_token,\n    66→                    size_t      out_len);\n    67→\n    68→/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    69→/*  Authenticated Encryption (AES-256-GCM)                                   */\n    70→/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    71→\n    72→/**\n    73→ * Encrypts |plaintext| and prepends a random IV, appending a 128-bit tag.\n    74→ *\n    75→ * |out_cipher| must be at least 12 B + pt_len + 16 B.\n    76→ * Returns total bytes written or negative errno.\n    77→ */\n    78→ssize_t cg_seal(cg_key_t      *key,\n    79→                const uint8_t *plaintext,\n    80→                size_t         pt_len,\n    81→                uint8_t       *out_cipher,\n    82→                size_t         out_cipher_sz);\n    83→\n    84→/**\n    85→ * Decrypts and authenticates a buffer produced by cg_seal().\n    86→ *\n    87→ * Returns number of plaintext bytes or ‑EBADMSG when auth fails.\n    88→ */\n    89→ssize_t cg_open(cg_key_t      *key,\n    90→                const uint8_t *cipher,\n    91→                size_t         cipher_len,\n    92→                uint8_t       *out_plain,\n    93→                size_t         out_plain_sz);\n    94→\n    95→/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    96→/*  Secure memory hygiene                                                    */\n    97→/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    98→\n    99→/* Overwrite |len| bytes starting at |ptr| even under aggressive optimisation */\n   100→void cg_secure_bzero(void *ptr, size_t len);\n   101→\n   102→/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n   103→/*  Key management (teaching mode)                                           */\n   104→/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n   105→\n   106→/**\n   107→ * Acquires an AES-256-GCM key.\n   108→ *   • Production:  pulled from HSM via PKCS#11.\n   109→ *   • Lab mode:    derived from EDUPAY_COMPLIANCE_KEY (64-hex chars) or\n   110→ *                  filled with /dev/urandom.\n   111→ */\n   112→cg_key_t *cg_key_acquire(void);\n   113→\n   114→/* Zeroise and free key material */\n   115→void cg_key_release(cg_key_t *key);\n   116→\n   117→#ifdef __cplusplus\n   118→}\n   119→#endif\n   120→#endif /* EDU_COMPLIANCE_GUARD_H */\n   121→```\n   122→\n   123→---\n   124→\n   125→## 2 ╱ 4 Reference implementation (`compliance_guard.c`)\n   126→\n   127→```c\n   128→/*──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n   129→  compliance_guard.c  —  Internal library for the Compliance micro-service\n   130→──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────*/\n   131→#include \"compliance_guard.h\"\n   132→\n   133→#include <openssl/evp.h>\n   134→#include <openssl/rand.h>\n   135→#include <errno.h>\n   136→#include <string.h>\n   137→#include <stdlib.h>\n   138→#include <stdio.h>\n   139→\n   140→#define CG_AES_GCM_IV_LEN   12u\n   141→#define CG_AES_GCM_TAG_LEN  16u\n   142→#define CG_PAN_MAX_LEN      19u     /* ISO/IEC 7812-1 */\n   143→#define CG_TOKEN_LEN        19u\n   144→\n   145→struct cg_key { uint8_t key[32]; };\n   146→\n   147→/* ───────────────────────────── Internal helpers ─────────────────────────── */\n   148→\n   149→static int cg_generate_iv(uint8_t *iv, size_t iv_len)\n   150→{\n   151→    return RAND_bytes(iv, (int)iv_len) == 1 ? 0 : -EIO;\n   152→}\n   153→\n   154→static int cg_validate_luhn(const char *digits)\n   155→{\n   156→    size_t len = strlen(digits);\n   157→    int sum = 0, alt = 0;\n   158→\n   159→    for (ssize_t i = (ssize_t)len - 1; i >= 0; --i)\n   160→    {\n   161→        int d = digits[i] - '0';\n   162→        if (d < 0 || d > 9) return -EINVAL;\n   163→        if (alt) { d *= 2; if (d > 9) d -= 9; }\n   164→        sum += d; alt = !alt;\n   165→    }\n   166→    return sum % 10 == 0 ? 0 : -EINVAL;\n   167→}\n   168→\n   169→static uint8_t cg_rand_digit(void)\n   170→{\n   171→    uint8_t b; RAND_bytes(&b, 1); return (uint8_t)(b % 10);\n   172→}\n   173→\n   174→static int cg_build_token(const char *pan, char *tok)\n   175→{\n   176→    size_t len = strlen(pan);\n   177→    if (len < 8 || len > CG_PAN_MAX_LEN) return -EINVAL;\n   178→\n   179→    memcpy(tok, pan, 6);                 /* Keep BIN for analytics */\n   180→    for (size_t i = 6; i < len - 1; ++i) /* Randomise body         */\n   181→        tok[i] = '0' + cg_rand_digit();\n   182→\n   183→    /* Recalculate Luhn check digit */\n   184→    int sum = 0, alt = 1;\n   185→    for (ssize_t i = (ssize_t)len - 2; i >= 0; --i)\n   186→    {\n   187→        int d = tok[i] - '0';\n   188→        if (alt) { d *= 2; if (d > 9) d -= 9; }\n   189→        sum += d; alt = !alt;\n   190→    }\n   191→    tok[len - 1] = '0' + ((10 - (sum % 10)) % 10);\n   192→    tok[len] = '\\0';\n   193→    return 0;\n   194→}\n   195→\n   196→/* ───────────────────────────── Public API ───────────────────────────────── */\n   197→\n   198→int cg_tokenise_pan(const char *pan, char *out_token, size_t out_len)\n   199→{\n   200→    if (!pan || !out_token)                 return -EINVAL;\n   201→    const size_t n = strlen(pan);\n   202→    if (n == 0 || n > CG_PAN_MAX_LEN)       return -EINVAL;\n   203→    if (out_len < n + 1)                    return -ENOSPC;\n   204→    if (cg_validate_luhn(pan) != 0)         return -EINVAL;\n   205→\n   206→    /* Switchable algorithm for classroom demos */\n   207→    if (getenv(\"EDUPAY_FPE\") && *getenv(\"EDUPAY_FPE\") == '1')\n   208→    {\n   209→        /* Placeholder for format-preserving AES-FF3-1 */\n   210→        return cg_build_token(pan, out_token); /* Fallback path */\n   211→    }\n   212→    return cg_build_token(pan, out_token);\n   213→}\n   214→\n   215→ssize_t cg_seal(cg_key_t      *key,\n   216→                const uint8_t *pt,\n   217→                size_t         pt_len,\n   218→                uint8_t       *out,\n   219→                size_t         out_sz)\n   220→{\n   221→    if (!key || !pt || !out || pt_len == 0)  return -EINVAL;\n   222→    const size_t need = CG_AES_GCM_IV_LEN + pt_len + CG_AES_GCM_TAG_LEN;\n   223→    if (out_sz < need)                       return -ENOSPC;\n   224→\n   225→    uint8_t *iv     = out;\n   226→    uint8_t *cipher = out + CG_AES_GCM_IV_LEN;\n   227→    uint8_t *tag    = cipher + pt_len;\n   228→\n   229→    if (cg_generate_iv(iv, CG_AES_GCM_IV_LEN) != 0) return -EIO;\n   230→\n   231→    ssize_t ret = -EIO;\n   232→    EVP_CIPHER_CTX *ctx = EVP_CIPHER_CTX_new();\n   233→    if (!ctx) return -ENOMEM;\n   234→\n   235→    do {\n   236→        if (EVP_EncryptInit_ex(ctx, EVP_aes_256_gcm(), NULL, NULL, NULL) != 1) break;\n   237→        if (EVP_CIPHER_CTX_ctrl(ctx, EVP_CTRL_GCM_SET_IVLEN,\n   238→                                CG_AES_GCM_IV_LEN, NULL) != 1)               break;\n   239→        if (EVP_EncryptInit_ex(ctx, NULL, NULL, key->key, iv) != 1)           break;\n   240→\n   241→        int len = 0, total = 0;\n   242→        if (EVP_EncryptUpdate(ctx, cipher, &len, pt, (int)pt_len) != 1)       break;\n   243→        total += len;\n   244→        if (EVP_EncryptFinal_ex(ctx, cipher + total, &len) != 1)              break;\n   245→        total += len;\n   246→        if (EVP_CIPHER_CTX_ctrl(ctx, EVP_CTRL_GCM_GET_TAG,\n   247→                                CG_AES_GCM_TAG_LEN, tag) != 1)               break;\n   248→\n   249→        ret = CG_AES_GCM_IV_LEN + total + CG_AES_GCM_TAG_LEN;\n   250→    } while (0);\n   251→\n   252→    EVP_CIPHER_CTX_free(ctx);\n   253→    return ret;\n   254→}\n   255→\n   256→ssize_t cg_open(cg_key_t      *key,\n   257→                const uint8_t *in,\n   258→                size_t         in_len,\n   259→                uint8_t       *out,\n   260→                size_t         out_sz)\n   261→{\n   262→    if (!key || !in || !out)                         return -EINVAL;\n   263→    if (in_len < CG_AES_GCM_IV_LEN + CG_AES_GCM_TAG_LEN) return -EINVAL;\n   264→\n   265→    const uint8_t *iv     = in;\n   266→    const uint8_t *cipher = in + CG_AES_GCM_IV_LEN;\n   267→    const size_t   ct_len = in_len - CG_AES_GCM_IV_LEN - CG_AES_GCM_TAG_LEN;\n   268→    const uint8_t *tag    = in + in_len - CG_AES_GCM_TAG_LEN;\n   269→\n   270→    if (out_sz < ct_len)                             return -ENOSPC;\n   271→\n   272→    ssize_t ret = -EIO;\n   273→    EVP_CIPHER_CTX *ctx = EVP_CIPHER_CTX_new();\n   274→    if (!ctx) return -ENOMEM;\n   275→\n   276→    do {\n   277→        if (EVP_DecryptInit_ex(ctx, EVP_aes_256_gcm(), NULL, NULL, NULL) != 1) break;\n   278→        if (EVP_CIPHER_CTX_ctrl(ctx, EVP_CTRL_GCM_SET_IVLEN,\n   279→                                CG_AES_GCM_IV_LEN, NULL) != 1)               break;\n   280→        if (EVP_DecryptInit_ex(ctx, NULL, NULL, key->key, iv) != 1)           break;\n   281→\n   282→        int len = 0, total = 0;\n   283→        if (EVP_DecryptUpdate(ctx, out, &len, cipher, (int)ct_len) != 1)      break;\n   284→        total += len;\n   285→\n   286→        if (EVP_CIPHER_CTX_ctrl(ctx, EVP_CTRL_GCM_SET_TAG,\n   287→                                CG_AES_GCM_TAG_LEN, (void *)tag) != 1)       break;\n   288→        if (EVP_DecryptFinal_ex(ctx, out + total, &len) != 1) {\n   289→            ret = -EBADMSG;                                                  break;\n   290→        }\n   291→        total += len;\n   292→        ret = total;\n   293→    } while (0);\n   294→\n   295→    EVP_CIPHER_CTX_free(ctx);\n   296→    return ret;\n   297→}\n   298→\n   299→void cg_secure_bzero(void *ptr, size_t len)\n   300→{\n   301→    if (!ptr || !len) return;\n   302→#if defined(__STDC_LIB_EXT1__)\n   303→    memset_s(ptr, len, 0, len);\n   304→#else\n   305→    volatile uint8_t *p = (volatile uint8_t *)ptr;\n   306→    while (len--) *p++ = 0;\n   307→#endif\n   308→}\n   309→\n   310→cg_key_t *cg_key_acquire(void)\n   311→{\n   312→    cg_key_t *k = calloc(1, sizeof *k);\n   313→    if (!k) return NULL;\n   314→\n   315→    const char *env = getenv(\"EDUPAY_COMPLIANCE_KEY\");\n   316→    if (env && strlen(env) == 64)\n   317→    {   /* Hex-decode deterministic, instructor-supplied key */\n   318→        for (size_t i = 0; i < 32; ++i)\n   319→            sscanf(&env[i * 2], \"%2hhx\", &k->key[i]);\n   320→    }\n   321→    else if (RAND_bytes(k->key, sizeof k->key) != 1)\n   322→    {\n   323→        free(k); k = NULL;\n   324→    }\n   325→    return k;\n   326→}\n   327→\n   328→void cg_key_release(cg_key_t *key)\n   329→{\n   330→    if (!key) return;\n   331→    cg_secure_bzero(key->key, sizeof key->key);\n   332→    free(key);\n   333→}\n   334→```\n   335→\n   336→---\n   337→\n   338→## 3 ╱ 4 End-to-end console demo (`example_usage.c`)\n   339→\n   340→```c\n   341→/* Compile with\n   342→ *   cc -std=c11 -Wall -Wextra example_usage.c compliance_guard.c -lcrypto -o demo\n   343→ * Run with\n   344→ *   EDUPAY_COMPLIANCE_KEY=$(openssl rand -hex 32) ./demo\n   345→ */\n   346→#include <stdio.h>\n   347→#include <string.h>\n   348→\n   349→#include \"compliance_guard.h\"\n   350→\n   351→int main(void)\n   352→{\n   353→    const char *pan = \"4485275742308327\"; /* Visa test card */\n   354→\n   355→    char token[CG_TOKEN_LEN + 1];\n   356→    if (cg_tokenise_pan(pan, token, sizeof token) != 0) {\n   357→        fprintf(stderr, \"Tokenisation failed\\n\");\n   358→        return 1;\n   359→    }\n   360→    printf(\"Tokenised PAN  : %s\\n\", token);\n   361→\n   362→    cg_key_t *key = cg_key_acquire();\n   363→    if (!key) { perror(\"key_acquire\"); return 1; }\n   364→\n   365→    uint8_t cipher[256];\n   366→    ssize_t n_cipher = cg_seal(key,\n   367→                               (const uint8_t *)token, strlen(token),\n   368→                               cipher, sizeof cipher);\n   369→    cg_secure_bzero(token, sizeof token); /* Immediately destroy clear-text */\n   370→    if (n_cipher < 0) {\n   371→        fprintf(stderr, \"Encryption failed: %zd\\n\", n_cipher);\n   372→        cg_key_release(key);\n   373→        return 1;\n   374→    }\n   375→\n   376→    uint8_t clear[64];\n   377→    ssize_t n_plain = cg_open(key, cipher, (size_t)n_cipher,\n   378→                              clear, sizeof clear);\n   379→    cg_key_release(key);\n   380→\n   381→    if (n_plain < 0) {\n   382→        fprintf(stderr, \"Decryption failed: %zd\\n\", n_plain);\n   383→        return 1;\n   384→    }\n   385→    printf(\"Decrypted token: %.*s\\n\", (int)n_plain, clear);\n   386→    cg_secure_bzero(clear, (size_t)n_plain);\n   387→\n   388→    return 0;\n   389→}\n   390→```\n   391→\n   392→---\n   393→\n   394→## 4 ╱ 4 Lab exercises\n   395→\n   396→1. Extend `cg_tokenise_pan()` to call an FF3-1 library when `EDUPAY_FPE=1`.  \n   397→   Verify that the output still passes Luhn and preserves BIN/IIN.\n   398→\n   399→2. Attach `cg_seal()` to the CQRS → Audit-Trail write model.  \n   400→   Use Wireshark to confirm that events on the message bus no longer contain\n   401→   clear-text PAN or student identifiers (FERPA §99.31).\n   402→\n   403→3. Fire up `valgrind --tool=memcheck ./demo` and inspect the heap after\n   404→   `cg_secure_bzero()` executes. No clear-text secrets should appear.\n   405→\n   406→---\n   407→\n   408→© 2024 EduPay Ledger Academy — MIT License\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. You can still analyze existing code, write reports, or answer questions about the code behavior.\n</system-reminder>\n"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"1fdb6557-e5b7-4c66-b3fd-53027102a2dd","uuid":"5859d6c0-5da6-46f8-b61e-20ba0b670471","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/app/project/docs/lessons/L03_Regulatory_Compliance_in_Code.md","content":"# Lesson 03 – Regulatory Compliance in Code\nEduPay Ledger Academy ─ Bounded Context: Compliance  \nStatus: Approved for classroom distribution ✅\n\n> “Security and privacy requirements **are** business requirements.”  \n> — Robert C. Martin, Clean Coder (paraphrased)\n\n---\n\n## 🧭 Learning objectives\n* Design code paths that meet FERPA, PCI-DSS v4.0 and PSD2 RTS.\n* Implement format-preserving tokenisation that keeps BIN analytics intact.\n* Encrypt audit-trail entries with AES-256-GCM while preserving CQRS read-models.\n* Shred secrets from RAM so students can pass a post-mortem memory scan.\n\n---\n\n## 1 ╱ 4 Header-only API surface (`compliance_guard.h`)\n\n```c\n/*──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  compliance_guard.h\n  ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  © 2024 EduPay Ledger Academy\n\n  This header exposes the minimal interface required to integrate regulatory\n  safeguards into any Clean-Architecture delivery layer.  All functions are\n  synchronous and fail-fast; wrap them in your platform’s preferred error\n  handling and tracing facilities when deploying in production.\n\n  Build note: link with -lcrypto (OpenSSL 1.1.1+) or LibreSSL 3.x.\n──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────*/\n#ifndef EDU_COMPLIANCE_GUARD_H\n#define EDU_COMPLIANCE_GUARD_H\n\n#include <stddef.h>   /* size_t   */\n#include <stdint.h>   /* uint8_t  */\n#include <stdbool.h>  /* bool     */\n\n#ifdef __cplusplus\nextern \"C\" {\n#endif\n\n/* Opaque symmetric-key handle ‑ see cg_key_acquire() */\ntypedef struct cg_key cg_key_t;\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*  PCI-DSS Safe Tokenisation                                                */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n/**\n * Generates a numeric, Luhn-valid token that preserves the first six\n * digits (BIN/IIN) of the supplied Primary Account Number.\n *\n * The function is idempotent; identical input produces identical output\n * when `EDUPAY_FPE=0`.  Set `EDUPAY_FPE=1` to switch to format-preserving\n * encryption (left as an exercise).\n *\n * Memory safety: the caller owns both the input PAN and output buffer.\n * Call cg_secure_bzero() after use.\n *\n * @return 0 on success, ‑EINVAL on bad input, negative errno on failure.\n */\nint cg_tokenise_pan(const char *pan,\n                    char       *out_token,\n                    size_t      out_len);\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*  Authenticated Encryption (AES-256-GCM)                                   */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n/**\n * Encrypts |plaintext| and prepends a random IV, appending a 128-bit tag.\n *\n * |out_cipher| must be at least 12 B + pt_len + 16 B.\n * Returns total bytes written or negative errno.\n */\nssize_t cg_seal(cg_key_t      *key,\n                const uint8_t *plaintext,\n                size_t         pt_len,\n                uint8_t       *out_cipher,\n                size_t         out_cipher_sz);\n\n/**\n * Decrypts and authenticates a buffer produced by cg_seal().\n *\n * Returns number of plaintext bytes or ‑EBADMSG when auth fails.\n */\nssize_t cg_open(cg_key_t      *key,\n                const uint8_t *cipher,\n                size_t         cipher_len,\n                uint8_t       *out_plain,\n                size_t         out_plain_sz);\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*  Secure memory hygiene                                                    */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n/* Overwrite |len| bytes starting at |ptr| even under aggressive optimisation */\nvoid cg_secure_bzero(void *ptr, size_t len);\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*  Key management (teaching mode)                                           */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n/**\n * Acquires an AES-256-GCM key.\n *   • Production:  pulled from HSM via PKCS#11.\n *   • Lab mode:    derived from EDUPAY_COMPLIANCE_KEY (64-hex chars) or\n *                  filled with /dev/urandom.\n */\ncg_key_t *cg_key_acquire(void);\n\n/* Zeroise and free key material */\nvoid cg_key_release(cg_key_t *key);\n\n#ifdef __cplusplus\n}\n#endif\n#endif /* EDU_COMPLIANCE_GUARD_H */\n```\n\n---\n\n## 2 ╱ 4 Reference implementation (`compliance_guard.c`)\n\n```c\n/*──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  compliance_guard.c  —  Internal library for the Compliance micro-service\n──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────*/\n#include \"compliance_guard.h\"\n\n#include <openssl/evp.h>\n#include <openssl/rand.h>\n#include <errno.h>\n#include <string.h>\n#include <stdlib.h>\n#include <stdio.h>\n\n#define CG_AES_GCM_IV_LEN   12u\n#define CG_AES_GCM_TAG_LEN  16u\n#define CG_PAN_MAX_LEN      19u     /* ISO/IEC 7812-1 */\n#define CG_TOKEN_LEN        19u\n\nstruct cg_key { uint8_t key[32]; };\n\n/* ───────────────────────────── Internal helpers ─────────────────────────── */\n\nstatic int cg_generate_iv(uint8_t *iv, size_t iv_len)\n{\n    return RAND_bytes(iv, (int)iv_len) == 1 ? 0 : -EIO;\n}\n\nstatic int cg_validate_luhn(const char *digits)\n{\n    size_t len = strlen(digits);\n    int sum = 0, alt = 0;\n\n    for (ssize_t i = (ssize_t)len - 1; i >= 0; --i)\n    {\n        int d = digits[i] - '0';\n        if (d < 0 || d > 9) return -EINVAL;\n        if (alt) { d *= 2; if (d > 9) d -= 9; }\n        sum += d; alt = !alt;\n    }\n    return sum % 10 == 0 ? 0 : -EINVAL;\n}\n\nstatic uint8_t cg_rand_digit(void)\n{\n    uint8_t b; RAND_bytes(&b, 1); return (uint8_t)(b % 10);\n}\n\nstatic int cg_build_token(const char *pan, char *tok)\n{\n    size_t len = strlen(pan);\n    if (len < 8 || len > CG_PAN_MAX_LEN) return -EINVAL;\n\n    memcpy(tok, pan, 6);                 /* Keep BIN for analytics */\n    for (size_t i = 6; i < len - 1; ++i) /* Randomise body         */\n        tok[i] = '0' + cg_rand_digit();\n\n    /* Recalculate Luhn check digit */\n    int sum = 0, alt = 1;\n    for (ssize_t i = (ssize_t)len - 2; i >= 0; --i)\n    {\n        int d = tok[i] - '0';\n        if (alt) { d *= 2; if (d > 9) d -= 9; }\n        sum += d; alt = !alt;\n    }\n    tok[len - 1] = '0' + ((10 - (sum % 10)) % 10);\n    tok[len] = '\\0';\n    return 0;\n}\n\n/* ───────────────────────────── Public API ───────────────────────────────── */\n\nint cg_tokenise_pan(const char *pan, char *out_token, size_t out_len)\n{\n    if (!pan || !out_token)                 return -EINVAL;\n    const size_t n = strlen(pan);\n    if (n == 0 || n > CG_PAN_MAX_LEN)       return -EINVAL;\n    if (out_len < n + 1)                    return -ENOSPC;\n    if (cg_validate_luhn(pan) != 0)         return -EINVAL;\n\n    /* Switchable algorithm for classroom demos */\n    if (getenv(\"EDUPAY_FPE\") && *getenv(\"EDUPAY_FPE\") == '1')\n    {\n        /* Placeholder for format-preserving AES-FF3-1 */\n        return cg_build_token(pan, out_token); /* Fallback path */\n    }\n    return cg_build_token(pan, out_token);\n}\n\nssize_t cg_seal(cg_key_t      *key,\n                const uint8_t *pt,\n                size_t         pt_len,\n                uint8_t       *out,\n                size_t         out_sz)\n{\n    if (!key || !pt || !out || pt_len == 0)  return -EINVAL;\n    const size_t need = CG_AES_GCM_IV_LEN + pt_len + CG_AES_GCM_TAG_LEN;\n    if (out_sz < need)                       return -ENOSPC;\n\n    uint8_t *iv     = out;\n    uint8_t *cipher = out + CG_AES_GCM_IV_LEN;\n    uint8_t *tag    = cipher + pt_len;\n\n    if (cg_generate_iv(iv, CG_AES_GCM_IV_LEN) != 0) return -EIO;\n\n    ssize_t ret = -EIO;\n    EVP_CIPHER_CTX *ctx = EVP_CIPHER_CTX_new();\n    if (!ctx) return -ENOMEM;\n\n    do {\n        if (EVP_EncryptInit_ex(ctx, EVP_aes_256_gcm(), NULL, NULL, NULL) != 1) break;\n        if (EVP_CIPHER_CTX_ctrl(ctx, EVP_CTRL_GCM_SET_IVLEN,\n                                CG_AES_GCM_IV_LEN, NULL) != 1)               break;\n        if (EVP_EncryptInit_ex(ctx, NULL, NULL, key->key, iv) != 1)           break;\n\n        int len = 0, total = 0;\n        if (EVP_EncryptUpdate(ctx, cipher, &len, pt, (int)pt_len) != 1)       break;\n        total += len;\n        if (EVP_EncryptFinal_ex(ctx, cipher + total, &len) != 1)              break;\n        total += len;\n        if (EVP_CIPHER_CTX_ctrl(ctx, EVP_CTRL_GCM_GET_TAG,\n                                CG_AES_GCM_TAG_LEN, tag) != 1)               break;\n\n        ret = CG_AES_GCM_IV_LEN + total + CG_AES_GCM_TAG_LEN;\n    } while (0);\n\n    EVP_CIPHER_CTX_free(ctx);\n    return ret;\n}\n\nssize_t cg_open(cg_key_t      *key,\n                const uint8_t *in,\n                size_t         in_len,\n                uint8_t       *out,\n                size_t         out_sz)\n{\n    if (!key || !in || !out)                         return -EINVAL;\n    if (in_len < CG_AES_GCM_IV_LEN + CG_AES_GCM_TAG_LEN) return -EINVAL;\n\n    const uint8_t *iv     = in;\n    const uint8_t *cipher = in + CG_AES_GCM_IV_LEN;\n    const size_t   ct_len = in_len - CG_AES_GCM_IV_LEN - CG_AES_GCM_TAG_LEN;\n    const uint8_t *tag    = in + in_len - CG_AES_GCM_TAG_LEN;\n\n    if (out_sz < ct_len)                             return -ENOSPC;\n\n    ssize_t ret = -EIO;\n    EVP_CIPHER_CTX *ctx = EVP_CIPHER_CTX_new();\n    if (!ctx) return -ENOMEM;\n\n    do {\n        if (EVP_DecryptInit_ex(ctx, EVP_aes_256_gcm(), NULL, NULL, NULL) != 1) break;\n        if (EVP_CIPHER_CTX_ctrl(ctx, EVP_CTRL_GCM_SET_IVLEN,\n                                CG_AES_GCM_IV_LEN, NULL) != 1)               break;\n        if (EVP_DecryptInit_ex(ctx, NULL, NULL, key->key, iv) != 1)           break;\n\n        int len = 0, total = 0;\n        if (EVP_DecryptUpdate(ctx, out, &len, cipher, (int)ct_len) != 1)      break;\n        total += len;\n\n        if (EVP_CIPHER_CTX_ctrl(ctx, EVP_CTRL_GCM_SET_TAG,\n                                CG_AES_GCM_TAG_LEN, (void *)tag) != 1)       break;\n        if (EVP_DecryptFinal_ex(ctx, out + total, &len) != 1) {\n            ret = -EBADMSG;                                                  break;\n        }\n        total += len;\n        ret = total;\n    } while (0);\n\n    EVP_CIPHER_CTX_free(ctx);\n    return ret;\n}\n\nvoid cg_secure_bzero(void *ptr, size_t len)\n{\n    if (!ptr || !len) return;\n#if defined(__STDC_LIB_EXT1__)\n    memset_s(ptr, len, 0, len);\n#else\n    volatile uint8_t *p = (volatile uint8_t *)ptr;\n    while (len--) *p++ = 0;\n#endif\n}\n\ncg_key_t *cg_key_acquire(void)\n{\n    cg_key_t *k = calloc(1, sizeof *k);\n    if (!k) return NULL;\n\n    const char *env = getenv(\"EDUPAY_COMPLIANCE_KEY\");\n    if (env && strlen(env) == 64)\n    {   /* Hex-decode deterministic, instructor-supplied key */\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < 32; ++i)\n            sscanf(&env[i * 2], \"%2hhx\", &k->key[i]);\n    }\n    else if (RAND_bytes(k->key, sizeof k->key) != 1)\n    {\n        free(k); k = NULL;\n    }\n    return k;\n}\n\nvoid cg_key_release(cg_key_t *key)\n{\n    if (!key) return;\n    cg_secure_bzero(key->key, sizeof key->key);\n    free(key);\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 3 ╱ 4 End-to-end console demo (`example_usage.c`)\n\n```c\n/* Compile with\n *   cc -std=c11 -Wall -Wextra example_usage.c compliance_guard.c -lcrypto -o demo\n * Run with\n *   EDUPAY_COMPLIANCE_KEY=$(openssl rand -hex 32) ./demo\n */\n#include <stdio.h>\n#include <string.h>\n\n#include \"compliance_guard.h\"\n\nint main(void)\n{\n    const char *pan = \"4485275742308327\"; /* Visa test card */\n\n    char token[CG_TOKEN_LEN + 1];\n    if (cg_tokenise_pan(pan, token, sizeof token) != 0) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"Tokenisation failed\\n\");\n        return 1;\n    }\n    printf(\"Tokenised PAN  : %s\\n\", token);\n\n    cg_key_t *key = cg_key_acquire();\n    if (!key) { perror(\"key_acquire\"); return 1; }\n\n    uint8_t cipher[256];\n    ssize_t n_cipher = cg_seal(key,\n                               (const uint8_t *)token, strlen(token),\n                               cipher, sizeof cipher);\n    cg_secure_bzero(token, sizeof token); /* Immediately destroy clear-text */\n    if (n_cipher < 0) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"Encryption failed: %zd\\n\", n_cipher);\n        cg_key_release(key);\n        return 1;\n    }\n\n    uint8_t clear[64];\n    ssize_t n_plain = cg_open(key, cipher, (size_t)n_cipher,\n                              clear, sizeof clear);\n    cg_key_release(key);\n\n    if (n_plain < 0) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"Decryption failed: %zd\\n\", n_plain);\n        return 1;\n    }\n    printf(\"Decrypted token: %.*s\\n\", (int)n_plain, clear);\n    cg_secure_bzero(clear, (size_t)n_plain);\n\n    return 0;\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4 ╱ 4 Lab exercises\n\n1. */\n    68→} evt_header_t;\n    69→\n    70→/* Event payloads --------------------------------------------------------- */\n    71→typedef struct {\n    72→    evt_header_t hdr;\n    73→    char         payment_id[32];\n    74→    char         student_id[32];\n    75→    char         currency[4]; /* ISO-4217 */\n    76→    uint64_t     amount_minor;/* minor units e.g. cents */\n    77→}  102→\n   103→/* A convenience union for generic handlers */\n   104→typedef union {\n   105→    evt_header_t               any;\n   106→    evt_payment_initiated_t    initiated;\n   107→    projection_close(ledger_projection_t* proj);\n   154→bool                 projection_handle_event(ledger_projection_t* proj, const ledger_event_t* evt, char* errbuf, size_t errlen);\n 162→\n   163→---\n   164→\n   165→## 5  In-Memory Stub (Unit-Test Harness)\n   166→\n   167→Quickly confirm our port by building an **In-Memory projection** before adding SQLite.\n   168→\n   169→```c\n   170→/* =========================================================================\n   171→ * File:    src/eduledger/projection_mem.c\n   172→ * Purpose: In-Memory projection for fast CI pipelines\n   173→ * ========================================================================= */\n   174→#include <stdlib.h>\n   175→#include <string.h>\n   176→#include \"eduledger/projection.h\"\n   177→\n   178→typedef struct {\n   179→    ledger_projection_t base;\n   180→    uint64_t            cursor;\n   181→    size_t              payments_total;\n   182→} mem_proj_t;\n   183→\n   184→/* --- Interface implementations ----------------------------------------- */\n   185→static ledger_projection_t* mem_open(const char* dsn, char* err, size_t len)\n   186→{\n   187→    (void)dsn; /* not used */\n   188→    mem_proj_t* p = calloc(1, sizeof *p);\n   189→    if (!p) {\n   190→        strncpy(err, \"OOM opening mem projection\", len);\n   191→        return NULL;\n   192→    }\n   193→    return (ledger_projection_t*)p;\n   194→}\n   195→\n   196→static void mem_close(ledger_projection_t* obj)\n   197→{\n   198→    free(obj);\n   199→}\n   200→\n   201→static bool mem_handle(ledger_projection_t* obj, const ledger_event_t* evt, char* err, size_t len)\n   202→{\n   203→    (void)err; (void)len;\n   204→    mem_proj_t* p = (mem_proj_t*)obj;\n   205→    /* idempotency: ignore older sequences */\n   206→    if (evt->any.hdr.sequence <= p->cursor) return true;\n   207→\n   208→    switch (evt->any.hdr.type) {\n   209→        case EVT_PAYMENT_INITIATED:\n   210→            p->payments_total++;\n   211→            break;\n   212→        default:\n   213→            /* other events ignored for demo */\n   214→            break;\n   215→    }\n   216→    p->cursor = evt->any.hdr.sequence;\n   217→    return true;\n   218→}\n   219→\n   220→static uint64_t mem_last(ledger_projection_t* obj)\n   221→{\n   222→    mem_proj_t* p = (mem_proj_t*)obj;\n   223→    return p->cursor;\n   224→}\n   225→\n   226→/* --- VTable registration ----------------------------------------------- */\n   227→static const ledger_projection_vtable_t VTABLE = {\n   228→    .open          = mem_open,\n   229→    .close         = mem_close,\n   230→    .handle_event  = mem_handle,\n   231→    .last_sequence = mem_last,\n   232→};\n   233→\n   234→/* Register at load time (constructor attr works on gcc/clang) */\n   235→__attribute__((constructor))\n   236→static void register_mem_projection(void)\n   237→{\n   238→    register_ledger_projection(&VTABLE);\n   239→}\n   240→```\n   241→\n   242→---\n   243→\n   244→## 6  SQLite3 Production Adapter\n   245→\n   246→The in-memory version is fine for CI but prod needs durability.   \n   247→Below is a fully functional **SQLite3 projection** with _idempotent upserts_ and **optimistic concurrency**.\n   248→\n   249→```c\n   250→/* =========================================================================\n   251→ * File:    src/eduledger/projection_sqlite.c\n   252→ * Purpose: Durable CQRS read model stored in SQLite\n   253→ * ========================================================================= */\n   254→#define _POSIX_C_SOURCE 200809L\n   255→#include <sqlite3.h>\n   256→#include <stdio.h>\n   257→#include <stdlib.h>\n   258→#include <string.h>\n   259→#include \"eduledger/projection.h\"\n   260→\n   261→#ifndef EDU_SQL_SCHEMA\n   262→#define EDU_SQL_SCHEMA                                                     \\\n   263→    \"PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL;\"                                             \\\n   264→    \"CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS projection_cursor(\"                        \\\n   265→    \"   id       INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK (id = 0),\"                      \\\n   266→    \"   seq      INTEGER NOT NULL\"                                         \\\n   267→    \");\"                                                                   \\\n   268→    \"INSERT OR IGNORE INTO projection_cursor(id, seq) VALUES(0, 0);\"       \\\n   269→    \"CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS vw_payments(\"                              \\\n   270→    \"   payment_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\"                                      \\\n   271→    \"   student_id TEXT NOT NULL,\"                                         \\\n   272→    \"   currency   TEXT NOT NULL,\"                                         \\\n   273→    \"   amount_minor INTEGER NOT NULL,\"                                    \\\n   274→    \"   status     TEXT NOT NULL,\"                                         \\\n   275→    \"   last_event_seq INTEGER NOT NULL\"                                   \\\n   276→    \");\"\n   277→#endif /* EDU_SQL_SCHEMA */\n   278→\n   279→typedef struct {\n   280→    ledger_projection_t base;\n   281→    sqlite3*            db;\n   282→} sqlite_proj_t;\n   283→\n   284→/* Error helper ----------------------------------------------------------- */\n   285→static bool sqlite_check(int rc, sqlite3* db, char* err, size_t len)\n   286→{\n   287→    if (rc == SQLITE_OK || rc == SQLITE_DONE || rc == SQLITE_ROW) return true;\n   288→    snprintf(err, len, \"SQLite error %d: %s\", rc, sqlite3_errmsg(db));\n   289→    return false;\n   290→}\n   291→\n   292→/* --- Interface implementations ----------------------------------------- */\n   293→static ledger_projection_t* s_open(const char* dsn, char* err, size_t len)\n   294→{\n   295→    sqlite3* db = NULL;\n   296→    int rc = sqlite3_open_v2(dsn ? dsn : \":memory:\",\n   297→                             &db,\n   298→                             SQLITE_OPEN_READWRITE | SQLITE_OPEN_CREATE | SQLITE_OPEN_FULLMUTEX,\n   299→                             NULL);\n   300→    if (!sqlite_check(rc, db, err, len)) return NULL;\n   301→\n   302→    rc = sqlite3_exec(db, EDU_SQL_SCHEMA, NULL, NULL, NULL);\n   303→    if (!sqlite_check(rc, db, err, len)) { sqlite3_close(db); return NULL; }\n   304→\n   305→    sqlite_proj_t* p = calloc(1, sizeof *p);\n   306→    if (!p) { strncpy(err, \"OOM\", len); sqlite3_close(db); return NULL; }\n   307→    p->db = db;\n   308→    return (ledger_projection_t*)p;\n   309→}\n   310→\n   311→static void s_close(ledger_projection_t* obj)\n   312→{\n   313→    sqlite_proj_t* p = (sqlite_proj_t*)obj;\n   314→    if (!p) return;\n   315→    sqlite3_close(p->db);\n   316→    free(p);\n   317→}\n   318→\n   319→static uint64_t s_last(ledger_projection_t* obj)\n   320→{\n   321→    sqlite_proj_t* p = (sqlite_proj_t*)obj;\n   322→    uint64_t seq = 0;\n   323→    sqlite3_stmt* stmt = NULL;\n   324→    const char* sql = \"SELECT seq FROM projection_cursor WHERE id = 0;\";\n   325→    if (sqlite3_prepare_v2(p->db, sql, -1, &stmt, NULL) == SQLITE_OK &&\n   326→        sqlite3_step(stmt) == SQLITE_ROW)\n   327→    {\n   328→        seq = (uint64_t)sqlite3_column_int64(stmt, 0);\n   329→    }\n   330→    sqlite3_finalize(stmt);\n   331→    return seq;\n   332→}\n   333→\n   334→static bool s_update_cursor(sqlite_proj_t* p, uint64_t seq, char* err, size_t len)\n   335→{\n   336→    sqlite3_stmt* stmt = NULL;\n   337→    const char* sql = \"UPDATE projection_cursor SET seq = ? WHERE id = 0;\";\n   338→    int rc = sqlite3_prepare_v2(p->db, sql, -1, &stmt, NULL);\n   339→    if (!sqlite_check(rc, p->db, err, len)) return false;\n   340→\n   341→    sqlite3_bind_int64(stmt, 1, (sqlite3_int64)seq);\n   342→    rc = sqlite3_step(stmt);\n   343→    sqlite3_finalize(stmt);\n   344→    return sqlite_check(rc, p->db, err, len);\n   345→}\n   346→\n   347→static bool handle_payment_initiated(sqlite_proj_t* p,\n   348→                                     const evt_payment_initiated_t* e,\n   349→                                     char* err, size_t len)\n   350→{\n   351→    const char* sql =\n   352→        \"INSERT INTO vw_payments(payment_id, student_id, currency, amount_minor, status, last_event_seq) \"\n   353→        \"VALUES(?, ?, ?, ?, 'INITIATED', ?)\"\n   354→        \"ON CONFLICT(payment_id) DO UPDATE SET \"\n   355→        \"  student_id=excluded.student_id,\"\n   356→        \"  currency=excluded.currency,\"\n   357→        \"  amount_minor=excluded.amount_minor,\"\n   358→        \"  status='INITIATED',\"\n   359→        \"  last_event_seq=excluded.last_event_seq \"\n   360→        \"WHERE excluded.last_event_seq > vw_payments.last_event_seq;\";\n   361→\n   362→    sqlite3_stmt* stmt = NULL;\n   363→    int rc = sqlite3_prepare_v2(p->db, sql, -1, &stmt, NULL);\n   364→    if (!sqlite_check(rc, p->db, err, len)) return false;\n   365→\n   366→    sqlite3_bind_text(stmt, 1, e->payment_id, -1, SQLITE_STATIC);\n   367→    sqlite3_bind_text(stmt, 2, e->student_id, -1, SQLITE_STATIC);\n   368→    sqlite3_bind_text(stmt, 3, e->currency, -1, SQLITE_STATIC);\n   369→    sqlite3_bind_int64(stmt, 4, (sqlite3_int64)e->amount_minor);\n   370→    sqlite3_bind_int64(stmt, 5, (sqlite3_int64)e->hdr.sequence);\n   371→\n   372→    rc = sqlite3_step(stmt);\n   373→    sqlite3_finalize(stmt);\n   374→    return sqlite_check(rc, p->db, err, len);\n   375→}\n   376→\n   377→static bool handle_payment_settled(sqlite_proj_t* p,\n   378→                                   const evt_payment_settled_t* e,\n   379→                                   char* err, size_t len)\n   380→{\n   381→    const char* sql =\n   382→        \"UPDATE vw_payments SET status='SETTLED', last_event_seq=? \"\n   383→        \"WHERE payment_id=? AND ? > last_event_seq;\";\n   384→\n   385→    sqlite3_stmt* stmt = NULL;\n   386→    int rc = sqlite3_prepare_v2(p->db, sql, -1, &stmt, NULL);\n   387→    if (!sqlite_check(rc, p->db, err, len)) return false;\n   388→\n   389→    sqlite3_bind_int64(stmt, 1, (sqlite3_int64)e->hdr.sequence);\n   390→    sqlite3_bind_text(stmt, 2, e->payment_id, -1, SQLITE_STATIC);\n   391→    sqlite3_bind_int64(stmt, 3, (sqlite3_int64)e->hdr.sequence);\n   392→\n   393→    rc = sqlite3_step(stmt);\n   394→    sqlite3_finalize(stmt);\n   395→    return sqlite_check(rc, p->db, err, len);\n   396→}\n   397→\n   398→static bool s_handle(ledger_projection_t* obj, const ledger_event_t* e, char* err, size_t len)\n   399→{\n   400→    sqlite_proj_t* p = (sqlite_proj_t*)obj;\n   401→\n   402→    /* idempotency short-circuit */\n   403→    if (e->any.hdr.sequence <= s_last(obj)) return true;\n   404→\n   405→    int rc = sqlite3_exec(p->db, \"BEGIN IMMEDIATE TRANSACTION;\", NULL, NULL, NULL);\n   406→    if (!sqlite_check(rc, p->db, err, len)) return false;\n   407→\n   408→    bool ok = true;\n   409→    switch (e->any.hdr.type) {\n   410→        case EVT_PAYMENT_INITIATED:\n   411→            ok = handle_payment_initiated(p, &e->initiated, err, len);\n   412→            break;\n   413→        case EVT_PAYMENT_SETTLED:\n   414→            ok = handle_payment_settled(p, &e->settled, err, len);\n   415→            break;\n   416→        default:\n   417→            /* unsupported events are ignored for this projection */\n   418→            break;\n   419→    }\n   420→\n   421→    if (ok) ok = s_update_cursor(p, e->any.hdr.sequence, err, len);\n   422→\n   423→    rc = sqlite3_exec(p->db, ok ? \"COMMIT;\" : \"ROLLBACK;\", NULL, NULL, NULL);\n   424→    if (!sqlite_check(rc, p->db, err, len)) ok = false;\n   425→\n   426→    return ok;\n   427→}\n   428→\n   429→/* --- VTable registration ----------------------------------------------- */\n   430→static const ledger_projection_vtable_t VTABLE = {\n   431→    .open          = s_open,\n   432→    .close         = s_close,\n   433→    .handle_event  = s_handle,\n   434→    .last_sequence = s_last,\n   435→};\n   436→\n   437→__attribute__((constructor))\n   438→static void register_sqlite_projection(void)\n   439→{\n   440→    register_ledger_projection(&VTABLE);\n   441→}\n   442→```\n   443→\n   444→---\n   445→\n   446→## 7  Projection Registry (Shared)\n   447→\n   448→All adapters share a **singleton registry** so the application can load whichever is linked first.\n   449→\n   450→```c\n   451→/* =========================================================================\n   452→ * File:    src/eduledger/projection_registry.c\n   453→ * Purpose: Adapter registry – decouples caller from implementation\n   454→ * ========================================================================= */\n   455→#include <pthread.h>\n   456→#include <string.h>\n   457→#include \"eduledger/projection.h\"\n   458→\n   459→static pthread_mutex_t                lock = PTHREAD_MUTEX_INITIALIZER;\n   460→static const ledger_projection_vtable_t* active = NULL;\n   461→\n   462→bool register_ledger_projection(const ledger_projection_vtable_t* vtable)\n   463→{\n   464→    pthread_mutex_lock(&lock);\n   465→    if (!active) active = vtable; /* first wins */\n   466→    pthread_mutex_unlock(&lock);\n   467→    return true;\n   468→}\n   469→\n   470→ledger_projection_t* projection_open(const char* dsn, char* err, size_t len)\n   471→{\n   472→    if (!active) { strncpy(err, \"No projection adapter registered\", len); return NULL; }\n   473→    return active->open(dsn, err, len);\n   474→}\n   475→\n   476→void projection_close(ledger_projection_t* p)\n   477→{\n   478→    if (p && active) active->close(p);\n   479→}\n   480→\n   481→bool projection_handle_event(ledger_projection_t* p, const ledger_event_t* e, char* err, size_t len)\n   482→{\n   483→    return p && active && active->handle_event(p, e, err, len);\n   484→}\n   485→\n   486→uint64_t projection_last_sequence(ledger_projection_t* p)\n   487→{\n   488→    return (p && active) ? active->last_sequence(p) : 0;\n   489→}\n   490→```\n   491→\n   492→---\n   493→\n   494→## 8  Driver Loop Example\n   495→\n   496→The event store will invoke `projection_handle_event()` for each new commit.  \n   497→For local testing we bundle a minimal CLI.\n   498→\n   499→```c\n   500→/* =========================================================================\n   501→ * File:    apps/projection_cli.c\n   502→ * Purpose: Stand-alone tool to hydrate a projection DB from an event log\n   503→ * ========================================================================= */\n   504→#define _POSIX_C_SOURCE 200809L\n   505→#include <stdio.h>\n   506→#include <stdlib.h>\n   507→#include <string.h>\n   508→#include \"eduledger/projection.h\"\n   509→\n   510→static void die(const char* msg)\n   511→{\n   512→    fprintf(stderr, \"fatal: %s\\n\", msg);\n   513→    exit(EXIT_FAILURE);\n   514→}\n   515→\n   516→/* Simulate reading newline-delimited events from stdin */\n   517→static ledger_event_t parse_event(const char* line)\n   518→{\n   519→    ledger_event_t e = {0};\n   520→    /* CSV: seq,type,payment,student,amount */\n   521→    sscanf(line, \"%lu,%d,%31[^,],%31[^,],%lu\",\n   522→           &e.any.hdr.sequence,\n   523→           (int*)&e.any.hdr.type,\n   524→           e.initiated.payment_id,\n   525→           e.initiated.student_id,\n   526→           &e.initiated.amount_minor);\n   527→    e.any.hdr.ts_utc = time(NULL);\n   528→    strcpy(e.initiated.currency, \"USD\");\n   529→    return e;\n   530→}\n   531→\n   532→int main(int argc, char* argv[])\n   533→{\n   534→    char err[256] = {0};\n   535→    const char* dsn = argc > 1 ? argv[1] : \"file:projection.db?cache=shared\";\n   536→\n   537→    ledger_projection_t* p = projection_open(dsn, err, sizeof err);\n   538→    if (!p) die(err);\n   539→\n   540→    char*  line = NULL;\n   541→    size_t n    = 0;\n   542→    while (getline(&line, &n, stdin) != -1) {\n   543→        ledger_event_t evt = parse_event(line);\n   544→        if (!projection_handle_event(p, &evt, err, sizeof err))\n   545→            die(err);\n   546→    }\n   547→    free(line);\n   548→\n   549→    printf(\"Projection at sequence %lu\\n\", projection_last_sequence(p));\n   550→    projection_close(p);\n   551→    return 0;\n   552→}\n   553→```\n   554→\n   555→---\n   556→\n   557→## 9  Compilation (GNU Make)\n   558→\n   559→```make\n   560→CC      ?= gcc\n   561→CFLAGS  := -std=c17 -Wall -Wextra -pedantic -O2 -fPIC\n   562→LDFLAGS := -lsqlite3 -lpthread\n   563→\n   564→SRC := $(shell find src -name '*.c')\n   565→OBJ := $(SRC:.c=.o)\n   566→\n   567→all: projection_cli\n   568→\n   569→projection_cli: $(OBJ) apps/projection_cli.o\n   570→\t$(CC) $^ $(LDFLAGS) -o $@\n   571→\n   572→%.o: %.c\n   573→\t$(CC) $(CFLAGS) -Iinclude -c $< -o $@\n   574→\n   575→clean:\n   576→\trm -f $(OBJ) apps/*.o projection_cli projection.db\n   577→```\n   578→\n   579→---\n   580→\n   581→## 10  Exercises\n   582→\n   583→1. Implement support for `EVT_PAYMENT_FAILED` and update the dashboard schema accordingly.  \n   584→2. Add a **Prometheus** exporter that reports `projection_lag_seconds = now() – last_event.ts_utc`.  \n   585→3. Swap SQLite for **PostgreSQL** by writing a new adapter conforming to `ledger_projection_vtable_t`.\n   586→\n   587→Happy projecting! 🚀\n   588→```\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware.
