Lesson 06 – Building CQRS Projections in _EduPay Ledger Academy_\n> “Queries in milliseconds, audits for decades.”  \n> —⁠_Platform Engineering Motto_\n\n---\n\n## 1  Learning Objectives\nBy the end of this lab you will be able to:\n\n* Enumerate the major responsibilities of a **CQRS Projection** in a payment‐processing system  \n* Implement a production‐grade read model in C that stays **100 % consistent** with the event store  \n* Isolate projection code behind a **port/adapter boundary** so that professors can swap storage engines during coursework  \n* Write an **idempotent** event-handling loop that survives crashes and supports once/only‐once delivery semantics  \n* Measure projection latency with a **Prometheus**‐friendly metric feed  \n\n> NOTE  \n> All code presented in this lesson compiles on `clang >= 14` or `gcc >= 12` and follows **C17**.  \n> A minimal Makefile is included at the end of the document.\n\n---\n\n## 2  Domain Recap\n\n```\n┌──────────────────────────┐             ┌───────────────────────┐\n│ WRITE MODEL (Commands)   │             │ READ MODEL (Queries)  │\n│  ledger_command_service  │────────────▶│  ledger_projection    │\n└──────────────────────────┘    Events   └───────────────────────┘\n```  \n\nThe **write model**—implemented in `src/ledger/commands/*`—is already capable of persisting business events to the _Event Store_.  \nYour job is to consume those events and project them into a **payment dashboard** optimized for low-latency read queries.\n\n---\n\n## 3  Event Definitions\n\nCreate a new header `include/eduledger/events.h` that centralises _immutable_ event shapes shared between micro-services.\n\n```c\n/* =========================================================================\n * File:    include/eduledger/events.h\n * Purpose: Canonical event definitions for Payment-Saga bounded context\n * Author:  EduPay Ledger Academy – Lesson 06\n * ========================================================================= */\n#ifndef EDU_LEDGER_EVENTS_H\n#define EDU_LEDGER_EVENTS_H\n\n#include <stdint.h>\n#include <time.h>\n\ntypedef enum {\n    EVT_NONE = 0,\n    EVT_PAYMENT_INITIATED,\n    EVT_PAYMENT_AUTHORIZED,\n    EVT_PAYMENT_CAPTURED,\n    EVT_PAYMENT_SETTLED,\n    EVT_PAYMENT_FAILED,\n} evt_type_t;\n\n/* Every event carries a header for routing & traceability */\ntypedef struct {\n    uint64_t    sequence;   /* monotonic sequence number from Event Store */\n    evt_type_t  type;       /* domain event discriminator                */\n    time_t      ts_utc;     /* server-side timestamp, seconds since EPOCH*/\n    char        saga_id[40];/* distributed-tx correlation id (UUID-v4)   */\n} evt_header_t;\n\n/* Event payloads --------------------------------------------------------- */\ntypedef struct {\n    evt_header_t hdr;\n    char         payment_id[32];\n    char         student_id[32];\n    char         currency[4]; /* ISO-4217 */\n    uint64_t     amount_minor;/* minor units e.g. cents */\n} evt_payment_initiated_t;\n\ntypedef struct {\n    evt_header_t hdr;\n    char         payment_id[32];\n    char         authorization_code[16];\n} evt_payment_authorized_t;\n\ntypedef struct {\n    evt_header_t hdr;\n    char         payment_id[32];\n    uint64_t     fee_minor;\n} evt_payment_captured_t;\n\ntypedef struct {\n    evt_header_t hdr;\n    char         payment_id[32];\n    char         settlement_batch_id[32];\n} evt_payment_settled_t;\n\ntypedef struct {\n    evt_header_t hdr;\n    char         payment_id[32];\n    char         reason[128];\n} evt_payment_failed_t;\n\n/* A convenience union for generic handlers */\ntypedef union {\n    evt_header_t               any;\n    evt_payment_initiated_t    initiated;\n    evt_payment_authorized_t   authorized;\n    evt_payment_captured_t     captured;\n    evt_payment_settled_t      settled;\n    evt_payment_failed_t       failed;\n} ledger_event_t;\n\n#endif /* EDU_LEDGER_EVENTS_H */\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4  Projection Port (Header Only)\n\nDefine a **Clean-Architecture port** so that multiple storage‐specific adapters can plug in.\n\n```c\n/* =========================================================================\n * File:    include/eduledger/projection.h\n * Purpose: Projection interface for CQRS read models\n * ========================================================================= */\n#ifndef EDU_LEDGER_PROJECTION_H\n#define EDU_LEDGER_PROJECTION_H\n\n#include <stdbool.h>\n#include <stddef.h>\n#include \"eduledger/events.h\"\n\n#ifdef __cplusplus\nextern \"C\" {\n#endif\n\ntypedef struct ledger_projection ledger_projection_t;\n\n/* Factory function pointer table */\ntypedef struct {\n    ledger_projection_t* (*open)(const char* dsn, char* errbuf, size_t errlen);\n    void                 (*close)(ledger_projection_t*);\n    bool                 (*handle_event)(ledger_projection_t*, const ledger_event_t*, char* errbuf, size_t errlen);\n    uint64_t             (*last_sequence)(ledger_projection_t*);\n} ledger_projection_vtable_t;\n\n/* Adapter registration (one per .so or static obj) */\nbool register_ledger_projection(const ledger_projection_vtable_t* vtable);\n\n/* Global factory helpers ------------------------------------------------- */\nledger_projection_t* projection_open(const char* dsn, char* errbuf, size_t errlen);\nvoid                 projection_close(ledger_projection_t* proj);\nbool                 projection_handle_event(ledger_projection_t* proj, const ledger_event_t* evt, char* errbuf, size_t errlen);\nuint64_t             projection_last_sequence(ledger_projection_t* proj);\n\n#ifdef __cplusplus\n}\n#endif\n#endif /* EDU_LEDGER_PROJECTION_H */\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5  In-Memory Stub (Unit-Test Harness)\n\nQuickly confirm our port by building an **In-Memory projection** before adding SQLite.\n\n```c\n/* =========================================================================\n * File:    src/eduledger/projection_mem.c\n * Purpose: In-Memory projection for fast CI pipelines\n * ========================================================================= */\n#include <stdlib.h>\n#include <string.h>\n#include \"eduledger/projection.h\"\n\ntypedef struct {\n    ledger_projection_t base;\n    uint64_t            cursor;\n    size_t              payments_total;\n} mem_proj_t;\n\n/* --- Interface implementations ----------------------------------------- */\nstatic ledger_projection_t* mem_open(const char* dsn, char* err, size_t len)\n{\n    (void)dsn; /* not used */\n    mem_proj_t* p = calloc(1, sizeof *p);\n    if (!p) {\n        strncpy(err, \"OOM opening mem projection\", len);\n        return NULL;\n    }\n    return (ledger_projection_t*)p;\n}\n\nstatic void mem_close(ledger_projection_t* obj)\n{\n    free(obj);\n}\n\nstatic bool mem_handle(ledger_projection_t* obj, const ledger_event_t* evt, char* err, size_t len)\n{\n    (void)err; (void)len;\n    mem_proj_t* p = (mem_proj_t*)obj;\n    /* idempotency: ignore older sequences */\n    if (evt->any.hdr.sequence <= p->cursor) return true;\n\n    switch (evt->any.hdr.type) {\n        case EVT_PAYMENT_INITIATED:\n            p->payments_total++;\n            break;\n        default:\n            /* other events ignored for demo */\n            break;\n    }\n    p->cursor = evt->any.hdr.sequence;\n    return true;\n}\n\nstatic uint64_t mem_last(ledger_projection_t* obj)\n{\n    mem_proj_t* p = (mem_proj_t*)obj;\n    return p->cursor;\n}\n\n/* --- VTable registration ----------------------------------------------- */\nstatic const ledger_projection_vtable_t VTABLE = {\n    .open          = mem_open,\n    .close         = mem_close,\n    .handle_event  = mem_handle,\n    .last_sequence = mem_last,\n};\n\n/* Register at load time (constructor attr works on gcc/clang) */\n__attribute__((constructor))\nstatic void register_mem_projection(void)\n{\n    register_ledger_projection(&VTABLE);\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 6  SQLite3 Production Adapter\n\nThe in-memory version is fine for CI but prod needs durability.   \nBelow is a fully functional **SQLite3 projection** with _idempotent upserts_ and **optimistic concurrency**.\n\n```c\n/* =========================================================================\n * File:    src/eduledger/projection_sqlite.c\n * Purpose: Durable CQRS read model stored in SQLite\n * ========================================================================= */\n#define _POSIX_C_SOURCE 200809L\n#include <sqlite3.h>\n#include <stdio.h>\n#include <stdlib.h>\n#include <string.h>\n#include \"eduledger/projection.h\"\n\n#ifndef EDU_SQL_SCHEMA\n#define EDU_SQL_SCHEMA                                                     \\\n    \"PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL;\"                                             \\\n    \"CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS projection_cursor(\"                        \\\n    \"   id       INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK (id = 0),\"                      \\\n    \"   seq      INTEGER NOT NULL\"                                         \\\n    \");\"                                                                   \\\n    \"INSERT OR IGNORE INTO projection_cursor(id, seq) VALUES(0, 0);\"       \\\n    \"CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS vw_payments(\"                              \\\n    \"   payment_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\"                                      \\\n    \"   student_id TEXT NOT NULL,\"                                         \\\n    \"   currency   TEXT NOT NULL,\"                                         \\\n    \"   amount_minor INTEGER NOT NULL,\"                                    \\\n    \"   status     TEXT NOT NULL,\"                                         \\\n    \"   last_event_seq INTEGER NOT NULL\"                                   \\\n    \");\"\n#endif /* EDU_SQL_SCHEMA */\n\ntypedef struct {\n    ledger_projection_t base;\n    sqlite3*            db;\n} sqlite_proj_t;\n\n/* Error helper ----------------------------------------------------------- */\nstatic bool sqlite_check(int rc, sqlite3* db, char* err, size_t len)\n{\n    if (rc == SQLITE_OK || rc == SQLITE_DONE || rc == SQLITE_ROW) return true;\n    snprintf(err, len, \"SQLite error %d: %s\", rc, sqlite3_errmsg(db));\n    return false;\n}\n\n/* --- Interface implementations ----------------------------------------- */\nstatic ledger_projection_t* s_open(const char* dsn, char* err, size_t len)\n{\n    sqlite3* db = NULL;\n    int rc = sqlite3_open_v2(dsn ? dsn : \":memory:\",\n                             &db,\n                             SQLITE_OPEN_READWRITE | SQLITE_OPEN_CREATE | SQLITE_OPEN_FULLMUTEX,\n                             NULL);\n    if (!sqlite_check(rc, db, err, len)) return NULL;\n\n    rc = sqlite3_exec(db, EDU_SQL_SCHEMA, NULL, NULL, NULL);\n    if (!sqlite_check(rc, db, err, len)) { sqlite3_close(db); return NULL; }\n\n    sqlite_proj_t* p = calloc(1, sizeof *p);\n    if (!p) { strncpy(err, \"OOM\", len); sqlite3_close(db); return NULL; }\n    p->db = db;\n    return (ledger_projection_t*)p;\n}\n\nstatic void s_close(ledger_projection_t* obj)\n{\n    sqlite_proj_t* p = (sqlite_proj_t*)obj;\n    if (!p) return;\n    sqlite3_close(p->db);\n    free(p);\n}\n\nstatic uint64_t s_last(ledger_projection_t* obj)\n{\n    sqlite_proj_t* p = (sqlite_proj_t*)obj;\n    uint64_t seq = 0;\n    sqlite3_stmt* stmt = NULL;\n    const char* sql = \"SELECT seq FROM projection_cursor WHERE id = 0;\";\n    if (sqlite3_prepare_v2(p->db, sql, -1, &stmt, NULL) == SQLITE_OK &&\n        sqlite3_step(stmt) == SQLITE_ROW)\n    {\n        seq = (uint64_t)sqlite3_column_int64(stmt, 0);\n    }\n    sqlite3_finalize(stmt);\n    return seq;\n}\n\nstatic bool s_update_cursor(sqlite_proj_t* p, uint64_t seq, char* err, size_t len)\n{\n    sqlite3_stmt* stmt = NULL;\n    const char* sql = \"UPDATE projection_cursor SET seq = ? WHERE id = 0;\";\n    int rc = sqlite3_prepare_v2(p->db, sql, -1, &stmt, NULL);\n    if (!sqlite_check(rc, p->db, err, len)) return false;\n\n    sqlite3_bind_int64(stmt, 1, (sqlite3_int64)seq);\n    rc = sqlite3_step(stmt);\n    sqlite3_finalize(stmt);\n    return sqlite_check(rc, p->db, err, len);\n}\n\nstatic bool handle_payment_initiated(sqlite_proj_t* p,\n                                     const evt_payment_initiated_t* e,\n                                     char* err, size_t len)\n{\n    const char* sql =\n        \"INSERT INTO vw_payments(payment_id, student_id, currency, amount_minor, status, last_event_seq) \"\n        \"VALUES(?, ?, ?, ?, 'INITIATED', ?)\"\n        \"ON CONFLICT(payment_id) DO UPDATE SET \"\n        \"  student_id=excluded.student_id,\"\n        \"  currency=excluded.currency,\"\n        \"  amount_minor=excluded.amount_minor,\"\n        \"  status='INITIATED',\"\n        \"  last_event_seq=excluded.last_event_seq \"\n        \"WHERE excluded.last_event_seq > vw_payments.last_event_seq;\";\n\n    sqlite3_stmt* stmt = NULL;\n    int rc = sqlite3_prepare_v2(p->db, sql, -1, &stmt, NULL);\n    if (!sqlite_check(rc, p->db, err, len)) return false;\n\n    sqlite3_bind_text(stmt, 1, e->payment_id, -1, SQLITE_STATIC);\n    sqlite3_bind_text(stmt, 2, e->student_id, -1, SQLITE_STATIC);\n    sqlite3_bind_text(stmt, 3, e->currency, -1, SQLITE_STATIC);\n    sqlite3_bind_int64(stmt, 4, (sqlite3_int64)e->amount_minor);\n    sqlite3_bind_int64(stmt, 5, (sqlite3_int64)e->hdr.sequence);\n\n    rc = sqlite3_step(stmt);\n    sqlite3_finalize(stmt);\n    return sqlite_check(rc, p->db, err, len);\n}\n\nstatic bool handle_payment_settled(sqlite_proj_t* p,\n                                   const evt_payment_settled_t* e,\n                                   char* err, size_t len)\n{\n    const char* sql =\n        \"UPDATE vw_payments SET status='SETTLED', last_event_seq=? \"\n        \"WHERE payment_id=? AND ? > last_event_seq;\";\n\n    sqlite3_stmt* stmt = NULL;\n    int rc = sqlite3_prepare_v2(p->db, sql, -1, &stmt, NULL);\n    if (!sqlite_check(rc, p->db, err, len)) return false;\n\n    sqlite3_bind_int64(stmt, 1, (sqlite3_int64)e->hdr.sequence);\n    sqlite3_bind_text(stmt, 2, e->payment_id, -1, SQLITE_STATIC);\n    sqlite3_bind_int64(stmt, 3, (sqlite3_int64)e->hdr.sequence);\n\n    rc = sqlite3_step(stmt);\n    sqlite3_finalize(stmt);\n    return sqlite_check(rc, p->db, err, len);\n}\n\nstatic bool s_handle(ledger_projection_t* obj, const ledger_event_t* e, char* err, size_t len)\n{\n    sqlite_proj_t* p = (sqlite_proj_t*)obj;\n\n    /* idempotency short-circuit */\n    if (e->any.hdr.sequence <= s_last(obj)) return true;\n\n    int rc = sqlite3_exec(p->db, \"BEGIN IMMEDIATE TRANSACTION;\", NULL, NULL, NULL);\n    if (!sqlite_check(rc, p->db, err, len)) return false;\n\n    bool ok = true;\n    switch (e->any.hdr.type) {\n        case EVT_PAYMENT_INITIATED:\n            ok = handle_payment_initiated(p, &e->initiated, err, len);\n            break;\n        case EVT_PAYMENT_SETTLED:\n            ok = handle_payment_settled(p, &e->settled, err, len);\n            break;\n        default:\n            /* unsupported events are ignored for this projection */\n            break;\n    }\n\n    if (ok) ok = s_update_cursor(p, e->any.hdr.sequence, err, len);\n\n    rc = sqlite3_exec(p->db, ok ? \"COMMIT;\" : \"ROLLBACK;\", NULL, NULL, NULL);\n    if (!sqlite_check(rc, p->db, err, len)) ok = false;\n\n    return ok;\n}\n\n/* --- VTable registration ----------------------------------------------- */\nstatic const ledger_projection_vtable_t VTABLE = {\n    .open          = s_open,\n    .close         = s_close,\n    .handle_event  = s_handle,\n    .last_sequence = s_last,\n};\n\n__attribute__((constructor))\nstatic void register_sqlite_projection(void)\n{\n    register_ledger_projection(&VTABLE);\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 7  Projection Registry (Shared)\n\nAll adapters share a **singleton registry** so the application can load whichever is linked first.\n\n```c\n/* =========================================================================\n * File:    src/eduledger/projection_registry.c\n * Purpose: Adapter registry – decouples caller from implementation\n * ========================================================================= */\n#include <pthread.h>\n#include <string.h>\n#include \"eduledger/projection.h\"\n\nstatic pthread_mutex_t                lock = PTHREAD_MUTEX_INITIALIZER;\nstatic const ledger_projection_vtable_t* active = NULL;\n\nbool register_ledger_projection(const ledger_projection_vtable_t* vtable)\n{\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&lock);\n    if (!active) active = vtable; /* first wins */\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&lock);\n    return true;\n}\n\nledger_projection_t* projection_open(const char* dsn, char* err, size_t len)\n{\n    if (!active) { strncpy(err, \"No projection adapter registered\", len); return NULL; }\n    return active->open(dsn, err, len);\n}\n\nvoid projection_close(ledger_projection_t* p)\n{\n    if (p && active) active->close(p);\n}\n\nbool projection_handle_event(ledger_projection_t* p, const ledger_event_t* e, char* err, size_t len)\n{\n    return p && active && active->handle_event(p, e, err, len);\n}\n\nuint64_t projection_last_sequence(ledger_projection_t* p)\n{\n    return (p && active) ? active->last_sequence(p) : 0;\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 8  Driver Loop Example\n\nThe event store will invoke `projection_handle_event()` for each new commit.  \nFor local testing we bundle a minimal CLI.\n\n```c\n/* =========================================================================\n * File:    apps/projection_cli.c\n * Purpose: Stand-alone tool to hydrate a projection DB from an event log\n * ========================================================================= */\n#define _POSIX_C_SOURCE 200809L\n#include <stdio.h>\n#include <stdlib.h>\n#include <string.h>\n#include \"eduledger/projection.h\"\n\nstatic void die(const char* msg)\n{\n    fprintf(stderr, \"fatal: %s\\n\", msg);\n    exit(EXIT_FAILURE);\n}\n\n/* Simulate reading newline-delimited events from stdin */\nstatic ledger_event_t parse_event(const char* line)\n{\n    ledger_event_t e = {0};\n    /* CSV: seq,type,payment,student,amount */\n    sscanf(line, \"%lu,%d,%31[^,],%31[^,],%lu\",\n           &e.any.hdr.sequence,\n           (int*)&e.any.hdr.type,\n           e.initiated.payment_id,\n           e.initiated.student_id,\n           &e.initiated.amount_minor);\n    e.any.hdr.ts_utc = time(NULL);\n    strcpy(e.initiated.currency, \"USD\");\n    return e;\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char* argv[])\n{\n    char err[256] = {0};\n    const char* dsn = argc > 1 ? argv[1] : \"file:projection.db?cache=shared\";\n\n    ledger_projection_t* p = projection_open(dsn, err, sizeof err);\n    if (!p) die(err);\n\n    char*  line = NULL;\n    size_t n    = 0;\n    while (getline(&line, &n, stdin) != -1) {\n        ledger_event_t evt = parse_event(line);\n        if (!projection_handle_event(p, &evt, err, sizeof err))\n            die(err);\n    }\n    free(line);\n\n    printf(\"Projection at sequence %lu\\n\", projection_last_sequence(p));\n    projection_close(p);\n    return 0;\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 9  Compilation (GNU Make)\n\n```make\nCC      ?= gcc\nCFLAGS  := -std=c17 -Wall -Wextra -pedantic -O2 -fPIC\nLDFLAGS := -lsqlite3 -lpthread\n\nSRC := $(shell find src -name '*.c')\nOBJ := $(SRC:.c=.o)\n\nall: projection_cli\n\nprojection_cli: $(OBJ) apps/projection_cli.o\n\t$(CC) $^ $(LDFLAGS) -o $@\n\n%.o: %.c\n\t$(CC) $(CFLAGS) -Iinclude -c $< -o $@\n\nclean:\n\trm -f $(OBJ) apps/*.o projection_cli projection.db\n```\n\n---\n\n## 10  Exercises\n\n1. Implement support for `EVT_PAYMENT_FAILED` and update the dashboard schema accordingly.  \n2. Add a **Prometheus** exporter that reports `projection_lag_seconds = now() – last_event.ts_utc`.  \n3. Swap SQLite for **PostgreSQL** by writing a new adapter conforming to `ledger_projection_vtable_t`.\n\nHappy projecting! 🚀\n```","numLines":588,"startLine":1,"totalLines":588}}}

\"Compensation for step %s failed.\", step->name);\n   497→            /* Compensation failures are logged, but saga continues */\n   498→        }\n   499→    }\n   500→\n   501→    saga.state = SAGA_TAG, saga->saga_id, fmt, args);\n    va_end(args);\n}\n\nstatic void\nsaga_publish_event(disburse_stipend_saga_t *saga,\n                   const stipend_event_t   *event)\n{\n    if (!saga || !saga->event_bus || !event) { return; }\n\n    event_bus_publish(saga->event_bus, (const event_base_t *)event);\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*  Forward Step Implementations                                              */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n/* ---- Step 1: Compliance Check ------------------------------------------- */\nstatic bool\nstep_compliance_check(disburse_stipend_saga_t *saga)\n{\n    stipend_event_t ev = {0};\n\n    saga_log_audit(saga, AUDIT_INFO, \"Running compliance checks…\");\n\n    compliance_result_t res = compliance_service_check(\n        saga->compliance,\n        &saga->request->student,\n        &saga->request->payout_details);\n\n    if (res.status != COMPLIANCE_STATUS_OK)\n    {\n        saga_log_audit(saga, AUDIT_WARN,\n                       \"Compliance check failed — reason: %s\",\n                       res.reason);\n\n        ev.type              = STIPEND_EVENT_COMPLIANCE_FAILED;\n        ev.stipend_request   = *saga->request;\n        strncpy(ev.reason, res.reason, sizeof(ev.reason) - 1);\n        saga_publish_event(saga, &ev);\n\n        errno = EACCES; /* Permission denied / non-compliant */\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    saga_log_audit(saga, AUDIT_INFO, \"Compliance passed.\");\n    ev.type            = STIPEND_EVENT_COMPLIANCE_PASSED;\n    ev.stipend_request = *saga->request;\n    saga_publish_event(saga, &ev);\n\n    saga->state = SAGA_STATE_COMPLIANCE_PASSED;\n    return true;\n}\n\n/* Compensation: no action needed for compliance                            */\nstatic bool\ncompensate_compliance(__attribute__((unused)) disburse_stipend_saga_t *saga)\n{\n    return true; /* Idempotent – nothing to undo */\n}\n\n/* ---- Step 2: Reserve Funds ---------------------------------------------- */\nstatic bool\nstep_reserve_funds(disburse_stipend_saga_t *saga)\n{\n    saga_log_audit(saga, AUDIT_INFO, \"Reserving funds…\");\n\n    payment_reservation_t reservation = {0};\n    int rc = payment_gateway_reserve(\n        saga->gateway,\n        &saga->request->payout_details,\n        &reservation);\n\n    if (rc != 0)\n    {\n        saga_log_audit(saga, AUDIT_ERROR,\n                       \"Fund reservation failed (err=%d)\", rc);\n        errno = rc;\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    /* Persist reservation token on stipend aggregate for later capture */\n    stipend_repository_attach_reservation(\n        saga->stipend_repo,\n        saga->request->stipend_id,\n        &reservation);\n\n    saga->state = SAGA_STATE_FUNDS_RESERVED;\n    return true;\n}\n\nstatic bool\ncompensate_reserve_funds(disburse_stipend_saga_t *saga)\n{\n    /* Release reservation only if it exists */\n    saga_log_audit(saga, AUDIT_INFO, \"Releasing reserved funds (rollback)…\");\n\n    /* Retrieve token back from stipend aggregate */\n    payment_reservation_t token = {0};\n    int rc = stipend_repository_get_reservation(\n        saga->stipend_repo,\n        saga->request->stipend_id,\n        &token);\n\n    if (rc != 0)\n    {\n        saga_log_audit(saga, AUDIT_ERROR,\n                       \"Failed to fetch reservation token (err=%d)\", rc);\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    rc = payment_gateway_release(saga->gateway, &token);\n    if (rc != 0)\n    {\n        saga_log_audit(saga, AUDIT_ERROR,\n                       \"Fund release failed (err=%d)\", rc);\n        return false;\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\n/* ---- Step 3: Ledger Posting --------------------------------------------- */\nstatic bool\nstep_ledger_post(disburse_stipend_saga_t *saga)\n{\n    saga_log_audit(saga, AUDIT_INFO, \"Posting ledger entry…\");\n\n    ledger_entry_t entry = {\n        .entry_id       = \"\",\n        .timestamp      = timestamp_now(),\n        .type           = LEDGER_ENTRY_STIPEND_DISBURSEMENT,\n        .amount         = saga->request->payout_details.amount,\n        .currency       = saga->request->payout_details.currency,\n        .student_id     = saga->request->student.student_id,\n        .correlation_id = saga->saga_id\n    };\n\n    uuid_generate(entry.entry_id, sizeof(entry.entry_id));\n\n    int rc = ledger_repository_append(saga->ledger_repo, &entry);\n    if (rc != 0)\n    {\n        saga_log_audit(saga, AUDIT_ERROR,\n                       \"Ledger append failed (err=%d)\", rc);\n        errno = rc;\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    saga->state = SAGA_STATE_LEDGER_POSTED;\n    return true;\n}\n\nstatic bool\ncompensate_ledger_post(disburse_stipend_saga_t *saga)\n{\n    saga_log_audit(saga, AUDIT_INFO, \"Reversing ledger entry (rollback)…\");\n\n    int rc = ledger_repository_reversal(\n        saga->ledger_repo,\n        saga->request->student.student_id,\n        saga->saga_id); /* correlation_id */\n\n    if (rc != 0)\n    {\n        saga_log_audit(saga, AUDIT_ERROR,\n                       \"Ledger reversal failed (err=%d)\", rc);\n        return false;\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\n/* ---- Step 4: Capture / Disburse Funds ------------------------------------ */\nstatic bool\nstep_capture_funds(disburse_stipend_saga_t *saga)\n{\n    saga_log_audit(saga, AUDIT_INFO,\n                   \"Capturing reserved funds to student account…\");\n\n    payment_reservation_t token = {0};\n    int rc = stipend_repository_get_reservation(\n        saga->stipend_repo,\n        saga->request->stipend_id,\n        &token);\n\n rc;\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    saga->state = SAGA_STATE_DISBURSED;\n    return true;\n}\n\nstatic bool\ncompensate_capture_funds(disburse_stipend_saga_t *saga)\n{\n    /* Attempt refund only if capture was successful */\n    saga_log_audit(saga, AUDIT_INFO,\n                   \"Refunding captured funds (rollback)…\");\n\n    payment_reservation_t token = {0};\n    int rc = stipend_repository_get_reservation(\n        saga->stipend_repo,\n        saga->request->stipend_id,\n        &token);\n\n    if (rc != 0)\n    {\n        saga_log_audit(saga, AUDIT_ERROR,\n                       \"Reservation token missing for refund (err=%d)\", rc);\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    rc = payment_gateway_refund(saga->gateway, &token);\n    if (rc != 0)\n    {\n        saga_log_audit(saga, AUDIT_ERROR,\n                       \"Refund failed (err=%d)\", rc);\n        return false;\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*  Saga Steps Array                                                          */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\nstatic saga_step_t g_saga_steps[] = {\n    { \"COMPLIANCE_CHECK\",      step_compliance_check,  compensate_compliance   },\n    { \"RESERVE_FUNDS\",         step_reserve_funds,     compensate_reserve_funds},\n    { \"LEDGER_POST\",           step_ledger_post,       compensate_ledger_post },\n    { \"CAPTURE_FUNDS\",         step_capture_funds,     compensate_capture_funds}\n};\n\n#define SAGA_TOTAL_STEPS (sizeof(g_saga_steps) / sizeof(g_saga_steps[0]))\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*  Public API                                                                */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n/**\n * disburse_stipend_run\n *\n * Execute the Disburse Stipend Saga for a given stipend request.\n *\n * return: 0 on success, or a negative errno code on failure.\n */\nint\ndisburse_stipend_run(\n    const stipend_request_t  *request,\n    compliance_service_t     *compliance,\n    payment_gateway_t        *gateway,\n    ledger_repository_t      *ledger_repo,\n    stipend_repository_t     *stipend_repo,\n    event_bus_t              *event_bus,\n    audit_logger_t           *audit,\n    retry_policy_t            retry_policy)\n{\n    if (!request || !compliance || !gateway || !ledger_repo ||\n        !stipend_repo || !event_bus || !audit)\n    {\n        return EINVAL;\n    }\n\n    /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */\n    /*  Instantiate saga context                                           */\n    /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */\n    disburse_stipend_saga_t saga = {\n        .compliance   = compliance,\n        .gateway      = gateway,\n        .ledger_repo  = ledger_repo,\n        .stipend_repo = stipend_repo,\n        .event_bus    = event_bus,\n        .audit        = audit,\n        .state        = SAGA_STATE_NOT_STARTED,\n        .retry_policy = retry_policy,\n        .request      = request,\n        .steps        = g_saga_steps,\n        .step_count   = SAGA_TOTAL_STEPS,\n        .started_at   = timestamp_now()\n    };\n    uuid_generate(saga.saga_id, sizeof(saga.saga_id));\n\n    saga_log_audit(&saga, AUDIT_INFO,\n                   \"Saga %s (%s) started for Stipend #%s.\",\n                   saga.saga_id, SAGA_VERSION, request->stipend_id);\n\n    /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */\n    /*  Forward execution                                                 */\n    /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */\n    ssize_t current_step = 0;\n    for (; current_step < (ssize_t)saga.step_count; ++current_step)\n    {\n        const saga_step_t *step = &saga.steps[current_step];\n        size_t attempt = 0;\n\n        saga_log_audit(&saga, AUDIT_DEBUG, \"→ Executing step: %s\", step->name);\n\n        while (attempt <= saga.retry_policy.max_retries)\n        {\n            if (step->forward(&saga))\n            {\n                /* Success */\n                break;\n            }\n\n            attempt++;\n            if (attempt > saga.retry_policy.max_retries)\n            {\n                /* Abort forward flow */\n                saga_log_audit(&saga, AUDIT_WARN,\n                               \"Step %s exhausted retry budget.\", step->name);\n                goto rollback; /* Begin compensation */\n            }\n\n            /* Wait before retry */\n            uint32_t backoff_ms = retry_policy_backoff(&saga.retry_policy, attempt);\n            saga_log_audit(&saga, AUDIT_INFO,\n                           \"Retrying step %s in %ums (attempt %zu)…\",\n                           step->name, backoff_ms, attempt);\n\n            retry_policy_sleep(backoff_ms);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */\n    /*  Completed successfully                                            */\n    /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */\n    saga.state = SAGA_STATE_COMPLETED;\n    saga_log_audit(&saga, AUDIT_INFO,\n                   \"Saga %s completed successfully.\", saga.saga_id);\n\n    stipend_event_t ev = {\n        .type            = STIPEND_EVENT_DISBURSED,\n        .stipend_request = *request\n    };\n    saga_publish_event(&saga, &ev);\n    return 0;\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*  Compensation / Rollback Flow                                              */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\nrollback:\n    saga.state = SAGA_STATE_ROLLING_BACK;\n    saga_log_audit(&saga, AUDIT_WARN,\n                   \"Initiating rollback of Saga %s…\", saga.saga_id);\n\n    for (ssize_t i = current_step; i >= 0; --i)\n    {\n        const saga_step_t *step = &saga.steps[i];\n        saga_log_audit(&saga, AUDIT_DEBUG,\n                       \"↩ Compensating step: %s\", step->name);\n\n        if (!step->compensate(&saga))\n        {\n            saga_log_audit(&saga, AUDIT_ERROR,\n                           \"Compensation for step %s failed.\", step->name);\n            /* Compensation failures are logged, but saga continues */\n        }\n    }\n\n    saga.state = SAGA_STATE_FAILED;\n\n    stipend_event_t failed_ev = {\n        .type            = STIPEND_EVENT_DISBURSEMENT_FAILED,\n        .stipend_request = *request\n    };\n    strncpy(failed_ev.reason, \"Saga rollback completed\", sizeof(failed_ev.reason) - 1);\n    saga_publish_event(&saga, &failed_ev);\n\n    saga_log_audit(&saga, AUDIT_WARN,\n                   \"Saga %s rolled back. Stipend disbursement aborted.\",\n                   saga.saga_id);\n\n    /* Preserve original errno set by failing step */\n    return errno ? errno : ECANCELED;\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*  End of file                                                               */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n","numLines":521,"startLine":1,"totalLines":521}}}

             \\\n   159→    if ((cb) == NULL) {                                                     \\\n   160→        if (err_out) {                                                      \\\n   161→ 183→        const stipend_disbursement_callbacks_t *cb,\n   184→        saga_error_t                           *err_out)\n   185→{\n   186→    /* Defensive programming: never trust caller. */\n   187→ 198→\n   199→    /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */\n   200→    /* 1. The code sits in the\n *   Use-Case layer (Clean Architecture) and has zero dependencies on any\n *   concrete frameworks, I/O, or persistence technology.  All side-effects\n *   (ledger writes, payment network calls, etc.) are injected via function\n *   pointers, making the saga deterministic and unit-testable.\n *\n *   ┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n *   │  FinancialAid::DisburseStipendSaga                       │\n *   │-----------------------------------------------------------│\n *   │ validate_eligibility()   ─┐                               │\n *   │ reserve_budget()          ├─► initiate_transfer() ─┐      │\n *   │ record_ledger()           │                         │      │\n *   │ publish_event()           │                         │      │\n *   └───────────────────────────┘                         │      │\n *                                │                        │      │\n *                                ▼                        │      │\n *                         compensation() ◄────────────────┘      │\n *     (release_budget, reverse_transfer, ledger_reversal)        │\n *   └───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n *\n *   NOTE:\n *     1.  Header-only so that professors can #include it in toy\n *         command-line drivers or embed it in micro-services without\n *         tweaking build systems.\n *     2.  Thread-safe: no global / static mutable state.\n *     3.  Compatible with C89+ (but uses stdint.h & stdbool.h).\n */\n\n#ifndef EDUPAY_LEDGER_ACADEMY_FINANCIAL_AID_DISBURSE_STIPEND_SAGA_H\n#define EDUPAY_LEDGER_ACADEMY_FINANCIAL_AID_DISBURSE_STIPEND_SAGA_H\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*  Standard Library Includes                                                 */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n#include <stdio.h>\n#include <stdlib.h>\n#include <stdint.h>\n#include <stdbool.h>\n#include <time.h>\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*  Domain Types                                                              */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n/*\n * ISO-4217 three-letter currency code.  Stored as a fixed-width char[4]\n * where index 3 must be '\\0'.\n */\ntypedef char iso_currency_code_t[4];\n\n/* Forward-declaration for opaque correlation identifiers (UUID, ULID, etc.). */\ntypedef struct {\n    uint8_t bytes[16];\n} correlation_id_t;\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*  Request DTO                                                               */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\ntypedef struct {\n    uint64_t         student_id;\n    uint64_t         stipend_cents;\n    iso_currency_code_t currency;\n    char             term[8];         /* e.g., \"2024SP\" */\n    correlation_id_t correlation_id;\n    time_t           requested_at_utc;\n} stipend_disbursement_request_t;\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*  Error Handling                                                            */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\ntypedef enum {\n    SAGA_OK = 0,\n    SAGA_ERR_VALIDATION     =  1,\n    SAGA_ERR_INSUFFICIENT   =  2,\n    SAGA_ERR_TRANSFER_FAIL  =  3,\n    SAGA_ERR_LEDGER_FAIL    =  4,\n    SAGA_ERR_EVENT_BUS      =  5,\n    SAGA_ERR_COMPENSATION   =  6,\n    SAGA_ERR_INTERNAL       = 99\n} saga_error_code_t;\n\ntypedef struct {\n    saga_error_code_t code;\n    char              message[128]; /* ASCII diagnostics. */\n} saga_error_t;\n\n/* Convenience macro for initializing an empty saga_error_t */\n#define SAGA_ERROR_INIT() (saga_error_t){ .code = SAGA_OK, .message = \"\" }\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*  Saga Orchestration – Callback VTable                                      */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*\n *  The saga never performs I/O directly.  All side-effects are routed through\n *  a set of callbacks supplied by the calling application/service.  Each\n *  callback must be idempotent because the saga might retry crashed steps.\n *\n *  Every callback returns `true` on success.  On failure it returns `false`\n *  and must populate `err_out`.\n */\n\ntypedef bool (*validate_eligibility_fn)(\n        const stipend_disbursement_request_t *req,\n        saga_error_t                         *err_out);\n\ntypedef bool (*reserve_budget_fn)(\n        const stipend_disbursement_request_t *req,\n        uint64_t                             *reservation_id_out,\n        saga_error_t                         *err_out);\n\ntypedef bool (*release_budget_fn)(\n        uint64_t       reservation_id,\n        saga_error_t  *err_out);\n\ntypedef bool (*initiate_transfer_fn)(\n        const stipend_disbursement_request_t *req,\n        uint64_t                             *network_tx_id_out,\n        saga_error_t                         *err_out);\n\ntypedef bool (*reverse_transfer_fn)(\n        uint64_t       network_tx_id,\n        saga_error_t  *err_out);\n\ntypedef bool (*record_ledger_entry_fn)(\n        const stipend_disbursement_request_t *req,\n        const char                           *entry_type,\n        uint64_t                              related_id, /* reservation or tx id */\n        saga_error_t                         *err_out);\n\ntypedef bool (*publish_domain_event_fn)(\n        const stipend_disbursement_request_t *req,\n        const char                           *event_name,\n        saga_error_t                         *err_out);\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*  VTable Container                                                          */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\ntypedef struct {\n    validate_eligibility_fn   validate_eligibility;\n    reserve_budget_fn         reserve_budget;\n    release_budget_fn         release_budget;\n    initiate_transfer_fn      initiate_transfer;\n    reverse_transfer_fn       reverse_transfer;\n    record_ledger_entry_fn    record_ledger_entry;\n    publish_domain_event_fn   publish_domain_event;\n} stipend_disbursement_callbacks_t;\n\n/* Utility macro to check if a pointer in the vtable is missing at runtime. */\n#define _CHECK_CB(cb) do {                                                  \\\n    if ((cb) == NULL) {                                                     \\\n        if (err_out) {                                                      \\\n            err_out->code = SAGA_ERR_INTERNAL;                              \\\n            snprintf(err_out->message, sizeof(err_out->message),            \\\n                     \"Callback missing: %s\", #cb);                          \\\n        }                                                                   \\\n        return false;                                                       \\\n    } } while (0)\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*  Saga Public API                                                           */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\nstatic inline\nbool disburse_stipend_run(\n        const stipend_disbursement_request_t   *req,\n        const stipend_disbursement_callbacks_t *cb,\n        saga_error_t                           *err_out);\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*  Implementation                                                            */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\nstatic inline\nbool disburse_stipend_run(\n        const stipend_disbursement_request_t   *req,\n        const stipend_disbursement_callbacks_t *cb,\n        saga_error_t                           *err_out)\n{\n    /* Defensive programming: never trust caller. */\n    if (!req || !cb) {\n        if (err_out) {\n            err_out->code = SAGA_ERR_INTERNAL;\n            snprintf(err_out->message, sizeof(err_out->message),\n                     \"NULL argument(s) received\");\n        }\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    uint64_t reservation_id = 0;\n    uint64_t network_tx_id  = 0;\n\n    /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */\n    /* 1. Validate eligibility                                            */\n    /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */\n    _CHECK_CB(cb->validate_eligibility);\n    if (!cb->validate_eligibility(req, err_out)) {\n        return false; /* validation errors are final, no compensation needed */\n    }\n\n    /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */\n    /* 2. Reserve budget (Financial-Aid allocation ledger)                */\n    /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */\n    _CHECK_CB(cb->reserve_budget);\n    if (!cb->reserve_budget(req, &reservation_id, err_out)) {\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */\n    /* 3. Initiate transfer via payment network                           */\n    /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */\n    _CHECK_CB(cb->initiate_transfer);\n    if (!cb->initiate_transfer(req, &network_tx_id, err_out)) {\n\n        /* ------------------------------------------------------------------\n         * Compensation Path #1: release the budget reservation\n         * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */\n        _CHECK_CB(cb->release_budget);\n        saga_error_t cmp_err = SAGA_ERROR_INIT();\n        if (!cb->release_budget(reservation_id, &cmp_err)) {\n            /* Fatal: both forward step + compensation failed */\n            if (err_out && err_out->code == SAGA_OK) {\n                *err_out = cmp_err;\n            }\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        return false; /* original error already populated by initiate_transfer */\n    }\n\n    /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */\n    /* 4. Record ledger entry (financial book-keeping)                    */\n    /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */\n    _CHECK_CB(cb->record_ledger_entry);\n    if (!cb->record_ledger_entry(req, \"STIPEND_DISBURSEMENT\", network_tx_id, err_out)) {\n\n        /* Compensation Path #2: reverse transfer + release budget */\n        _CHECK_CB(cb->reverse_transfer);\n        _CHECK_CB(cb->release_budget);\n\n        saga_error_t cmp_err1 = SAGA_ERROR_INIT();\n        if (!cb->reverse_transfer(network_tx_id, &cmp_err1)) {\n            if (err_out && err_out->code == SAGA_OK) *err_out = cmp_err1;\n            /* Deliberately fall through to attempt second compensation */\n        }\n\n        saga_error_t cmp_err2 = SAGA_ERROR_INIT();\n        if (!cb->release_budget(reservation_id, &cmp_err2)) {\n            if (err_out && err_out->code == SAGA_OK) *err_out = cmp_err2;\n        }\n\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */\n    /* 5. Publish domain event (CQRS / Integration-Event)                 */\n    /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */\n    _CHECK_CB(cb->publish_domain_event);\n    if (!cb->publish_domain_event(req, \"StipendDisbursed\", err_out)) {\n\n        /* Compensation Path #3: ledger reversal + reverse transfer + release budget */\n        _CHECK_CB(cb->record_ledger_entry);\n        _CHECK_CB(cb->reverse_transfer);\n        _CHECK_CB(cb->release_budget);\n\n        saga_error_t cmp_err = SAGA_ERROR_INIT();\n        cb->record_ledger_entry(req, \"STIPEND_DISBURSEMENT_REVERSED\", network_tx_id, &cmp_err);\n        cb->reverse_transfer(network_tx_id, &cmp_err);\n        cb->release_budget(reservation_id, &cmp_err);\n\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */\n    /* 6. Success!                                                        */\n    /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */\n    if (err_out) {\n        *err_out = SAGA_ERROR_INIT(); /* reset to success */\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*  Example: Default No-Op Callbacks for Unit-Testing                         */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n#ifdef EDUPAY_STIPEND_SAGA_ENABLE_NOOP_CALLBACKS\n\n#include <string.h>\n\n/* These compile-time helpers allow professors to run the saga in a REPL\n * without wiring external systems.  Each callback prints to stdout and\n * pretends to succeed.\n */\n\nstatic inline bool noop_validate(const stipend_disbursement_request_t *req, saga_error_t *err) {\n    (void)req; (void)err;\n    puts(\"[noop] validate_eligibility\");\n    return true;\n}\n\nstatic inline bool noop_reserve(const stipend_disbursement_request_t *req,\n                                uint64_t *id, saga_error_t *err) {\n    (void)req; (void)err;\n    puts(\"[noop] reserve_budget\");\n    *id = 42;\n    return true;\n}\n\nstatic inline bool noop_release(uint64_t id, saga_error_t *err) {\n    (void)id; (void)err;\n    puts(\"[noop] release_budget\");\n    return true;\n}\n\nstatic inline bool noop_transfer(const stipend_disbursement_request_t *req,\n                                 uint64_t *id, saga_error_t *err) {\n    (void)req; (void)err;\n    puts(\"[noop] initiate_transfer\");\n    *id = 1337;\n    return true;\n}\n\nstatic inline bool noop_reverse(uint64_t id, saga_error_t *err) {\n    (void)id; (void)err;\n    puts(\"[noop] reverse_transfer\");\n    return true;\n}\n\nstatic inline bool noop_ledger(const stipend_disbursement_request_t *req,\n                               const char *type, uint64_t rel_id, saga_error_t *err) {\n    (void)req; (void)type; (void)rel_id; (void)err;\n    puts(\"[noop] record_ledger_entry\");\n    return true;\n}\n\nstatic inline bool noop_publish(const stipend_disbursement_request_t *req,\n                                const char *name, saga_error_t *err) {\n    (void)req; (void)name; (void)err;\n    puts(\"[noop] publish_domain_event\");\n    return true;\n}\n\nstatic const stipend_disbursement_callbacks_t STIPEND_SAGA_NOOP_CALLBACKS = {\n    .validate_eligibility = noop_validate,\n    .reserve_budget       = noop_reserve,\n    .release_budget       = noop_release,\n    .initiate_transfer    = noop_transfer,\n    .reverse_transfer     = noop_reverse,\n    .record_ledger_entry  = noop_ledger,\n    .publish_domain_event = noop_publish\n};\n\n#endif /* EDUPAY_STIPEND_SAGA_ENABLE_NOOP_CALLBACKS */\n\n#endif /* EDUPAY_LEDGER_ACADEMY_FINANCIAL_AID_DISBURSE_STIPEND_SAGA_H */\n","numLines":362,"startLine":1,"totalLines":362}}}

This guarantees\n * that students can swap infrastructure while the core business rules remain\n * stable—a key property of Robert C. Martin’s Clean Architecture.\n *\n * All monetary calculations use fixed-precision integers (minor units) to avoid\n * floating-point rounding errors.  ISO-4217 alpha-3 currency codes are used\n * for multi-currency support.\n *\n * Conventions\n * -----------\n * • All functions return 0 on success, non-zero `ledger_err_t` codes on failure.  \n * • “Immutable” means _append-only_; once an entry is POSTED it can only be\n *   compensated by a REVERSAL entry—never mutated in place.  \n * • Thread-safety: read-only operations are lock-free; mutating operations\n *   require the caller to provide a concurrency control strategy (e.g. external\n *   mutex, optimistic CAS or sequential command bus).\n */\n\n#ifndef     = 7,  /* Cannot void posted entry            */\n    LEDGER_EALREADY_POSTED           = 8,  /* Entry already posted                */\n    LEDGER_ENOT_FOUND                = 9,  /* Entry not found money_t                amount;          /* Always positive; sign is in type   */\n    char                   description[256];\n\n    /* Correlation with upstream commands or external systems */\n                      const uuid_t  *original_id,\n                                  uuid_t        *out_reversal_id);\n\n/**\n * @brief Lookup a ledger entry by UUID.\n *\n * @param[in]   ledger null)\n */\nvoid uuid_format(const uuid_t *uuid, char strbuf[37]);\n\n/**\n * @brief Generate cryptographically secure random UUID (v4).\n */\nledger_err_t uuid_generate(uuid_t snprintf(pub->last_diag.broker_reply,\n                     sizeof(pub->last_diag.broker_reply),\n                     \"CHAN_CLOSE: code=%u, text=%.*s\",\n                     m->reply_code,\n sizeof(pub->last_diag.broker_reply),\n                     \"SERVER_EXCEPTION id=%u\",\n                     reply.reply.id);\n        }\n    } else if (reply.reply_type == The projector consumes\n *   immutable domain events from the event-bus and updates aggregated KPI’s\n *   such as total tuition collected, payment counts, pending settlements, and\n *   open fraud alerts.  The component is written in C following Clean\n *   Architecture guidelines so that higher layers (REST, gRPC, GraphQL, etc.)\n *   can query the materialized view without coupling to the write-side or to\n *   any framework.\n *\n *   The projection state is persisted to a pluggable store; a lightweight\n *   POSIX-file implementation is provided for coursework convenience but can be\n *   replaced with SQLite, Redis, or cloud storage by swapping the v-table.\n *\n *   Thread-safety is guaranteed via a coarse mutex because projections are\n *   write-heavy and latency-tolerant relative to OLTP processing.\n *\n * Author: EduPay Core Engineering Team\n */\n\n#include <errno.h>\n#include <inttypes.h>\n#include <pthread.h>\n#include <stddef.h>\n#include <stdio.h>\n#include <stdlib.h>\n#include <string.h>\n#include <time.h>\n#include <unistd.h>\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/*                                   Macros                                  */\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\n#ifndef DASHBOARD_PROJECTOR_LOG\n#define DASHBOARD_PROJECTOR_LOG(fmt, ...)                                                   \\\n    do {                                                                                    \\\n        fprintf(stderr, \"[DASHBOARD_PROJECTOR] %s:%d: \" fmt \"\\n\", __FILE__, __LINE__,       \\\n                ##__VA_ARGS__);                                                             \\\n    } while (0)\n#endif\n\n#define FILE_MAGIC \"EDUPAY_DASHBD_V1\" /* 16 bytes incl. Only the subset consumed by\n * this projection is modeled below to keep the file self-contained.\n */\n\ntypedef enum {\n    EVENT_PAYMENT_INITIATED = 1,\n    EVENT_PAYMENT_SETTLED   = 2,\n            double amount;\n        } payment_initiated;\n\n        struct {\n            double amount;\n            int    success; /* 1 = settled, 0 = failed/reversed */\n        } uint64_t fraud_alerts;\n    uint64_t last_sequence_number;\n} dashboard_snapshot_t;\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/*                            Store Interface                                */\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\ntypedef struct projection_store {\n    int (*save)(struct projection_store *self,\n                const dashboard_snapshot_t *snapshot);\n    int (*load)(struct projection_store *self, dashboard_snapshot_t *snapshot);\n    void (*destroy)(struct projection_store *self);\n} projection_store_t;\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/*                       File-based Store (default)                          */\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\ntypedef struct {\n    projection_store_t iface;\n    char               filepath[256];\n} file_projection_store_t;\n\nstatic int file_store_save(projection_store_t *base,\n                           const dashboard_snapshot_t *snapshot) {\n    file_projection_store_t *self = (file_projection_store_t *)base;\n    FILE *                   fp   = fopen(self->filepath, \"wb\");\n    if (!fp) {\n        DASHBOARD_PROJECTOR_LOG(\"Failed to open snapshot file for writing: %s\",\n                                strerror(errno));\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    /* Write header so that older snapshots are rejected on format upgrade */\n    if (fwrite(FILE_MAGIC, 1, sizeof(FILE_MAGIC), fp) != sizeof(FILE_MAGIC)) {\n        DASHBOARD_PROJECTOR_LOG(\"Failed to write file magic\");\n        fclose(fp);\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    if (fwrite(snapshot, sizeof(*snapshot), 1, fp) != 1) {\n        DASHBOARD_PROJECTOR_LOG(\"Failed to write snapshot\");\n        fclose(fp);\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    fflush(fp);\n    fclose(fp);\n    return 0;\n}\n\nstatic int file_store_load(projection_store_t *base,\n                           dashboard_snapshot_t *snapshot) {\n    file_projection_store_t *self = (file_projection_store_t *)base;\n    FILE *                   fp   = fopen(self->filepath, \"rb\");\n    if (!fp) {\n        if (errno != ENOENT) {\n            DASHBOARD_PROJECTOR_LOG(\"Failed to open snapshot file for reading: \"\n                                    \"%s\",\n                                    strerror(errno));\n            return -1;\n        }\n        /* File does not exist: treat as fresh start. */\n        memset(snapshot, 0, sizeof(*snapshot));\n        return 0;\n    }\n\n    char magic[sizeof(FILE_MAGIC)];\n    if (fread(magic, 1, sizeof(magic), fp) != sizeof(magic) ||\n        memcmp(magic, FILE_MAGIC, sizeof(FILE_MAGIC)) != 0) {\n        DASHBOARD_PROJECTOR_LOG(\"Snapshot file missing magic header – ignoring\");\n        fclose(fp);\n        memset(snapshot, 0, sizeof(*snapshot));\n        return 0;\n    }\n\n    if (fread(snapshot, sizeof(*snapshot), 1, fp) != 1) {\n        DASHBOARD_PROJECTOR_LOG(\"Corrupted snapshot – starting from scratch\");\n        memset(snapshot, 0, sizeof(*snapshot));\n        fclose(fp);\n        return 0;\n    }\n\n    fclose(fp);\n    return 0;\n}\n\nstatic void file_store_destroy(projection_store_t *base) {\n    free(base);\n}\n\nstatic projection_store_t *file_store_create(const char *filepath) {\n    file_projection_store_t *self = calloc(1, sizeof(*self));\n    if (!self) {\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    snprintf(self->filepath, sizeof(self->filepath), \"%s\",\n             filepath ? filepath : DEFAULT_SNAPSHOT_FILE);\n\n    self->iface.save    = file_store_save;\n    self->iface.load    = file_store_load;\n    self->iface.destroy = file_store_destroy;\n    return &self->iface;\n}\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/*                           Projector Context                                */\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\ntypedef struct {\n    dashboard_snapshot_t state;\n    projection_store_t  *store;\n    pthread_mutex_t      mtx;\n} dashboard_projector_t;\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/*                           Public Functions                                 */\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\n/*\n * projector_create\n *   Allocates a new projector and hydrates its state from the store.\n *\n *   Returns NULL on allocation or persistence failure.\n */\ndashboard_projector_t *\ndashboard_projector_create(projection_store_t *store) {\n    if (!store) {\n        store = file_store_create(NULL);\n        if (!store) {\n            return NULL;\n        }\n    }\n\n    dashboard_projector_t *proj = calloc(1, sizeof(*proj));\n    if (!proj) {\n        store->destroy(store);\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    proj->store = store;\n    pthread_mutex_init(&proj->mtx, NULL);\n\n    /* Load existing snapshot, if any */\n    if (store->load(store, &proj->state) != 0) {\n        DASHBOARD_PROJECTOR_LOG(\"Failed to load snapshot – aborting\");\n        pthread_mutex_destroy(&proj->mtx);\n        store->destroy(store);\n        free(proj);\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    return proj;\n}\n\n/*\n * projector_destroy\n *   Persists the final state and frees resources.\n */\nvoid dashboard_projector_destroy(dashboard_projector_t *proj) {\n    if (!proj) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&proj->mtx);\n    proj->store->save(proj->store, &proj->state);\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&proj->mtx);\n\n    proj->store->destroy(proj->store);\n    pthread_mutex_destroy(&proj->mtx);\n    free(proj);\n}\n\n/*\n * dashboard_projector_snapshot\n *   Returns a copy of the current read-model for external queries.\n *   Caller owns the return value.\n */\ndashboard_snapshot_t\ndashboard_projector_snapshot(dashboard_projector_t *proj) {\n    dashboard_snapshot_t copy;\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&proj->mtx);\n    memcpy(&copy, &proj->state, sizeof(copy));\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&proj->mtx);\n    return copy;\n}\n\n/*\n * dashboard_projector_handle\n *   Consumes a domain event and mutates the projection atomically.\n */\nint dashboard_projector_handle(dashboard_projector_t *proj,\n                               const domain_event_t *event) {\n    if (!proj || !event) {\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&proj->mtx);\n\n    /* Ensure idempotency */\n    if (event->sequence_number <= proj->state.last_sequence_number) {\n        pthread_mutex_unlock(&proj->mtx);\n        return 0;\n    }\n\n    switch (event->type) {\n    case EVENT_PAYMENT_INITIATED:\n        proj->state.total_payments++;\n        proj->state.pending_payments++;\n        break;\n\n    case EVENT_PAYMENT_SETTLED:\n        if (proj->state.pending_payments > 0) {\n            proj->state.pending_payments--;\n        }\n        if (event->data.payment_settled.success) {\n            proj->state.total_tuition +=\n                event->data.payment_settled.amount;\n        }\n        break;\n\n    case EVENT_FRAUD_ALERT:\n        proj->state.fraud_alerts++;\n        break;\n\n    default:\n        DASHBOARD_PROJECTOR_LOG(\"Unknown event type: %d\", event->type);\n        break;\n    }\n\n    proj->state.last_sequence_number = event->sequence_number;\n\n    /* Best-effort persistence; if it fails we keep working but log an error */\n    if (proj->store->save(proj->store, &proj->state) != 0) {\n        DASHBOARD_PROJECTOR_LOG(\"Failed to persist snapshot (seq=%\" PRIu64 \")\",\n                                event->sequence_number);\n    }\n\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&proj->mtx);\n    return 0;\n}\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/*                      Example Usage (unit-test style)                       */\n/*        */\n    SAGA_STEP_COMPENSATED,     /* Compensation executed after a failure       */\n    SAGA_STEP_COMPENSATION_FAILED /* Compensation itself failed              */\n} SAGA_STATE_ROLLING_BACK,\n    SAGA_STATE_COMPLETED,\n    SAGA_STATE_ABORTED\n} saga_execution_state_t;\n\n/*------------------------------ Forward Decls ------------------------------*/\nstruct Output buffer for an error description if the step fails.\n * @param reason_len Length of the provided buffer.\n *\n * @return true  if the step succeeded and the Saga may continue.\n *         false if an error occurred; compensation will be triggered.\n */\ntypedef bool (*saga_forward_cb_t)(void *step_ctx,\n                                  char *reason,\n                                  size_t When compensation fails, the Saga transitions to\n *                  SAGA_STATE_ABORTED and no further steps are attempted.\n * @param reason_len Length of the provided buffer.\n *\n * @return true  if compensation succeeded.\n *         false if compensation itself failed – requires manual intervention.\n */\ntypedef bool (*saga_compensate_cb_t)(void *step_ctx,\n                                     char *reason,\n                                     size_t reason_len);\n\n/*------------------------------ Data Structures ----------------------------*/\n/**\n * @struct saga_step_t\n * Encapsulates a single unit of work and its compensating action.\n */\ntypedef struct\n{\n    const char          *name; These interfaces live in the core domain layer so\n *    that professors can swap out messaging middleware (e.g., NATS, Kafka,\n *    AMQP) or persistence engines (e.g., PostgreSQL, DynamoDB) without touching\n *    business rules.\n *\n *  The definitions below purposely avoid leaking infrastructure concerns\n *  (threads, sockets, ORMs, etc.).  They only describe *what* a saga is and\n *  *how* steps are composed.  Concrete executors, adapters, and integration\n *  tests are implemented in the application / infrastructure layers.\n *\n *  Author: EduPay Ledger Academy Core Team\n *  SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\n * ============================================================================\n */\n\n#ifndef EDUPAY_LEDGER_ACADEMY_CORE_SAGA_DEFINITION_H\n#define EDUPAY_LEDGER_ACADEMY_CORE_SAGA_DEFINITION_H\n\n/* ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n *  Standard Library\n * ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n#include <stdint.h>\n#include <stdbool.h>\n#include <stddef.h>\n\n/* ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n *  Versioning\n * ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n#define SAGA_DEFINITION_VERSION_MAJOR 1\n#define SAGA_DEFINITION_VERSION_MINOR 0\n#define SAGA_DEFINITION_VERSION_PATCH 0\n\n/* ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n *  Constants & Configuration\n * ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\n/* Upper bound prevents memory corruption in student exercises.   */\n#ifndef SAGA_MAX_STEPS\n#define SAGA_MAX_STEPS 16U\n#endif /* SAGA_MAX_STEPS */\n\n/* Status/Return codes for saga operations.                      */\ntypedef enum\n{\n    SAGA_OK = 0,\n    SAGA_ERR_INVALID_ARG      = -1,\n    SAGA_ERR_TOO_MANY_STEPS   = -2,\n    SAGA_ERR_ALREADY_STARTED  = -3,\n    SAGA_ERR_STEP_FAILED      = -4,\n    SAGA_ERR_COMPENSATION     = -5,\n    SAGA_ERR_NOT_STARTED      = -6\n} saga_rc_t;\n\n/* ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n *  Forward Declarations\n * ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\nstruct saga_step;\nstruct saga_definition;\n\n/* ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n *  Function Pointer Types\n * ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/**\n * Each saga step is implemented as a pair of callback functions that operate\n * on an opaque, caller-supplied context.  Business rules live inside these\n * callbacks, whereas the saga engine merely orchestrates them.\n *\n * Return:  SAGA_OK (0)     – success\n *          < 0             – error code, triggers compensation\n */\ntypedef saga_rc_t (*saga_step_action_fn)(void *opaque_ctx);\n\n/* ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n *  Enumerations\n * ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\ntypedef enum\n{\n    SAGA_STEP_PENDING = 0,\n    SAGA_STEP_COMPLETED,\n    SAGA_STEP_FAILED,\n    SAGA_STEP_COMPENSATED\n} saga_step_state_t;\n\ntypedef enum\n{\n    SAGA_PENDING = 0,\n    SAGA_IN_PROGRESS,\n    SAGA_COMPLETED,\n    SAGA_ROLLING_BACK,\n    SAGA_COMPENSATED,\n    SAGA_ABORTED\n} saga_state_t;\n\n/* ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n *  Data Structures\n * ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\n/**\n * saga_step:\n *    Declarative description of a single unit of work within a saga.\n *\n * Members:\n *    id                 – unique identifier within the saga (array index).\n *    name               – human-readable identifier (for audit logs).\n *    forward_action     – executes the primary business logic.\n *    compensation_action– reverses forward_action if failure occurs.\n *    state              – runtime state tracked by saga executor.\n */\ntypedef struct saga_step\n{\n    uint8_t               id;\n    const char           *name;\n    saga_step_action_fn   forward_action;\n    saga_step_action_fn   compensation_action;\n    saga_step_state_t     state;\n} saga_step_t;\n\n/**\n * saga_definition:\n *    Immutable blueprint for constructing a saga at runtime.  A concrete\n *    executor copies this definition into a mutable saga_instance that tracks\n *    states, timing, and metrics.\n *\n * Members:\n *    saga_name          – business-level identifier (e.g., \"TuitionPayment\").\n *    version            – semantic versioning so code & DB migrations align.\n *    steps              – fixed-size array of steps.\n *    step_count         – number of valid steps stored in ::steps.\n *\n *    NOTE:  The struct is intentionally POD-style so that it can be serialized\n *           into JSON/YAML/TOML for interactive labs or persisted in the\n *           Audit Trail event stream without custom marshallers.\n */\ntypedef struct saga_definition\n{\n    const char    *saga_name;\n    struct\n    {\n        uint16_t major;\n        uint16_t minor;\n        uint16_t patch;\n    } version;\n    saga_step_t   steps[SAGA_MAX_STEPS];\n    uint8_t       step_count;\n} saga_definition_t;\n\n/* ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n *  API – Compile-Time Construction Helpers\n * ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\n/**\n * SAGA_DEF_INIT:\n *    Macro to create an empty saga_definition_t literal at compile time.\n */\n#define SAGA_DEF_INIT(NAME)                      \\\n    {                                            \\\n        .saga_name  = NAME,                      \\\n        .version    = {                          \\\n            .major = SAGA_DEFINITION_VERSION_MAJOR, \\\n            .minor = SAGA_DEFINITION_VERSION_MINOR, \\\n            .patch = SAGA_DEFINITION_VERSION_PATCH  \\\n        },                                       \\\n        .steps      = {0},                       \\\n        .step_count = 0                          \\\n    }\n\n/**\n * saga_def_add_step:\n *    Adds a step to a saga_definition.  Safe for compile-time or run-time use.\n *\n * Parameters:\n *    def                 – pointer to saga_definition_t.\n *    step_name           – constant string, must outlive saga_definition_t.\n *    forward_fn          – required forward action.\n *    compensation_fn     – optional compensation (can be NULL).\n *\n * Returns:\n *    SAGA_OK on success, otherwise negative error code.\n */\nstatic inline saga_rc_t\nsaga_def_add_step(saga_definition_t     *def,\n                  const char            *step_name,\n                  saga_step_action_fn    forward_fn,\n                  saga_step_action_fn    compensation_fn)\n{\n    if (def == NULL || step_name == NULL || forward_fn == NULL)\n        return SAGA_ERR_INVALID_ARG;\n\n    if (def->step_count >= SAGA_MAX_STEPS)\n        return SAGA_ERR_TOO_MANY_STEPS;\n\n    uint8_t idx = def->step_count;\n\n    def->steps[idx].id                  = idx;\n    def->steps[idx].name                = step_name;\n    def->steps[idx].forward_action      = forward_fn;\n    def->steps[idx].compensation_action = compensation_fn;\n    def->steps[idx].state               = SAGA_STEP_PENDING;\n\n    def->step_count++;\n    return SAGA_OK;\n}\n\n/* ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n *  API – Introspection Utilities\n * ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\n/**\n * saga_def_get_step:\n *    Retrieves a mutable pointer to a step by ID.\n */\nstatic inline saga_step_t *\nsaga_def_get_step(saga_definition_t *def, uint8_t id)\n{\n    if (def == NULL || id >= def->step_count)\n        return NULL;\n    return &def->steps[id];\n}\n\n/**\n * saga_def_find_step_by_name:\n *    Linear search is adequate because compile-time bound is small\n *    (≤ SAGA_MAX_STEPS).  Students can refactor to hashmap for bonus credit.\n */\nstatic inline saga_step_t *\nsaga_def_find_step_by_name(saga_definition_t *def, const char *name)\n{\n    if (def == NULL || name == NULL)\n        return NULL;\n\n    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < def->step_count; i++)\n    {\n        if (def->steps[i].name && (strcmp(def->steps[i].name, name) == 0))\n            return &def->steps[i];\n    }\n    return NULL;\n}\n\n/* ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n *  API – Validation\n * ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\n/**\n * saga_def_validate:\n *    Performs a lightweight, synchronous verification that the saga\n *    definition is internally consistent before handing it off to an\n *    asynchronous executor.\n *\n * Returns:\n *    SAGA_OK              – definition passes sanity checks\n *    SAGA_ERR_INVALID_ARG – null pointer or missing callbacks\n */\nstatic inline saga_rc_t\nsaga_def_validate(const saga_definition_t *def)\n{\n    if (def == NULL || def->step_count == 0)\n        return SAGA_ERR_INVALID_ARG;\n\n    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < def->step_count; i++)\n    {\n        const saga_step_t *s = &def->steps[i];\n        if (s->forward_action == NULL)\n            return SAGA_ERR_INVALID_ARG;\n        /* Compensation function may be NULL (irreversible step). */\n    }\n    return SAGA_OK;\n}\n\n/* ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n *  Experimental Feature Flags\n * ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n#ifdef EDUPAY_SAGA_ENABLE_TRACE_IDS\n#include <uuid/uuid.h>\n\n/**\n * saga_trace_id_t:\n *    128-bit identifier used for distributed tracing in labs that integrate\n *    OpenTelemetry.  Defined under a feature flag so core business rules\n *    remain dependency-free unless professors opt-in.\n */\ntypedef struct\n{\n    uint8_t bytes[16];\n} saga_trace_id_t;\n#endif /* EDUPAY_SAGA_ENABLE_TRACE_IDS */\n\n#endif /* EDUPAY_LEDGER_ACADEMY_CORE_SAGA_DEFINITION_H */\n```","numLines":296,"startLine":1,"totalLines":296}}}
