The feature automatically applies a student's newly disbursed financial aid stipend towards their outstanding tuition balance, provided the student has explicitly opted in. This proposal adheres to the platform's existing architectural patterns: Clean Architecture, CQRS with Event Sourcing, and the Saga Pattern for distributed transaction management.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Interaction Diagram / Sequence\n\n### 1.1 High-Level Flow\n\n```\n┌─────────────────────┐    ┌─────────────────────┐    ┌─────────────────────┐    ┌─────────────────────┐\n│ financial_aid_srv   │    │   Saga Coordinator  │    │  projections_srv    │    │   bursar_service    │\n│ (DisburseStipend)   │    │                     │    │  (Read Models)      │    │  (Ledger/Account)   │\n└─────────┬───────────┘    └──────────┬──────────┘    └──────────┬──────────┘    └──────────┬──────────┘\n          │                           │                          │                          │\n          │ 1. Execute DisburseStipendSaga                       │                          │\n          ├──────────────────────────►│                          │                          │\n          │                           │                          │                          │\n          │ 2. Steps 1-4 Complete     │                          │                          │\n          │   (Compliance, Reserve,   │                          │                          │\n          │    Ledger Post, Capture)  │                          │                          │\n          │                           │                          │                          │\n          │                           │ 3. Query: Check Auto-Payment Eligibility            │\n          │                           │   (student_consent + outstanding_balance)           │\n          │                           ├─────────────────────────►│                          │\n          │                           │                          │                          │\n          │                           │◄─────────────────────────┤                          │\n          │                           │ 4. Response: {opt_in: true, balance: $5000}         │\n          │                           │                          │                          │\n          │                           │ 5. Command: ApplyStipendToTuition                   │\n          │                           ├──────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────►│\n          │                           │                          │                          │\n          │                           │                          │ 6. Process Payment       │\n          │                           │                          │    (Credit Account,      │\n          │                           │                          │     Post to Ledger)      │\n          │                           │                          │                          │\n          │                           │◄─────────────────────────┼──────────────────────────┤\n          │                           │ 7. Event: StipendAppliedToTuition                   │\n          │                           │                          │                          │\n          │                           │──────────────────────────┼──────────────────────────►\n          │                           │ 8. Publish to EventBus (RabbitMQ/NATS)              │\n          │                           │                          │                          │\n          │                           │                          │◄─────────────────────────┤\n          │                           │                          │ 9. Update Projections    │\n          │                           │                          │    (Dashboard, Balances) │\n          │                           │                          │                          │\n          │ 10. Saga COMPLETED        │                          │                          │\n          │◄──────────────────────────┤                          │                          │\n          │                           │                          │                          │\n```\n\n### 1.2 Detailed Step-by-Step Flow\n\n| Step | Actor | Action | Description |\n|------|-------|--------|-------------|\n| 1 | `financial_aid_service` | Initiates `DisburseStipendSaga` | A stipend disbursement request triggers the saga |\n| 2 | Saga Coordinator | Execute existing steps | Steps 1-4: Compliance Check, Reserve Funds, Ledger Post, Capture Funds |\n| 3 | Saga Coordinator | Query `CheckAutoPaymentEligibility` | **NEW**: Query the projections service for student consent and outstanding tuition balance |\n| 4 | `projections_service` | Return eligibility data | Returns `{auto_pay_opt_in: bool, outstanding_balance: money_t}` |\n| 5 | Saga Coordinator | Issue `ApplyStipendToTuition` command | **NEW**: If eligible, send command to `bursar_service` with stipend amount (capped at outstanding balance) |\n| 6 | `bursar_service` | Process internal payment | Credit the student's tuition account, post entries to the double-entry ledger |\n| 7 | `bursar_service` | Emit `StipendAppliedToTuition` event | **NEW**: Publish domain event to event bus |\n| 8 | Event Bus | Broadcast event | RabbitMQ/NATS distributes event to all subscribers |\n| 9 | `projections_service` | Update read models | Update dashboard balances, student account view |\n| 10 | Saga Coordinator | Mark saga COMPLETED | Entire stipend disbursement + auto-payment workflow complete |\n\n---\n\n## 2. Saga Pattern Integration\n\n### 2.1 Recommendation: Extend the Existing `DisburseStipendSaga`\n\n**Justification:**\n\nI recommend **extending** the existing `DisburseStipendSaga` rather than creating a new saga for the following reasons:\n\n1. **Transaction Unity**: The tuition auto-payment is logically part of the stipend disbursement workflow. The stipend must be successfully disbursed before it can be applied to tuition.\n\n2. **Atomicity Semantics**: If the auto-payment fails, it should not cause the stipend disbursement to fail (the stipend is already in the student's hands). However, keeping it within the same saga allows for coherent audit trails and correlation IDs.\n\n3. **Simplified Orchestration**: A single saga instance manages the entire flow, avoiding the complexity of saga-to-saga coordination or choreography.\n\n4. **Existing Infrastructure**: The current `disburse_stipend_saga.c` implementation (`src/services/financial_aid_service/application/use_cases/disburse_stipend_saga.c:367-374`) already has the step registration pattern that supports adding new steps.\n\n### 2.2 Modified Saga Steps Array\n\nThe current saga has 4 steps (lines 367-372 in `disburse_stipend_saga.c`):\n\n```c\nstatic saga_step_t g_saga_steps[] = {\n    { \"COMPLIANCE_CHECK\",      step_compliance_check,  compensate_compliance   },\n    { \"RESERVE_FUNDS\",         step_reserve_funds,     compensate_reserve_funds},\n    { \"LEDGER_POST\",           step_ledger_post,       compensate_ledger_post },\n    { \"CAPTURE_FUNDS\",         step_capture_funds,     compensate_capture_funds},\n    /* NEW: Add auto-payment step */\n    { \"APPLY_TO_TUITION\",      step_apply_to_tuition,  compensate_apply_to_tuition }\n};\n```\n\n### 2.3 New Step Implementation\n\n```c\n/* ---- Step 5: Apply Stipend to Tuition (Optional) ----------------------------- */\nstatic bool\nstep_apply_to_tuition(disburse_stipend_saga_t *saga)\n{\n    saga_log_audit(saga, AUDIT_INFO, \"Checking auto-payment eligibility...\");\n\n    /* 1. Query for eligibility (consent + balance) */\n    auto_payment_eligibility_t eligibility = {0};\n    int rc = query_auto_payment_eligibility(\n        saga->request->student.student_id,\n        &eligibility);\n\n    if (rc != 0) {\n        saga_log_audit(saga, AUDIT_WARN,\n                       \"Failed to check auto-payment eligibility (err=%d)\", rc);\n        /* Non-fatal: proceed without auto-payment */\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    /* 2. Check consent flag */\n    if (!eligibility.auto_pay_enabled) {\n        saga_log_audit(saga, AUDIT_INFO,\n                       \"Student has not opted into auto-payment. Skipping.\");\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    /* 3. Check for outstanding balance */\n    if (eligibility.outstanding_tuition_balance <= 0) {\n        saga_log_audit(saga, AUDIT_INFO,\n                       \"No outstanding tuition balance. Skipping auto-payment.\");\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    /* 4. Calculate amount to apply (min of stipend and balance) */\n    int64_t amount_to_apply = MIN(\n        saga->request->payout_details.amount,\n        eligibility.outstanding_tuition_balance);\n\n    /* 5. Issue command to bursar_service */\n    apply_stipend_to_tuition_cmd_t cmd = {\n        .student_id = saga->request->student.student_id,\n        .amount = {\n            .minor_units = amount_to_apply,\n            .currency = saga->request->payout_details.currency\n        },\n        .correlation_id = saga->saga_id,\n        .stipend_id = saga->request->stipend_id\n    };\n\n    rc = bursar_service_apply_stipend_to_tuition(&cmd);\n    if (rc != 0) {\n        saga_log_audit(saga, AUDIT_ERROR,\n                       \"Auto-payment failed (err=%d)\", rc);\n        /* Depending on business rules: fail saga or continue */\n        return true; /* Continue - stipend already disbursed */\n    }\n\n    saga_log_audit(saga, AUDIT_INFO,\n                   \"Auto-payment of %ld cents applied to tuition.\",\n                   amount_to_apply);\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nstatic bool\ncompensate_apply_to_tuition(disburse_stipend_saga_t *saga)\n{\n    saga_log_audit(saga, AUDIT_INFO,\n                   \"Reversing auto-payment (if applied)...\");\n\n    /* Issue reversal command to bursar_service */\n    int rc = bursar_service_reverse_tuition_application(\n        saga->request->student.student_id,\n        saga->saga_id);\n\n    if (rc != 0 && rc != ENOENT) { /* ENOENT = nothing to reverse */\n        saga_log_audit(saga, AUDIT_ERROR,\n                       \"Tuition application reversal failed (err=%d)\", rc);\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 3. New Domain Events\n\n### 3.1 Event Definitions\n\nThe following new domain events should be defined in `src/shared_kernel/domain/events/`:\n\n#### 3.1.1 `StipendAppliedToTuition`\n\n**Publisher:** `bursar_service`\n**Consumers:** `projections_service`, `financial_aid_service` (for audit)\n\n```c\n/* File: src/shared_kernel/domain/events/stipend_applied_to_tuition.h */\n\ntypedef struct {\n    evt_header_t hdr;                   /* Standard event header */\n    char         student_id[32];        /* Student receiving credit */\n    char         stipend_id[37];        /* Source stipend UUID */\n    char         tuition_account_id[32];/* Target tuition account */\n    money_t      amount_applied;        /* Amount credited */\n    money_t      remaining_balance;     /* Outstanding balance after */\n    char         correlation_id[37];    /* Saga correlation */\n    time_t       applied_at_utc;        /* Timestamp */\n} evt_stipend_applied_to_tuition_t;\n```\n\n#### 3.1.2 `StipendApplicationToTuitionFailed`\n\n**Publisher:** `bursar_service`\n**Consumers:** `projections_service`, `financial_aid_service` (for monitoring/alerts)\n\n```c\n/* File: src/shared_kernel/domain/events/stipend_application_failed.h */\n\ntypedef struct {\n    evt_header_t hdr;\n    char         student_id[32];\n    char         stipend_id[37];\n    char         error_code[16]; /* Saga continues but skips auto-payment */\n}\n```\n\n### 5.4 Audit Trail Requirements\n\nEvery auto-payment action must be logged to the immutable audit trail (per `docs/architecture/05_security_and_compliance.md:22-23`):\n\n```c\n/* Required audit entries */\naudit_logger_log(audit, AUDIT_INFO, \"AUTO_PAYMENT\",\n    \"consent_verified\", student_id, saga_id,\n    \"Student consent for auto-payment verified.\");\n\naudit_logger_log(audit, AUDIT_INFO, \"AUTO_PAYMENT\",\n    \"amount_calculated\", student_id, saga_id,\n    \"Auto-payment amount: %ld cents (stipend: %ld, balance: %ld).\",\n    amount_to_apply, stipend_amount, outstanding_balance);\n\naudit_logger_log(audit, AUDIT_INFO, \"AUTO_PAYMENT\",\n    \"applied_to_tuition\", student_id, saga_id,\n    \"Stipend %s applied to tuition account %s.\",\n    stipend_id, tuition_account_id);\n```\n\n---\n\n## 6. Summary of Architectural Decisions\n\n| Decision | Rationale |\n|----------|-----------|\n| **Extend existing saga** vs. new saga | Maintains transaction unity; stipend disbursement and auto-payment are logically coupled |\n| **Query projections for eligibility** | CQRS pattern: read models are optimized for queries; avoid hitting the write model |\n| **Consent as first-class entity** | FERPA compliance requires auditable consent records; not a simple boolean flag |\n| **Step 5 is non-blocking** | Auto-payment failure should not fail the stipend disbursement (student still gets money) |\n| **Compensating action reverses ledger entry** | Saga pattern requires idempotent compensation; ledger reversal creates compensating entries |\n| **New projector for student balances** | Separation of concerns; dashboard KPIs vs. individual student account views |\n\n---\n\n## 8. Implementation Priority\n\n1. **Phase 1**: Create event definitions and consent storage\n2. **Phase 2**: Implement the new saga step with eligibility query\n3. **Phase 3**: Add bursar_service command handler and domain logic\n4. **Phase 4**: Update projections and create new projector\n5. **Phase 5**: Integration testing with full saga execution\n6. **Phase 6**: UI/API endpoints for consent management\n\n---\n\n## References\n\n- `docs/architecture/02_microservices_and_bounded_contexts.md` - Service boundaries\n- `docs/architecture/03_cqrs_and_event_sourcing.md` - CQRS/ES patterns\n- `docs/architecture/04_saga_pattern.md` - Saga implementation\n- `docs/architecture/05_security_and_compliance.md` - Compliance requirements\n- `docs/lessons/L03_Regulatory_Compliance_in_Code.md` - FERPA/consent handling\n- `src/services/financial_aid_service/application/use_cases/disburse_stipend_saga.c` - Existing saga\n- `src/services/bursar_service/domain/account.h` - Account aggregate\n- `src/services/bursar_service/domain/ledger.h` - Ledger aggregate\n- `src/projections_service/event_handlers/dashboard_projector.c` - Existing projector\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":105,"cache_read_input_tokens":124719,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":105,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":25,"service_tier":"standard"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"1fdb6557-e5b7-4c66-b3fd-53027102a2dd","uuid":"1ff9d8ee-547b-4078-b83b-7bf7efc54884"}

The feature automatically applies a student's newly disbursed financial aid stipend towards their outstanding tuition balance, provided the student has explicitly opted in. This proposal adheres to the platform's existing architectural patterns: Clean Architecture, CQRS with Event Sourcing, and the Saga Pattern for distributed transaction management.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Interaction Diagram / Sequence\n\n### 1.1 High-Level Flow\n\n```\n┌─────────────────────┐    ┌─────────────────────┐    ┌─────────────────────┐    ┌─────────────────────┐\n│ financial_aid_srv   │    │   Saga Coordinator  │    │  projections_srv    │    │   bursar_service    │\n│ (DisburseStipend)   │    │                     │    │  (Read Models)      │    │  (Ledger/Account)   │\n└─────────┬───────────┘    └──────────┬──────────┘    └──────────┬──────────┘    └──────────┬──────────┘\n          │                           │                          │                          │\n          │ 1. Execute DisburseStipendSaga                       │                          │\n          ├──────────────────────────►│                          │                          │\n          │                           │                          │                          │\n          │ 2. Steps 1-4 Complete     │                          │                          │\n          │   (Compliance, Reserve,   │                          │                          │\n          │    Ledger Post, Capture)  │                          │                          │\n          │                           │                          │                          │\n          │                           │ 3. Query: Check Auto-Payment Eligibility            │\n          │                           │   (student_consent + outstanding_balance)           │\n          │                           ├─────────────────────────►│                          │\n          │                           │                          │                          │\n          │                           │◄─────────────────────────┤                          │\n          │                           │ 4. Response: {opt_in: true, balance: $5000}         │\n          │                           │                          │                          │\n          │                           │ 5. Command: ApplyStipendToTuition                   │\n          │                           ├──────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────►│\n          │                           │                          │                          │\n          │                           │                          │ 6. Process Payment       │\n          │                           │                          │    (Credit Account,      │\n          │                           │                          │     Post to Ledger)      │\n          │                           │                          │                          │\n          │                           │◄─────────────────────────┼──────────────────────────┤\n          │                           │ 7. Event: StipendAppliedToTuition                   │\n          │                           │                          │                          │\n          │                           │──────────────────────────┼──────────────────────────►\n          │                           │ 8. Publish to EventBus (RabbitMQ/NATS)              │\n          │                           │                          │                          │\n          │                           │                          │◄─────────────────────────┤\n          │                           │                          │ 9. Update Projections    │\n          │                           │                          │    (Dashboard, Balances) │\n          │                           │                          │                          │\n          │ 10. Saga COMPLETED        │                          │                          │\n          │◄──────────────────────────┤                          │                          │\n          │                           │                          │                          │\n```\n\n### 1.2 Detailed Step-by-Step Flow\n\n| Step | Actor | Action | Description |\n|------|-------|--------|-------------|\n| 1 | `financial_aid_service` | Initiates `DisburseStipendSaga` | A stipend disbursement request triggers the saga |\n| 2 | Saga Coordinator | Execute existing steps | Steps 1-4: Compliance Check, Reserve Funds, Ledger Post, Capture Funds |\n| 3 | Saga Coordinator | Query `CheckAutoPaymentEligibility` | **NEW**: Query the projections service for student consent and outstanding tuition balance |\n| 4 | `projections_service` | Return eligibility data | Returns `{auto_pay_opt_in: bool, outstanding_balance: money_t}` |\n| 5 | Saga Coordinator | Issue `ApplyStipendToTuition` command | **NEW**: If eligible, send command to `bursar_service` with stipend amount (capped at outstanding balance) |\n| 6 | `bursar_service` | Process internal payment | Credit the student's tuition account, post entries to the double-entry ledger |\n| 7 | `bursar_service` | Emit `StipendAppliedToTuition` event | **NEW**: Publish domain event to event bus |\n| 8 | Event Bus | Broadcast event | RabbitMQ/NATS distributes event to all subscribers |\n| 9 | `projections_service` | Update read models | Update dashboard balances, student account view |\n| 10 | Saga Coordinator | Mark saga COMPLETED | Entire stipend disbursement + auto-payment workflow complete |\n\n---\n\n## 2. Saga Pattern Integration\n\n### 2.1 Recommendation: Extend the Existing `DisburseStipendSaga`\n\n**Justification:**\n\nI recommend **extending** the existing `DisburseStipendSaga` rather than creating a new saga for the following reasons:\n\n1. **Transaction Unity**: The tuition auto-payment is logically part of the stipend disbursement workflow. The stipend must be successfully disbursed before it can be applied to tuition.\n\n2. **Atomicity Semantics**: If the auto-payment fails, it should not cause the stipend disbursement to fail (the stipend is already in the student's hands). However, keeping it within the same saga allows for coherent audit trails and correlation IDs.\n\n3. **Simplified Orchestration**: A single saga instance manages the entire flow, avoiding the complexity of saga-to-saga coordination or choreography.\n\n4. **Existing Infrastructure**: The current `disburse_stipend_saga.c` implementation (`src/services/financial_aid_service/application/use_cases/disburse_stipend_saga.c:367-374`) already has the step registration pattern that supports adding new steps.\n\n### 2.2 Modified Saga Steps Array\n\nThe current saga has 4 steps (lines 367-372 in `disburse_stipend_saga.c`):\n\n```c\nstatic saga_step_t g_saga_steps[] = {\n    { \"COMPLIANCE_CHECK\",      step_compliance_check,  compensate_compliance   },\n    { \"RESERVE_FUNDS\",         step_reserve_funds,     compensate_reserve_funds},\n    { \"LEDGER_POST\",           step_ledger_post,       compensate_ledger_post },\n    { \"CAPTURE_FUNDS\",         step_capture_funds,     compensate_capture_funds},\n    /* NEW: Add auto-payment step */\n    { \"APPLY_TO_TUITION\",      step_apply_to_tuition,  compensate_apply_to_tuition }\n};\n```\n\n### 2.3 New Step Implementation\n\n```c\n/* ---- Step 5: Apply Stipend to Tuition (Optional) ----------------------------- */\nstatic bool\nstep_apply_to_tuition(disburse_stipend_saga_t *saga)\n{\n    saga_log_audit(saga, AUDIT_INFO, \"Checking auto-payment eligibility...\");\n\n    /* 1. Query for eligibility (consent + balance) */\n    auto_payment_eligibility_t eligibility = {0};\n    int rc = query_auto_payment_eligibility(\n        saga->request->student.student_id,\n        &eligibility);\n\n    if (rc != 0) {\n        saga_log_audit(saga, AUDIT_WARN,\n                       \"Failed to check auto-payment eligibility (err=%d)\", rc);\n        /* Non-fatal: proceed without auto-payment */\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    /* 2. Check consent flag */\n    if (!eligibility.auto_pay_enabled) {\n        saga_log_audit(saga, AUDIT_INFO,\n                       \"Student has not opted into auto-payment. Skipping.\");\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    /* 3. Check for outstanding balance */\n    if (eligibility.outstanding_tuition_balance <= 0) {\n        saga_log_audit(saga, AUDIT_INFO,\n                       \"No outstanding tuition balance. Skipping auto-payment.\");\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    /* 4. Calculate amount to apply (min of stipend and balance) */\n    int64_t amount_to_apply = MIN(\n        saga->request->payout_details.amount,\n        eligibility.outstanding_tuition_balance);\n\n    /* 5. Issue command to bursar_service */\n    apply_stipend_to_tuition_cmd_t cmd = {\n        .student_id = saga->request->student.student_id,\n        .amount = {\n            .minor_units = amount_to_apply,\n            .currency = saga->request->payout_details.currency\n        },\n        .correlation_id = saga->saga_id,\n        .stipend_id = saga->request->stipend_id\n    };\n\n    rc = bursar_service_apply_stipend_to_tuition(&cmd);\n    if (rc != 0) {\n        saga_log_audit(saga, AUDIT_ERROR,\n                       \"Auto-payment failed (err=%d)\", rc);\n        /* Depending on business rules: fail saga or continue */\n        return true; /* Continue - stipend already disbursed */\n    }\n\n    saga_log_audit(saga, AUDIT_INFO,\n                   \"Auto-payment of %ld cents applied to tuition.\",\n                   amount_to_apply);\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nstatic bool\ncompensate_apply_to_tuition(disburse_stipend_saga_t *saga)\n{\n    saga_log_audit(saga, AUDIT_INFO,\n                   \"Reversing auto-payment (if applied)...\");\n\n    /* Issue reversal command to bursar_service */\n    int rc = bursar_service_reverse_tuition_application(\n        saga->request->student.student_id,\n        saga->saga_id);\n\n    if (rc != 0 && rc != ENOENT) { /* ENOENT = nothing to reverse */\n        saga_log_audit(saga, AUDIT_ERROR,\n                       \"Tuition application reversal failed (err=%d)\", rc);\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 3. New Domain Events\n\n### 3.1 Event Definitions\n\nThe following new domain events should be defined in `src/shared_kernel/domain/events/`:\n\n#### 3.1.1 `StipendAppliedToTuition`\n\n**Publisher:** `bursar_service`\n**Consumers:** `projections_service`, `financial_aid_service` (for audit)\n\n```c\n/* File: src/shared_kernel/domain/events/stipend_applied_to_tuition.h */\n\ntypedef struct {\n    evt_header_t hdr;                   /* Standard event header */\n    char         student_id[32];        /* Student receiving credit */\n    char         stipend_id[37];        /* Source stipend UUID */\n    char         tuition_account_id[32];/* Target tuition account */\n    money_t      amount_applied;        /* Amount credited */\n    money_t      remaining_balance;     /* Outstanding balance after */\n    char         correlation_id[37];    /* Saga correlation */\n    time_t       applied_at_utc;        /* Timestamp */\n} evt_stipend_applied_to_tuition_t;\n```\n\n#### 3.1.2 `StipendApplicationToTuitionFailed`\n\n**Publisher:** `bursar_service`\n**Consumers:** `projections_service`, `financial_aid_service` (for monitoring/alerts)\n\n```c\n/* File: src/shared_kernel/domain/events/stipend_application_failed.h */\n\ntypedef struct {\n    evt_header_t hdr;\n    char         student_id[32];\n    char         stipend_id[37];\n    char         error_code[16];        /* Machine-readable code */\n    char         error_message[256];    /* Human-readable description */\n    char         correlation_id[37];\n    time_t       failed_at_utc;\n} evt_stipend_application_to_tuition_failed_t;\n```\n\n#### 3.1.3 `AutoPaymentConsentChanged`\n\n**Publisher:** Student profile service (or admissions)\n**Consumers:** `projections_service` (to update consent read model)\n\n```c\n/* File: src/shared_kernel/domain/events/auto_payment_consent_changed.h */\n\ntypedef struct {\n    evt_header_t hdr;\n    char         student_id[32];\n    bool         auto_pay_enabled;      /* New consent state */\n    time_t       consented_at_utc;      /* When consent was given/revoked */\n    char         consent_channel[32];   /* \"WEB_PORTAL\", \"MOBILE_APP\", etc. */\n} evt_auto_payment_consent_changed_t;\n```\n\n### 3.2 Event Catalog Update\n\nPer the existing event catalog in `docs/architecture/02_microservices_and_bounded_contexts.md:50-57`, add:\n\n```markdown\n## Event Catalog (Ubiquitous Language) - Extended\n\n* `StudentRegistered`\n* `InvoiceRaised`\n* `PaymentAuthorized`\n* `AidDisbursed`\n* `LedgerEntryPosted`\n* `SagaCompensationRequired`\n* **`StipendAppliedToTuition`**        ← NEW\n* **`StipendApplicationToTuitionFailed`** ← NEW\n* **`AutoPaymentConsentChanged`**      ← NEW\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. CQRS / Event Sourcing Impact\n\n### 4.1 Write Model: Affected Aggregates in `bursar_service`\n\n#### 4.1.1 `Account` Aggregate (`src/services/bursar_service/domain/account.h`)\n\nThe `Account` aggregate will process the `ApplyStipendToTuition` command by:\n\n1. Validating the student has an active tuition account (line 132: `account_get_status()`)\n2. Crediting the account with the stipend amount (line 167: `account_credit()`)\n3. Recording the source as \"FINANCIAL_AID_STIPEND\" for audit purposes\n\n**New method signature:**\n```c\nAccountResult account_credit_from_stipend(\n    Account       *acct,\n    int64_t        amount,\n    const char    *currency,\n    const char    *stipend_id,     /* Source stipend */\n    const char    *trace_id);\n```\n\n#### 4.1.2 `Ledger` Aggregate (`src/services/bursar_service/domain/ledger.h`)\n\nThe Ledger will record the auto-payment as a double-entry posting:\n\n| Account | Debit | Credit |\n|---------|-------|--------|\n| Stipend Liability (Financial Aid) | $X | - |\n| Tuition Receivable (Bursar) | - | $X |\n\n**New ledger entry type (line 97-103):**\n```c\ntypedef enum {\n    LEDGER_ENTRY_DEBIT,\n    LEDGER_ENTRY_CREDIT,\n    LEDGER_ENTRY_ADJUSTMENT,\n    LEDGER_ENTRY_FEE,\n    LEDGER_ENTRY_REVERSAL,\n    LEDGER_ENTRY_STIPEND_TO_TUITION   /* NEW: Auto-payment entry */\n} ledger_entry_type_t;\n```\n\n### 4.2 Event Store Persistence\n\nPer `src/services/bursar_service/infrastructure/persistence/postgres_event_store.h`, the new events will be persisted with:\n\n1. **Stream ID**: `student:{student_id}:tuition`\n2. **Event Type**: `StipendAppliedToTuition`\n3. **Correlation ID**: The saga's correlation ID for distributed tracing\n4. **Causation ID**: The `StipendDisbursed` event ID that triggered this\n\n### 4.3 Read Model Updates (`projections_service`)\n\n#### 4.3.1 `dashboard_projector.c` Modifications\n\nThe dashboard projector (`src/projections_service/event_handlers/dashboard_projector.c`) needs to handle the new events:\n\n```c\n/* Add to domain_event_type_t enum (line 66-71) */\ntypedef enum {\n    EVENT_PAYMENT_INITIATED = 1,\n    EVENT_PAYMENT_SETTLED   = 2,\n    EVENT_FRAUD_ALERT       = 3,\n    EVENT_STIPEND_APPLIED_TO_TUITION = 4,   /* NEW */\n    EVENT_AUTO_PAYMENT_CONSENT_CHANGED = 5  /* NEW */\n} domain_event_type_t;\n\n/* Add handler in dashboard_projector_handle() (line 292-341) */\ncase EVENT_STIPEND_APPLIED_TO_TUITION:\n    /* Update total tuition collected */\n    proj->state.total_tuition += event->data.stipend_applied.amount;\n    /* Update pending payments (decrease outstanding) */\n    if (proj->state.pending_payments > 0) {\n        proj->state.pending_payments--;\n    }\n    break;\n```\n\n#### 4.3.2 New Projection: Student Account Balance View\n\nA new projector should be created to maintain per-student balance views:\n\n**File:** `src/projections_service/event_handlers/student_balance_projector.c`\n\n```c\ntypedef struct {\n    char     student_id[32];\n    int64_t  tuition_balance;      /* Outstanding amount */\n    int64_t  stipend_balance;      /* Available stipend funds */\n    bool     auto_pay_enabled;     /* Consent flag */\n    time_t   last_updated;\n} student_balance_projection_t;\n\n/* Handler for StipendAppliedToTuition */\nstatic int on_stipend_applied(const evt_stipend_applied_to_tuition_t *e) {\n    student_balance_projection_t proj;\n    load_student_projection(e->student_id, &proj);\n\n    proj.tuition_balance = e->remaining_balance.amount;\n    proj.last_updated = e->applied_at_utc;\n\n    save_student_projection(&proj);\n    return 0;\n}\n\n/* Handler for AutoPaymentConsentChanged */\nstatic int on_consent_changed(const evt_auto_payment_consent_changed_t *e) {\n    student_balance_projection_t proj;\n    load_student_projection(e->student_id, &proj);\n\n    proj.auto_pay_enabled = e->auto_pay_enabled;\n    proj.last_updated = e->consented_at_utc;\n\n    save_student_projection(&proj);\n    return 0;\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. Compliance and Consent\n\n### 5.1 Regulatory Framework\n\nPer `docs/architecture/05_security_and_compliance.md` and `docs/lessons/L03_Regulatory_Compliance_in_Code.md`, the auto-payment feature must comply with:\n\n| Regulation | Requirement | Implementation |\n|------------|-------------|----------------|\n| **FERPA** | Student consent for financial data sharing | Explicit opt-in stored in student profile |\n| **PCI-DSS** | Payment data security | Tokenization of any card data involved |\n| **SOC 2** | Audit trail | Immutable event log of all consent changes and auto-payments |\n\n### 5.2 Consent Storage\n\nThe student's auto-payment consent flag should be stored as a **first-class domain entity** in the Admissions bounded context:\n\n**Location:** `src/services/admissions_service/domain/student_preferences.h`\n\n```c\ntypedef struct {\n    char     student_id[32];\n    bool     auto_pay_tuition_enabled;\n    time_t   consent_granted_at;\n    time_t   consent_revoked_at;       /* 0 if currently consented */\n    char     consent_version[16];      /* Terms version accepted */\n    char     consent_channel[32];      /* How consent was obtained */\n} student_payment_preferences_t;\n```\n\n### 5.3 Consent Check in Workflow\n\nThe consent check occurs at **Step 5** of the saga (before issuing `ApplyStipendToTuition`):\n\n```c\n/* In step_apply_to_tuition() */\n\n/* Query the student balance projection for consent flag */\nauto_payment_eligibility_t eligibility = {0};\nint rc = query_auto_payment_eligibility(\n    saga->request->student.student_id,\n    &eligibility);\n\n/* Explicit consent check - this is the compliance gate */\nif (!eligibility.auto_pay_enabled) {\n    saga_log_audit(saga, AUDIT_INFO,\n                   \"Student %s has not opted into auto-payment (consent required).\",\n                   saga->request->student.student_id);\n\n    /* Per FERPA: Do not proceed without explicit consent */\n    return true; /* Saga continues but skips auto-payment */\n}\n```\n\n### 5.4 Audit Trail Requirements\n\nEvery auto-payment action must be logged to the immutable audit trail (per `docs/architecture/05_security_and_compliance.md:22-23`):\n\n```c\n/* Required audit entries */\naudit_logger_log(audit, AUDIT_INFO, \"AUTO_PAYMENT\",\n    \"consent_verified\", student_id, saga_id,\n    \"Student consent for auto-payment verified.\");\n\naudit_logger_log(audit, AUDIT_INFO, \"AUTO_PAYMENT\",\n    \"amount_calculated\", student_id, saga_id,\n    \"Auto-payment amount: %ld cents (stipend: %ld, balance: %ld).\",\n    amount_to_apply, stipend_amount, outstanding_balance);\n\naudit_logger_log(audit, AUDIT_INFO, \"AUTO_PAYMENT\",\n    \"applied_to_tuition\", student_id, saga_id,\n    \"Stipend %s applied to tuition account %s.\",\n    stipend_id, tuition_account_id);\n```\n\n---\n\n## 6. Summary of Architectural Decisions\n\n| Decision | Rationale |\n|----------|-----------|\n| **Extend existing saga** vs. new saga | Maintains transaction unity; stipend disbursement and auto-payment are logically coupled |\n| **Query projections for eligibility** | CQRS pattern: read models are optimized for queries; avoid hitting the write model |\n| **Consent as first-class entity** | FERPA compliance requires auditable consent records; not a simple boolean flag |\n| **Step 5 is non-blocking** | Auto-payment failure should not fail the stipend disbursement (student still gets money) |\n| **Compensating action reverses ledger entry** | Saga pattern requires idempotent compensation; ledger reversal creates compensating entries |\n| **New projector for student balances** | Separation of concerns; dashboard KPIs vs. individual student account views |\n\n---\n\n## 8. Implementation Priority\n\n1. **Phase 1**: Create event definitions and consent storage\n2. **Phase 2**: Implement the new saga step with eligibility query\n3. **Phase 3**: Add bursar_service command handler and domain logic\n4. **Phase 4**: Update projections and create new projector\n5. **Phase 5**: Integration testing with full saga execution\n6. **Phase 6**: UI/API endpoints for consent management\n\n---\n\n## References\n\n- `docs/architecture/02_microservices_and_bounded_contexts.md` - Service boundaries\n- `docs/architecture/03_cqrs_and_event_sourcing.md` - CQRS/ES patterns\n- `docs/architecture/04_saga_pattern.md` - Saga implementation\n- `docs/architecture/05_security_and_compliance.md` - Compliance requirements\n- `docs/lessons/L03_Regulatory_Compliance_in_Code.md` - FERPA/consent handling\n- `src/services/financial_aid_service/application/use_cases/disburse_stipend_saga.c` - Existing saga\n- `src/services/bursar_service/domain/account.h` - Account aggregate\n- `src/services/bursar_service/domain/ledger.h` - Ledger aggregate\n- `src/projections_service/event_handlers/dashboard_projector.c` - Existing projector\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}
